### [1] [GæniceN: a minimal model to relax magnetization states self-consistently in general artificial spin ices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37889)
*H. Braun,E. Freeman,G. Alatteili,E. Iacocca*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在Gaenice框架基础上提出GaeniceN，用迭代方法实现Landau-Lifshitz方程的耗散项，以自洽方式稳定人工自旋冰的磁化态，为后续GHz动力学计算提供初始条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工自旋冰在GHz频段存在多模式以及纳米磁体边缘磁化偏差，使动力学研究困难；Gaenice采用每个纳米磁体三个宏自旋的最小模型来刻画动力学，但需要可靠的弛豫磁化态作为初始条件。

Method: 基于Gaenice同一框架，将Landau-Lifshitz方程的耗散分量以迭代方式实现，从而高效收敛到弛豫态，即GaeniceN弛豫方法。

Result: 以金字塔形人工自旋冰在低幅值外场下的相变为例，通过计算系统平均能量，GaeniceN正确预测了相之间的转变。

Conclusion: GaeniceN与Gaenice同源，其输出可直接作为Gaenice计算铁磁共振或能带结构等动力学的初始条件；两者结合可辅助设计面向磁子学应用的人工自旋冰。

Abstract: Artificial spin ices are geometric arrangement of nanomagnets that exhibit reconfigurable magnetization states and GHz dynamics. Their investigation in the GHz regime is challenging because of the existence of multiple modes and the impact of magnetization deviations at the edges of the nanomagnets. A minimal model to capture such dynamics require three macrospins per nanomagnet, an approach that was implemented for generic artificial spin ices in the tool Gaenice. Here, we use the Gaenice framework to realize a relaxation method to stabilize magnetization states self-consistently, called GaeniceN. We implement the dissipative component of the Landau-Lifshitz equation in an iterative approach to efficiently converge to the relaxed state. As an example, we find our approach to correctly predict the transition between phases in a pyramid ASI subject to a low-magnitude external field by computing the system's average energy. Because they are rooted on the same framework, GaeniceN produces the initial condition needed for Gaenice to compute dynamics such as ferromagnetic resonance or band structures. Combined, these tools will be able to aid in the design of artificial spin ices with applications in magnonics.

</details>


### [2] [Anomalous Hall Conductivity as an Intrinsic Property of a Tight-Binding Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37939)
*E. S. Andriyakhina,G. Lemut,P. W. Brouwer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 弹道体系中反常直流霍尔响应依赖微观嵌入与测量方式；作者证明在杂质散射弛豫存在时总霍尔电导σ_xy^H才与嵌入无关，并给出能带项本身嵌入无关的对称性判据，指出该判据不足以保证能带项等于或可靠近似总霍尔响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清"反常霍尔电导是否为体系内禀属性"这一基本问题：弹道体系中dc霍尔响应普遍依赖轨道几何嵌入及测量协议的具体实现，需界定其在何种条件下可视为材料的本征性质。

Method: 理论分析弹道体系的反常直流霍尔响应，将其分解为内禀能带项与外在的侧跳、反常分布项，比较各分项与总响应σ_xy^H对轨道嵌入的依赖；并引入杂质散射弛豫机制，推导能带项嵌入无关的对称性判据。

Result: 有杂质散射弛豫时，完整的直流霍尔响应σ_xy^H与嵌入无关；而内禀能带项、侧跳项与反常分布项各自仍依赖嵌入。所导出的对称性判据可使命带项本身嵌入无关，但该判据既不能使能带项等同于σ_xy^H，也不能使其成为σ_xy^H的可靠近似。

Conclusion: "嵌入依赖性"可作为判别响应系数是材料定态内禀性质还是仍受测量协议影响的诊断工具；仅凭对称性判据不足以将能带项视为完整霍尔电导或其可靠近似。

Abstract: In a ballistic system, the anomalous dc Hall response generically depends on microscopic details, including the geometric embedding of the orbitals and the precise implementation of the measurement. Here, we consider the question under what circumstances the anomalous Hall conductivity can be regarded as an intrinsic property of a system. We show that the full dc Hall response $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$ becomes embedding-independent in the presence of relaxation by impurity scattering, although the individual (intrinsic) band and (extrinsic) side-jump and anomalous-distribution contributions to the Hall conductivity separately remain embedding-dependent. We further derive a symmetry criterion for when the band term itself becomes embedding-independent, but argue that this alone neither identifies it with the full Hall conductivity $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$, nor makes it a reliable approximation for $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$. More broadly, our results establish embedding dependence as a diagnostic of whether a response coefficient represents a stationary intrinsic property of a material or retains sensitivity to measurement protocol.

</details>


### [3] [Few-Electron Partitioning in the Fractional Quantum Hall Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37950)
*T. Jonckheere,J. Rech,B. Grémaud*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究分数量子霍尔点接触中N电子态的分配统计，在g=1/2可解点给出非微扰电流与噪声公式，并证明电荷以e为单位转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受少电子分配实验[Shaju et al., Nature 642, 928 (2025)]启发，研究分数量子霍尔点接触处N电子态的分配统计。

Method: 在可解点g=1/2处推导任意驱动下的非微扰电流与噪声公式；对洛伦兹脉冲（Levitons），将全计数统计约化为N×N行列式。

Result: 证明电荷仅以e为单位转移；N=2,3,4的对称化累积量显示出强反聚束及高阶关联，可在(ν_1,ν_2)=(1,1/3)结中观测。

Conclusion: 少电子分配的统计特征揭示了分数量子霍尔边缘上强反聚束与高阶关联，为分数量子霍尔点接触的电子分配实验提供可检验预言。

Abstract: Motivated by few-electron partitioning experiments [Shaju et al., Nature 642, 928 (2025)], we study the partition statistics of $N$-electron states at a fractional quantum Hall point contact. At the solvable point $g=1/2$, we obtain nonperturbative current and noise formulas for arbitrary drives, while for Lorentzian pulses (Levitons) the full counting statistics reduces to an $N\times N$ determinant. We show that the charge is transferred solely in units of $e$, and that symmetrized cumulants for $N=2,3,4$ reveal strong antibunching and higher-order correlations, observable in a $(ν_1,ν_2)=(1,1/3)$ junction.

</details>


### [4] [Coherent control and high purity single-photon emission from telecom quantum dots on metamorphic InGaAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38060)
*Beatrice Costa,Bianca Scaparra,Hubert Riedl,Gregor Koblmüller,Eugenio Zallo,Jonathan J. Finley,Lukas Hanschke,Kai Müller*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用准共振与声子辅助激发，揭示嵌入弛豫InGaAs基体/变质InGaAs层中InAs量子点的本征光学性质，实现21 μeV线宽、慢衰减通道消失与10^-2量级二阶关联，并在O、C波段观察到三离子相干控制与Rabi型振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向光纤量子通信需要发射于通信O波段与C波段的单光子源，而该波段InAs量子点常因激发方式引入的展宽与慢衰减通道难以呈现本征光学特性，需区分激发诱导效应与本征发射体性质。

Method: 采用弛豫InGaAs基体及变质InGaAs层上的InAs量子点，系统对比准共振激发与声子辅助激发方案，结合光致发光激发谱表征高激发态，并进行三离子跃迁的相干控制实验。

Result: 观测到最窄21 μeV的谱线宽、慢衰减通道消失、二阶自相关约10^-2量级的单光子纯度；PLE谱显示更高激发态；在O、C两个通信波段均实现三离子相干控制与Rabi型振荡。

Conclusion: 该量子点体系在通信波段具备优良的单光子与相干操控性能，具备面向量子技术应用（如光纤量子通信单光子源）的潜力。

Abstract: Semiconductor quantum dots emitting in the telecommunication O- and C-bands are promising candidates as single-photon sources for fiber-based quantum communication. In this work, we show how coherent quasi-resonant and phonon-assisted excitation help reveal the intrinsic optical properties of InAs quantum dots embedded in relaxed InGaAs matrices on top of metamorphic InGaAs layers for achieving emission in the telecom bands. By systematically comparing excitation schemes, we separate excitation-induced effects from intrinsic emitter properties. This allows us to observe linewidths as narrow as 21 $μ$eV, the disappearance of slow decay channels, and single-photon purity reaching second-order autocorrelation values in the order of $10^{-2}$. Photoluminescence excitation reveals higher excited states, enabling coherent control of trion transitions and the observation of Rabi-like oscillations in both telecom bands. These results confirm the promising potential of these quantum dots for applications in quantum technologies.

</details>


### [5] [Incoherent dark excitons extend Floquet-like physics across time and momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38156)
*Xing Zhu,Jacques Hawecker,Enrico Perfetto,Vivek Pareek,David R. Bacon,Harley Suchiang,Nanami Tomoda,Amrita Mitra,Takumi Fukuda,Sebastien Roux,Ouri Karni,Jack Featherstone,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Michael K. L. Man,Julien Madéo,Felipe H. da Jornada,Gianluca Stefanucci,Keshav M. Dani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用WSe₂/MoS₂异质结中的暗层间激子密集非相干布居，使导带与价带的能量复制带发生杂化，实现可持续近1 ns的类Floquet现象，并可通过激子密度与扭转角调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Floquet现象依赖相干、时间周期性的驱动（如强光场），其效应仅能维持驱动相干时间，限于亚皮秒尺度，实用价值受限。作者希望验证超越平均场图像的理论预言：非相干激子布居亦可诱导静态耦合，产生长寿命的类Floquet杂化。

Method: 采用时间分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES），在WSe₂/MoS₂异质结中注入密集的暗层间激子，观测导带与价带能量复制带之间的杂化；改变层间激子密度与扭转角，分析能带色散演化与动量偏移。

Result: 类Floquet杂化可维持近1 ns；随激子密度增加，价带及其复制带由倒抛物型演化为Mexican-hat色散，表明存在非零静态耦合且耦合强度随激子密度线性增长；通过调节扭转角，具有净动量的暗激子产生的复制带与能带在能量和动量上同时错位并发生杂化。

Conclusion: 非相干暗激子布居可提供一种稳健、长寿命的电子态调控途径，突破相干、零动量驱动的限制；动量空间偏移为超越传统Floquet工程提供了新的自由度。

Abstract: Floquet phenomena - where a coherent, time-periodic drive hybridizes the conduction band with energy-replicated copies of the valence band - have been demonstrated with intense optical fields and, more recently, other bosonic excitations. However, within mean-field approximations and the current understanding of Floquet phenomena, these effects survive only for the coherence time of the drive, limiting practical use and current demonstrations to the sub-picosecond regime. Here, consistent with recent theoretical predictions beyond the mean-field picture, using time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy in WSe$_2$/MoS$_2$ heterostructures, we show that incoherent, dense populations of dark interlayer excitons hybridize the conduction band with energy-replicated copies of the valence band, mimicking Floquet-like phenomena - now persisting for nearly a nanosecond. With increasing interlayer exciton density, the valence band and its replica evolve from an inverted-parabolic to a Mexican-hat dispersion, a signature of the predicted non-zero static coupling that hybridizes the bands and scales linearly with the exciton density. By tuning twist angle, we further show that dark excitons with net momentum create replicas that hybridize with bands offset not just in energy but also in momentum, providing another powerful degree of freedom beyond conventional Floquet engineering. These results establish a robust, long-lived route to engineering electronic states, beyond a coherent, zero-momentum drive.

</details>


### [6] [Charge Filling-Dependent Valley Polarization and its Dynamics Driven by Correlated Interlayer Exciton States in WSe2/WS2 Heterobilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38251)
*Zhiwei Li,Haonan Wang,Yubei Xiang,Wataru Idehara,Hailin Liu,Shinichiro Matano,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kazunari Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究WSe2/WS2摩尔异质双层中间层激子随电荷填充因子变化的谷弛豫动力学，结合时间分辨光谱与Monte Carlo模拟揭示跨越Mott转变的激子动力学演化，发现整数填充下辐射复合增强而谷弛豫速率显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔体系中的强关联电子态与激子态已被广泛研究，但其对谷自由度及其动力学的影响尚属空白，而谷自由度动力学对基础理解与谷电子学应用至关重要。

Method: 在WSe2/WS2摩尔异质双层中，采用时间分辨光学光谱测量层间激子（IXs）的电荷填充依赖谷弛豫动力学，并结合Monte Carlo模拟分析跨越Mott转变的动力学演化。

Result: 在整数填充因子n（=-1）处，IXs的辐射衰减与复合速率增强；在n=±1处谷弛豫速率显著降低。波函数重叠减小与声子散射过程受抑，导致非辐射复合与谷间散射被抑制。

Conclusion: 这些反常行为源于整数填充时引入的电荷载流子局域化所导致的复合路径改变与激子相干性增强；激子与谷动力学可作为探测摩尔体系强关联态的灵敏探针，并为调控二维量子材料中的激子相干性与谷电子学性质提供途径。

Abstract: Moiré heterostructures of transition metal dichalcogenides provide an emerging platform for studying the strongly correlated electronic and excitonic states. The valley degree of freedom and its dynamics in the strongly correlated electronic and excitonic states in the moiré system are crucial for fundamental understanding and applications of valleytronics. Although the strongly correlated states in moiré systems have been extensively investigated, their impact on valley degrees of freedom and its dynamics remains unexplored. Here, we study the charge filling-dependent valley relaxation dynamics of interlayer excitons (IXs) in WSe2/WS2 moiré heterobilayer system. The evolution of moiré IXs dynamics across the Mott transition is elucidated by combining time-resolved optical spectroscopy with Monte Carlo simulation. Our results show that the radiative decay and recombination rate of IXs are enhanced at the integer filling factors n (=-1). Moreover, the valley relaxation rate of IXs at n=$\pm$1 is significantly decreased. The resulting reduction in wavefunction overlap and phonon scattering process suppresses the nonradiative recombination and intervalley scattering of IXs. These anomalous behaviors are attributed to the modification of IXs recombination pathways and enhance excitonic coherence due to the localization of introduced charge carriers at integer filling of moiré site. Our results explore exciton and valley dynamics as sensitive probes of strongly correlated states in moiré systems and highlight possible ways to control excitonic coherence and valleytronic properties in two-dimensional quantum materials.

</details>


### [7] [The Flexoelectric Twist: Persistence of Heavy Fermion Picture in Small-Angle Twisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38305)
*Qirong Yao,Ka Cheuk Fan,Tianyu Qiao,Hao Shi,Xi Dai,Berthold Jäck*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文通过低温扫描隧道显微镜研究小角度（θ≈0.3–0.7°）扭角双层石墨烯，发现莫尔晶格重构与原子晶格膨胀通过柔性电效应打破粒子–空穴对称性，支持AA位点局域化的重费米子f态与AB/BA位点巡游c电子共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一魔角附近TBG的平带已被广泛研究，但在更小扭角（θ<1°）下晶格弛豫显著重塑莫尔势场；不同长度尺度的莫尔晶格重构如何通过电–力耦合影响电子结构尚不清楚。

Method: 对θ≈0.3–0.7°的小角度TBG器件进行扫描隧道显微镜/扫描隧道谱（STM/STS）测量，获得空间分辨的dI/dV谱；结合唯象模型，分别引入莫尔尺度晶格重构与原子尺度膨胀（柔性电响应）来解释谱学特征。

Result: 在AA位点观察到显著dI/dV峰，且整体谱关于电荷中性点不对称；莫尔晶格重构可在AA位点形成紧局域的重费米子f态，原子晶格膨胀产生的柔性电场进一步显式破坏粒子–空穴对称性并使AA位点f态下移，从而与AB/BA位点的巡游c电子在能量上共存；空间分辨谱定量呈现了这种共存。

Conclusion: 小角度TBG中重费米子模型依然成立，柔性电效应对其低能电子结构有决定性影响；小角度TBG可作为高度可调的莫尔平台，通过扭角和静电背栅分别调控f占据与c密度，用于研究相互作用近藤晶格问题。

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) near the first magic angle ($θ\approx1.05^{\circ}$) hosts flat bands that provide a fertile ground for exploring strongly correlated and topological phases. At smaller twist angles ($θ<1^{\circ}$), attice relaxation profoundly alters the moiré landscape. The extent to which moiré lattice reconstruction at different length scales affects the electronic structure through electro-mechanical coupling remains unresolved. Here, using scanning tunneling microscopy on small-angle TBG devices ($θ\approx0.3-0.7^{\circ}$), we observe a pronounced $dI/dV$ peak at the AA site of the moiré lattice on top of a spectrum that is asymmetric about the charge neutrality point, lattice reconstruction over moiré lattice length scales, as well as a dilation of the atomic lattice at the AA sites. We demonstrate at a phenomenological level that the inclusion of moiré lattice reconstruction supports compact localized heavy fermion $f-$ states localized at AA sites. The additional inclusion of atomic lattice dilation enerates a strong flexo-electric response that explicitly breaks particle-hole symmetry, pushing compact, localized $f$-states at the AA sites down in energy. Consequently, these heavy fermion states energetically coexist with itinerant $c$-electrons at the AB/BA sites. Our spatially resolved spectroscopic measurements quantitatively visualize this coexistence, confirming the persistence of the heavy fermion model in the small-angle regime $θ<1^{\circ}$. Our findings provide microscopic insights into how flexo-electricity affects the low-energy electronic structure of small-angle TBG. More broadly, our study establishes small-angle TBG as a new class of moiré materials to explore the interacting Kondo lattice problem with high tunability of the $f-$occupation and $c-$density through twist-angle and electrostatic back gates, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Time evolution in the multi-component dynamical Keldysh models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38338)
*M. N. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 构造了含长相关时间多分量随机高斯场的动力学量子模型的时间演化算符，精确求解了 SU(2)、U(1)、Z_2 三类对称情形的单粒子格林函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：多分量随机场下动力学量子模型的时间演化通常难以精确处理，尤其当随机场具有长但有限的关联时间时，缺乏系统性解析工具。本文旨在为这类问题建立可精确求解的框架。

Method: 方法：构造时间演化算符；以Keldysh模型（无穷关联时间极限）为出发点，处理多分量（三、二、一）高斯随机场，分别对应 SU(2)、U(1)、Z_2 对称类；对含非零均值的二分量随机场给出Rice分布描述；推导并求解对角与非对角单粒子格林函数的耦合线性微分方程组；用连分式法处理有限关联时间修正。

Result: 结果：得到含非零均值二分量随机场的统计服从Rice分布；获得对角与非对角单粒子格林函数的精确解；有限时间关联的贡献可由连分式形式系统纳入。

Conclusion: 结论：Keldysh型多分量随机场模型可通过耦合线性方程组精确求解，有限关联时间效应可借助连分式方法统一处理，为长关联时间动力学的解析研究提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We construct a time evolution operator for a dynamical quantum models with a multi-component random Gaussian field characterized by a long correlation time. We analyze three paradigmatic cases: non-Abelian $SU(2)$ symmetry class for the three-component Gaussian noise and Abelian symmetry classes $U(1)$ and $Z_2$ for the two- and one-component noises correspondingly. Starting with a limiting case of the infinite correlation time (Keldysh model) we shown that statistics of the two-component random field with a non-zero mean is described by the Rice distribution. We derived and solved the system of coupled linear differential equations for the exact diagonal and off-diagonal single-particle Green's functions. We showed how the finite time correlation in Keldysh models are accounted by a continuous fractions method.

</details>


### [9] [Strain-overlap and surface-confinement engineering in AlScN/YIG magnetoacoustic devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38399)
*Kevin Künstle,Kaya Gauch,Agnė Žukauskaitė,Stephan Barth,Mathias Weiler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将AlScN/YIG/GGG确立为集成压电-磁性异质结构，用于电学激发声表面波（SAW）并耦合至单晶YIG中的自旋波，并指出AlScN厚度是平衡磁弹性衰减与纯SAW传输的关键设计参数。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Using the simulated complex strain components and an effective-driving-field model, we calculate the relative SAW attenuation for several AlScN thicknesses and wavelengths. The calculations qualitatively reproduce the measurements: at all investigated wavelengths, thinner AlScN layers yield stronger relative attenuation. Experimentally, the attenuation approaches 70dB/mm for a 54.5nm-thick AlScN layer at a wavelength of 1.2$μ$m. However, the strongest relative attenuation does not maximize the detectable signal. Instead, the absolute field-induced transmission change peaks at an AlScN thickness of approximately 100nm, with the precise optimum depending on the acoustic wavelength. These results identify the AlScN thickness as a key design parameter for balancing magnetoelastic attenuation and pure SAW transmission in AlScN/YIG devices.

An analytical model identifies the origin of this local response with coherent electron lensing, in which the tip modifies the phase accumulated along interfering trajectories. The measured fringe patterns in the switching current indicate that this mechanism affects superconducting transport, consistent with tip-induced shifts of the resonance conditions for Andreev bound states. These results establish SGM as a tunable local lens for controlling interference in superconducting ballistic graphene devices.

Result: 稳定Wyckoff等价与固定填充下的绝热等价互不蕴含：无自旋Fm'm'm'中Euler class可区分稳定Wyckoff等价构型；无自旋P1̄'中绝热等价构型在稳定Wyckoff形变下仍不同。二者在≤2维及三维自旋系统中一致；三维无自旋系统中，Chern绝缘体泵在134个磁空间群中引入额外等价，这些群保持时空定向且存在site-symmetry群含222、1̄'或4̄'的点。

Abstract: We study the freedom in choosing Wannier centers and the associated symmetry representations in noninteracting Wannierizable insulators. We compare Wyckoff deformations of atomic configurations with symmetry-preserving adiabatic deformations of occupied states, both with and without adding common occupied atomic orbitals. Explicit examples show that stable Wyckoff equivalence and adiabatic equivalence at fixed filling do not imply each other. In spinless $Fm'm'm'$, an Euler class distinguishes stably Wyckoff-equivalent configurations. In spinless $P\overline1'$, adiabatically equivalent configurations remain distinct under stable Wyckoff deformations. We also classify when stable Wyckoff and stable adiabatic equivalence coincide. They agree in two or fewer dimensions and in three-dimensional spinful systems. For three-dimensional spinless systems, Chern insulator pumps give additional equivalences in 134 magnetic space groups. These groups preserve spacetime orientation and have a point whose site-symmetry group contains $222$, $\overline1'$, or $\overline4'$.

</details>

Conclusion: 手征磁效应与反常霍尔效应在轴子介质表面模式中扮演互补且可耦合的角色，其共存导致非互易性和角动量耦合等可观测特征，为轴子电动力学的界面光学探测提供理论依据。

Abstract: Surface plasmon polaritons at the interface between a conductor governed by axion electrodynamics and an ordinary dielectric are studied using a general phenomenological model, in which the macroscopic response is described by an axial vector $ \mathbf{G} $, responsible for the anomalous Hall effect, and a pseudoscalar $ W $, accounting for the chiral magnetic effect. We extend the analysis of the chiral magnetic effect in the planar geometry, address its role in the cylindrical one and, in both geometries, discuss the case in which the two effects coexist. We find an upper bound on $ W $, a nonreciprocity requiring both effects and the absence of freely propagating modes, depending on the orientation of $ \mathbf{G} $, for the planar interface, and, for the cylindrical one, a novel coupling between the linear and orbital angular momenta of the modes, which already arises from the chiral magnetic effect alone and persists when the anomalous Hall effect is also present. The exact eigenmodes of the axion medium are derived, and the symmetries and propagation features of the surface modes are made explicit by analytical and asymptotic evaluation of the dispersion relations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由费米子与粒子增益/损耗耦合会弛豫到高斯稳态，每个自然轨道有独立占据数。作者希望理解当哈密顿量随机、且有大量随机增益/损耗通道时，这些占据数的统计分布形态，以及哈密顿量在粒子寿命内跨能级输运对占据数差异的平均作用是否充分。

Conclusion: 增益/损耗驱动的高斯稳态中，占据数分布由浴主导而非哈密顿量细节，展宽的浴上界普遍成立。这为随机开放费米子系统的稳态统计与粒子数涨落提供了简洁的普适描述。

Abstract: Free fermions coupled to particle gain and loss relax to a Gaussian steady state, in which every natural orbital has its own occupation. We find the distribution of these occupations $\varrho_{\rm st}$ for a random Hamiltonian coupled to many random gain and loss channels. Each orbital is filled and emptied at its own rates, and the Hamiltonian, moving particles across the levels during their lifetime, averages the differences between the orbitals. We show that this averaging is incomplete: the spread of the occupations decreases only as the inverse square root of the number of channels per level. At large system size the distribution depends on the grain Hamiltonian only through its density of states $ν(E)$. The spread is bounded by the baths alone: even Hamiltonians with unbounded, heavy-tailed spectra cannot widen it beyond this bound. The spread suppresses the particle-number fluctuations below their binomial value.

</details>

Abstract: The structure of the electron and hole quantum confinement levels in spherical PbS and PbSe nanocrystals (NCs) is studied within the framework of a four-band effective-mass model. This level structure was initially calculated within the Kang--Wise model, which employs the standard boundary condition of vanishing carrier wave functions at the NC surface. This model, however, does not allow one to account for the effect of the NC surface on the level structure. Here, the effect of the NC surface is modeled using general boundary conditions (GBCs) imposed on the envelope wave functions at the surface. We show that the surface properties modify the size dependence of the electron and hole confinement levels and lead to an additional spin--orbit splitting of the $1P_{h,e}$ levels. Using the GBCs, we are able to quantitatively describe the size dependence of the lowest optical transitions in bare PbS and PbSe NCs and explain the differences between the absorption spectra of bare and Cl-passivated PbS NCs

### [15] [HERA: Assessing an Autonomous Agent for Quantum Device Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39074)
*Zachary W. Gomez,Nathan Bittner,Jordan T. McCourt,Qingrui Cao,Sandesh S. Kalantre,Benjamin H. Alexander,Zhiren Zheng,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Aaron Sharpe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: HERA是一个基于专家知识库的自主智能体，可完成量子器件低温输运测量，在双盲对照中结论与专家一致，并能自主发现与表征新材料体系的超导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体已开始执行实验室测量，但其科学性取决于结论能否经受审查。由于模型具有随机性、且上下文会显著左右其结论，必须通过受控实验来验证其可靠性；本文旨在建立并评估这样一种可审查的自主实验智能体。

Method: 提出HERA智能体，依托实验室成员整理的知识库（含测量协议、软件工具与文献参考）自主开展量子器件低温输运测量。采用三类评估：与专家对两个多层石墨烯器件堆叠顺序进行盲测对照；对知识库做消融实验；以及数百次离线回放（模型仅设计核心测量方案而不实际执行）。

Result: HERA在两个器件上的结论均与专家封存的结论一致；消融与回放实验量化了知识库的影响，显示在具备知识库时各模型都设计出更优的测量方案且推理更严谨；在迁移到新问题（另一材料体系的超导性发现与表征）时，仅需数小时专家时间开发新协议，HERA即可自主完成全过程。

Conclusion: 实验表明，配备面向任务定制的知识库后，语言模型智能体能够开展可靠、可审查的实验科学工作。

Abstract: Large language model agents have begun to carry out laboratory measurements, but their scientific conclusions are useful only if they hold up under scrutiny. Demonstrating this requires controlled studies because models are stochastic and the context a model is given steers its conclusions. Here we introduce HERA, an autonomous agent designed to carry out cryogenic transport measurements of quantum devices. HERA draws on a knowledge base curated by lab members that holds measurement protocols, software tools, and literature references. We assessed HERA with a blinded trial against an expert on the stacking order of two multilayer graphene devices, an ablation of its knowledge base, and hundreds of offline replays in which models designed the trial's central measurement without running it. HERA's conclusions matched the expert's sealed conclusions on both tested devices. The ablation and the replay studies measured the impact of the knowledge base across different models, showing that with it every model designed better measurements and reasoned more soundly. To test how readily HERA extends to a new problem, we tasked it with finding and characterizing superconductivity in a different material system. This required a few hours of expert time to develop a new experimental protocol, after which HERA did both autonomously. These results show that, with a knowledge base designed for the task, language model agents are capable of sound experimental science.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum three-wave mixing is an important resource in superconducting quantum circuits. The traditional implementation uses Josephson junction arrays and is activated by magnetic flux. Here we introduce the Berry curvature dipole (BCD) quantum circuit element, which achieves quantum three-wave mixing without breaking time-reversal symmetry. This quantum three-wave mixing results from the BCD nonlinear Hall effect in noncentrosymmetric quantum materials taken to a dissipationless, quantum coherent few-photon regime. We show how nonlinear Hall materials can be integrated to superconducting microwave circuits to accomplish multiphoton vacuum Rabi splittings and dynamical stabilization of bosonic Schrödinger cat states. Such BCD circuit quantum electrodynamics are a concrete exemplar of a physical resource for quantum information processing enabled by nonlinear response in quantum materials.

</details>

Motivation: 交变磁体兼具净零磁化与强各向异性自旋劈裂，其d波对称性如何在界面处调控热驱动磁振子自旋输运尚不清楚，尤其是AM域翻转下FM/AM与AFM/AM结的非互易行为及可测信号有待阐明。

Method: 基于界面磁振子隧穿理论，将结电流分解为与界面角无关的分量及具d波形式的角度依赖分量；针对亚晶格对称界面分析FM/AM与AFM/AM结在AM域翻转下的对称性，并引入跨界面成对自旋的选择性隧穿图像（自旋平行时透射、反平行时阻断）进行解释。

Result: FM/AM结中角度依赖分量为奇，经域平均后抵消；AFM/AM结中角度无关分量因对称性为零，角度依赖分量在固定Néel矢量下可经域平均保留。AFM/AM结表现为磁振子阀：成对自旋反平行的构型抑制电流，抑制程度受磁振子亚晶格混合限制。各向异性隧穿表现为[310]与[130]法向界面间电流反号；固定界面下电流幅值可读取AM相对于AFM的Néel矢量取向。

Conclusion: d波交变磁体结的热磁振子自旋流由选择性界面隧穿主导，FM/AM与AFM/AM结呈互补的域平均行为；该效应既提供界面电流反号的可测判据，又使AFM/AM结成为可读取交变磁体Néel矢量的磁振子阀。

Abstract: We investigate the thermally driven magnon spin current across a junction between a $d$-wave altermagnet (AM) and a ferromagnet (FM) or an antiferromagnet (AFM).
  The current consists of a component independent of the interface angle and an angularly dependent component with the $d$-wave form.
  For a sublattice-symmetric interface, the two junctions behave oppositely under reversal of the altermagnetic domain: in the FM/AM junction the angularly dependent component is odd and cancels on averaging over the domains, whereas in the AFM/AM junction the angularly independent component vanishes by symmetry and the angularly dependent component survives the domain average at a fixed Néel vector of the AFM.
  Both follow from a selective tunneling: a pair of spins bonded across the interface transmits magnons when the spins are parallel and not when antiparallel, which makes the AFM/AM junction a magnon valve: the configuration in which the bonded spins are antiparallel blocks the current up to the sublattice admixture of the magnons.
  The anisotropic tunneling is detectable as the sign reversal of the current between the interfaces normal to $[310]$ and $[130]$, and the valve reads the Néel vector of the altermagnet relative to that of the antiferromagnet through the magnitude of the current at a fixed interface.

Abstract: Solid-state color centers embedded into mechanical resonators, where orbital states mediate interactions between spins and long-lived phonons, are a promising platform for hybrid quantum systems. The negatively charged silicon vacancy center in diamond (SiV-) is particularly favorable for strain-coupling to nanomechanical resonators because of its large spin-strain susceptibility and compatibility with device integration. Tensioned nanomechanical resonators offer an attractive route due to their ultra-long mechanical coherence times enabled by diluting defect-induced dissipation, though it is unclear if the same dilution effect undermines strain-mediated coupling to material defect centers. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a tensioned diamond nanobeam platform that enables enhanced strain coupling to SiV- orbital states. Through monitoring the optical response of the SiVs to the static and dynamical strain of the mechanical resonator, we observe that dynamical strain localizes near the clamps of these resonators under static strain. This allows us to enhance SiV-mechanical coupling and the mechanical coherence simultaneously, and provide a SiV-strain sensitivity with greatly improved accuracy over previous literature values. Thus, our work opens a pathway towards seconds-long coherence hybrid quantum systems utilizing point defects in diamond nanomechanics for quantum information processing.

Abstract: Multilayer graphenes have emerged as a versatile avenue towards exploring novel electronic phases of matter. Recently, nanoscale multilayer graphene islands have been realized experimentally, where the island is less than 50 nm in diameter and distinct in layer configuration from the contact portion of the same graphene sheets, the latter of which act as leads for transport probes. Here, we theoretically investigate how the proximity between these islands and their chemically identical environment modifies the electronic structure. Within the tight-binding approximation, we find that the proximity effect is enhanced by the closely matched momenta between both parts, creating an unusually strong band renormalization which persists as the island size is increased, and only limited by the coherence length. An effective single-unit-cell Hamiltonian to capture these modifications is derived, expressed in terms of interlayer hopping parameters that scale with the relative island area. We predict a substantially altered low-energy density of states for 3- and 4-layer graphene islands, demonstrating the tunability of the electronic structure by spatial size.

Abstract: We investigate persistent charge and spin currents in a one-dimensional quantum ring threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm (AB) flux, considering generalized Aubry-André-Harper (AAH) modulation with staggered onsite energies and antiferromagnetic (AFM) ordering in both the noninteracting and interacting regimes, with the latter treated using a self-consistent Hartree-Fock mean field theory (MFT). At half filling, two distinct current-enhancement peaks emerge as the modulation strength increases, with quasiperiodic hopping modulation playing a crucial role in establishing the two-peak structure within a restricted parameter regime. Spin-resolved bond-current profiles reveal a switch in the dominant spin contribution between the two peaks, while the inverse participation ratio (IPR) and flux-dependent spin-resolved ground-state energies provide complementary insights into the spatial characteristics of the eigenstates and their spin-dependent flux sensitivity. At quarter filling, the spin current exhibits a single enhancement peak, in contrast to the two-peak response observed at half filling. The Hubbard interaction affects the two half-filled peaks differently: it suppresses the primary peak, whereas the secondary peak exhibits a nonmonotonic, re-entrant response, with the current recovering at finite interaction strength. The interacting results are further characterized through the Drude weight, and comparisons with exact diagonalization (ED) and density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations for a finite half-filled system support the reliability of the MFT description. Our findings elucidate how quasiperiodic modulation, AFM order, band filling, and electronic correlations collectively govern the magnitude and spin-resolved character of persistent currents, giving rise to multiple enhancement regimes and interaction-induced re-entrant behavior.

Conclusion: Chern数是调控等离激元诱导磁化的关键参数，拓扑边缘等离激元的磁化行为与常规边缘等离激元存在本质差异。

Abstract: Higher Chern insulators host multiple dissipationless chiral edge states, one per unit of Chern number. We study the collective plasmon modes of these edge states at a domain wall between Chern numbers $\mathcal{C}$ and $-\mathcal{C}$, using a semiclassical model with Coulomb interactions. The plasmon dispersion is linear at long wavelengths, with a velocity that increases linearly with $\mathcal{C}$. As in conventional two-dimensional metals, the edge plasmon generates an elliptically polarized electric field that decays into the bulk and drives electrons in circular orbits, producing a static orbital magnetization (inverse Faraday effect). For topological edge plasmons, the magnetization has a nontrivial spatial profile set by two competing length scales: the finite extent of the edge states and the plasmon wavelength. At fixed edge-charge amplitude, it grows strongly with $\mathcal{C}$. At the domain wall, it approaches a finite plateau at low frequency and decays as $1/ω^2$ at high frequency. Its temperature dependence is nonmonotonic, with a maximum at an intermediate temperature. These results show how the Chern number controls plasmon-induced magnetization, in contrast to conventional edge plasmons.

</details>

Result: 本章指出eNVM器件凭借类突触电导调制与信息保持能力，可支撑稠密低功耗神经形态电路；其可扩展性使其适用于大规模神经形态架构，并总结了各类器件在系统集成方面的近期进展。

Conclusion: eNVM（ReRAM、PCM、自旋电子存储器等）是推进神经形态计算的关键使能技术，未来需在器件特性、集成与应用层面平衡其挑战与机遇。

Abstract: This chapter explores the critical role of emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) devices in advancing neuromorphic computing. Inspired by the human brain's remarkable ability to perform complex tasks like pattern recognition and adaptation, neuromorphic computing offers immense potential for implementing complex neural networks (NNs) in real hardware. Despite ongoing research into the brain's complexities, its fundamental operation relies on neurons (processing units communicating via electrochemical signals) and synapses (junctions between neurons). The modifiable strength of the synapse is key to learning and memory functionality, allowing the brain to adapt and rewire itself. For dense and low-power circuits in neuromorphic computing, these eNVM devices are ideal due to their synaptic-like behavior and non-volatile information storage. Properties such as conductance modulation of these devices emulate synaptic plasticity, which is crucial for learning, and their scalability makes them promising for large-scale neuromorphic architectures. This chapter will delve into the various types of eNVM devices such as resistive RAM (ReRAM), phase-change memory (PCM), spintronics-based memory devices, and their applications in neuromorphic computing, discussing their challenges and opportunities, and highlighting recent advances in their integration within neuromorphic systems.

</details>

Abstract: Twisting two stacked graphene layers superimposes a moiré superlattice that reshapes their electronic structure, yet how it shapes the collective optical response of finite, nanoscale bilayers remains largely unexplored. Using an atomistic tight-binding and self-consistent density-matrix approach, we compute the twist-dependent plasmonic response of twisted bilayer graphene nanoislands comprising thousands of atoms. The spectra are strongly tunable and split into two regimes at a critical angle where a single moiré wavelength spans the island; beyond it, an ordered ladder of resonances emerges whenever an integer number of moiré periods fits within the overlapping bilayer. In doped islands, the interlayer coupling also produces a moiré-enabled Born--Kuhn chiral plasmon: opposite transverse dipoles in the two layers form an electric quadrupole that hybridizes with the bright dipole, opening an anticrossing near the critical angle, with a handedness that reverses with the twist direction. Triangular islands confirm that the moiré lattice, not the flake boundary, governs both effects, establishing twist as a mechanical handle on chiral graphene plasmons.

Scanning tunnelling spectroscopy (STS) and Kelvin probe force spectroscopy (KPFS) have been used to map electronic states and local contact potential differences (LCPD). Compared to close-packed $C_{60}$ islands, the stripe phase exhibits a larger interfacial dipole and increased occupation of previously unoccupied $C_{60}$ states, consistent with enhanced charge transfer from Ag(111) to $C_{60}$. Analysis indicates that larger $C_{60}$ spacing in the stripe geometry reduces Coulomb repulsion, stabilizing the intermixed phase and enhancing charge transfer with respect to close-packed domains. These results provide direct evidence of charged $C_{60}$ molecules in stripe structures and guidelines for engineering intermixed SAMs with tailored interfacial electronic properties.

Abstract: Multiterminal Josephson junctions have recently attracted substantial attention. Abstract: Coupling quantum materials to electromagnetic cavities or resonators without external driving has gained prominence as a new control mechanism. Modeling the resonator as a lumped-element circuit, we show that the coupling enhances, rather than suppresses, the superconducting transition temperature $T_c$. When the sample is smaller than the Pearl length, phase fluctuations near the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) transition strongly amplify this enhancement. Crucially, the resistive quasiparticle response of the sample must be included in the analysis: at low frequencies the superfluid shunts the normal-fluid channel, reducing the total sample impedance. For representative parameters, a $1 \,μ\mathrm{m} \times 1 \,μ\mathrm{m}$, $8 $nm-thick disordered film, we find a relative $T_c$ enhancement of about $1\%$, an order of magnitude larger than the $0.1\% $ obtained when phase fluctuations are neglected. Ghaemi-Dizicheh, Phys.\ Rev.\ B \textbf{107}, 125155 (2023)] to one-dimensional spinful non-Hermitian tight-binding lattices with matrix-valued Abelian and non-Abelian gauge couplings. We derive a minimal channel-resolved transfer matrix and identify its eigenvalues with the generalized Bloch factors. The same transfer matrix determines the periodic- and open-boundary spectra, generalized Brillouin zone, skin localization, and finite-sample scattering conditions. In the Abelian limit, the model separates into fixed spin sectors, yielding exact spectral results and closed finite-length transport expressions in terms of Chebyshev polynomials. These results reveal bipolar spin-dependent skin localization and distinguish the reciprocal hopping scale, which governs poles, zeros, and reflectionlessness, from the directed-amplification factor controlling transmission asymmetry, transparency, and invisibility. For non-Abelian gauge fields, noncommuting link matrices coherently mix the spin sectors, such that four transfer eigenmodes jointly determine the non-Bloch spectrum and transport eigenchannels. We show that this mixing produces spin-hybridized lasing and coherent-perfect-absorption channels, Zeeman-tunable lasing thresholds, and direction-dependent reflectionless transport accompanied by pronounced spin conversion. The resulting boundary-condition criteria describe lasing, coherent perfect absorption, reflectionlessness, transparency, and invisibility, while identifying spin polarization and conversion as experimentally accessible signatures that distinguish Abelian from non-Abelian non-Hermitian transport.

Abstract: Van Hove (vH) singularities can enhance superconductivity by generating a large pairing susceptibility. Abstract: Ordinary $0$-form symmetries $G$ can mix with $1$-form symmetries $Π_2$ to form a $2$-group $\mathcal{G}$. We give a concrete lattice definition for a class of finite $2$-group symmetries in terms of local relations among symmetry operators. These relations identify the Postnikov class $[ρ]\in H^3(BG,Π_2)$ that characterizes the mixing. We develop a method to extract anomalies of these symmetries in one spatial dimension by restricting symmetry operations to an interval and comparing their composition near its endpoints. We also construct explicit qubit symmetry actions in $d=1,2,3$ spatial dimensions for a $2$-group with $G=Π_2=\mathbb{Z}_2$. These actions share the same nontrivial Postnikov class but include both anomaly-free and anomalous realizations. Their cohomological anomalies are classified by $H^{d+2}(B\mathcal G,U(1))$, which is $\mathbb{Z}_2$ for $d=1,2$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ for $d=3$, and our constructions realize every class in these dimensions. Our results connect higher-group symmetry and its anomalies to concrete lattice operations, providing a microscopic framework for studying anomalous symmetries.

Abstract: Preparing complex quantum states of light for quantum information processing tasks often proceeds by converting from simpler states via experimentally accessible operations. We provide fundamental bounds on such probabilistic conversions, both exact and approximate. To do so, we establish a theory of weak, fully relative majorization that compares the disorder of phase-space functions. Our framework is versatile and can be tailored to different systems and sets of allowed operations. Focusing on Wigner functions in multimode photonic systems, we establish rigorous no-go results and tradeoff relations for exact and approximate conversions involving squeezed states, cat states, Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill states, and Fock states. In doing so, we derive novel bounds and recover known tight ones. Our results demonstrate that phase-space disorder imposes strong limitations on photonic state preparation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文刻画了 Grover 算法全过程中的 non-stabilizerness（magic），给出每轮迭代末编译无关 magic 的解析预测（稳定子 Rényi 熵保持 intensive），并证明 magic 开销强烈依赖于是否已知解：已知解时存在有界 magic 的分解，而 agnostic 实现（如 3-SAT）产生 volume-law magic，从而排除基于稳定子方法的高效模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover 算法是量子优势的教科书范例，但其相对于可高效经典模拟的 stabilizer 形式究竟消耗多少“量子资源”（magic）尚不清楚；同时已有工作多关注理想线路，而实际编译到基本门会额外引入 magic，需厘清该开销及 solution-awareness 的作用。

Result: 得到每轮迭代末 stabilizer Rényi entropy 的解析预测，并证明其随系统规模增长仍保持 intensive；当搜索问题的解已知时，存在高效分解，即使使用 extensive 数量的 Toffoli 门也能使 magic 保持有界；而 agnostic 实现在 3-SAT 上确认为 volume-law non-stabilizerness。

Conclusion: Grover 算法的 magic 增长与 solution-awareness 在电路编译中的作用得到澄清：编译无关 magic 本身为 intensive，但 agnostic 编译会导致 volume-law magic，使基于稳定子的方法在实际相关情形下无法高效模拟。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm is a textbook example of quantum advantage, providing a quadratic query speedup over classical methods for unstructured search. In this Letter, we characterize the non-stabilizerness, or magic, developed throughout Grover's protocol, quantifying quantum complexity with respect to the efficiently simulable stabilizer states. First, we compute the compilation-independent magic at the end of each Grover iteration, deriving an analytic prediction for the stabilizer Rényi entropy and proving that it remains intensive as the system size grows. We then investigate the magic overhead due to decomposing the circuit into elementary gates. When the solution to the search problem is known, we show that an efficient decomposition exists that keeps magic bounded despite using an extensive number of Toffoli gates. In contrast, agnostic implementations are expected to generate volume-law non-stabilizerness, as we confirm for the 3-SAT problem, which rules out efficient simulation by stabilizer-based methods in practically relevant cases. Our findings clarify the growth of magic in Grover's algorithm and the role of solution-awareness in its circuit compilation.

</details>

Abstract: The characterization of possible transformations between quantum states and the quantification of entanglement are central for understanding quantum resources. Explicit results are, however, scarce since they require complicated optimizations over transformation protocols or state decompositions. We introduce a method to optimize functions of the entanglement spectrum of bipartite states over mixed-state decompositions with arbitrary accuracy. Our results are based on a convergent semidefinite programming hierarchy of optimizations over entanglement spectra using a natural polynomial basis and Schur-Weyl duality. Our approach can also be used to determine non-polynomial measures, such as the entanglement of formation, and to characterize the dimensionality of entanglement. Moreover, extracting the decomposition allows us to formulate criteria to determine state transformations under local operations and classical communication (LOCC). In particular, we provide a hierarchy of certificates of LOCC non-convertibility from pure to mixed states in any dimension and a heuristic approach for LOCC convertibility.

Abstract: We review a series of recent no-go theorems that extend the methodological and conceptual framework introduced by Bell's theorem to more sophisticated operational scenarios involving independent sources, quantum networks, joint measurements, and multiple observers. Many of these results were originally developed within quantum information science and are scattered across the literature, where their technical and application-driven success has partly obscured their broader foundational significance. We organize them around a common Bell-inspired methodology that extends beyond quantum nonlocality, and emphasize how increasingly rich operational structures change which physical assumptions can be tested.

Abstract: We formulate the standard quantum teleportation protocol within the framework of deformation quantization, employing the Stratonovich--Weyl (SW) correspondence and the associated star product on the coadjoint orbits of $SU(2)$. Each qubit is represented by a spherical phase space $S^2$ endowed with the Kirillov--Kostant--Souriau (KKS) symplectic structure. Within this setting, we explicitly construct the phase space representations of the entangled Bell resource, the controlled-NOT and Hadamard operators, the projective measurements, and the conditional Pauli corrections. We demonstrate how the quantum information initially encoded in the Wigner function for the input qubit is redistributed through bipartite and tripartite phase space correlations. Upon consideration of a conditional unitary transformation, the measurement outcome produces a Wigner function on the receiver's coadjoint orbit that is related to the input by an outcome-dependent rotation, which can subsequently be inverted through the implementation of an appropriate Pauli correction. The covariance of the SW correspondence provides a geometric interpretation of this process, in which quantum information is redistributed and reconstructed without transferring the underlying symplectic geometry. Our formulation can also be extended to study quantum teleportation in more general phase space geometries equipped with suitable star products.

Abstract: With D. Aerts, we showed in 2014, within the extended Bloch representation of quantum mechanics, that the Born rule can be understood as resulting from a universal average over all the possible ways in which the measurement-interactions can be selected. The cellular construction used there was since questioned, in the context of Bertrand's paradox, by N. Shackel, who observed that the result depends on the discretization adopted, that the sequence of averages is constant, and that the sense in which the average is taken over "all" densities remains unclear. Taking these criticisms as a stimulus, we re-examine the notion. The key is to recognize that a universal measurement is a random way of selecting a measurement which does not privilege any portion of the structure describing the available interactions, a property we call indifference. We prove that every indifferent universal measurement, however constructed, produces exactly the Born probabilities: the universal average is unique, and the cellular construction of 2014 is one among infinitely many ways of obtaining it, exact at every finite resolution. Non-trivial indifferent universal measurements exist, but must involve deterministic measurements: Dirichlet processes are indifferent universal measurements reaching every way of selecting an interaction, whereas an indifferent universal measurement free of deterministic components necessarily selects the uniform density, so that the Born rule holds not only on average but with certainty. Deviations from the quantum statistics in sequences of measurements are governed by a single parameter, in principle accessible to experiment. The only geometric inputs are the Euclidean length of the membrane and the orthogonal landing of the point-particle on it; the latter is where the content of Gleason's theorem lies. Generalizations to N outcomes and to Bertrand's paradox are discussed.

</details>

Abstract: A unified Fourier framework for quantum weight enumerators of prime-power-dimensional qudits is developed in this paper. We introduce the notions of Weyl power spectrum and Weyl-conjugation autocorrelation for the Weyl-operator quantities nderlying the Shor--Laflamme weight enumerators. We further prove that all twisted Fourier transforms are Weyl translations of either the ordinary MacWilliams transform or the Rains shadow transform. This two-class reduction implies that the twists yield no additional independent coefficientwise positivity constraints under compatible projections. Nevertheless, the framework leads to refined MacWilliams and shadow identities that retain more Fourier information than ordinary Hamming weight enumerators. We employ these refined identities in linear programming, together with simplex mapping and shadow-coset counting, to exclude surviving candidate weight distributions. These techniques yield tighter bounds for binary and qutrit quantum codes, including nonexistence results for specific code parameters.

Abstract: Generative learning of a quantum measurement distribution from finite data requires choosing a model capacity that balances representation accuracy against estimation cost. In the well-sampled regime, $V(s,N)\simeq[NC(s)]^{-1}$, and exponential or algebraic laws for $B$ and $C$ yield four scaling templates for the optimal error, from inverse powers of $N$ to inverse powers of $\ln N$. For local autoregressive models, the context radius sets the model capacity, and $B$ is the summed conditional mutual information lost by truncating the context. For transverse-field Ising Born distributions, the independently evaluated bias and estimation cost predict both the optimal context radius and the optimal error. In the well-sampled regime, the estimation cost grows exponentially with context radius. Its competition with approximately exponential bias decay in the gapped regimes predicts power-law error decay in $N$, while algebraic bias decay at criticality predicts asymptotic inverse-power decay in $\ln N$. Bulk-site results are consistent with the corresponding finite-sample predictions. These results connect the structure of quantum measurement distributions to the model capacity justified by finite data.

Abstract: Continuous variable quantum key distribution enables two parties to extract provably secure keys for cryptography by exploiting aspects of quantum mechanics. A central challenge in achieving QKD over long distances is overcoming optical loss, which limits the secret key rates and achievable transmission distances. Recent work has illustrated how to perform continuous variable quantum key distribution while eliminating information leakage to an eavesdropper in a pure-loss channel and minimizing information leakage in a noisy channel. In this work, we analyse this protocol under conditions where the quantum channel linking the sender and receiver is a fading channel, which can be used to model free-space links. We find that the protocol maintains strong performance over fading channels and outperforms comparable protocols.

Abstract: Quantum open-source software provides essential infrastructure for quantum science, and artificial intelligence (AI) is reshaping its capabilities and transforming its development. However, verifying quantum software at scale remains difficult, and AI-generated contributions can increase the workload of maintainers. In this Perspective, we examine verification as a central requirement for reliable AI-assisted quantum software development and research. We analyze public repositories, records covering 110 repositories from five annual quantum open-source hackathons that we organize, and responses from more than 1600 participants in a community survey. Our analysis connects limitations in automated verification with the review burden on quantum software maintainers. We propose verification practices and contribution standards that require contributors to support their changes with reproducible evidence for maintainer review. We also examine how quantum open-source software can provide infrastructure for AI-driven research whose results the community can independently verify and build upon. We maintain an open-source companion repository to support reproducibility and help projects adopt and refine these proposals. Together, these contributions help the quantum open-source software community translate advances in AI into reliable quantum research through reproducible verification.

</details>

The nuclei are prepared in a spin superposition, while a magnetic field induces Zeeman splitting. The resulting nuclear energy phase is coherently transferred to the neutron, producing an intensity beat at the nuclear Zeeman frequency. The nuclear and neutron phases then heterodyne coherently, producing a signal at the sum or difference frequency depending on their relative helicity.

### [57] [Experimental Observation of Error-Resilience Phase Transition on Superconducting Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38148)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机酉变换将量子信息扰乱到高度非局域自由度中从而抵抗局域噪声，但Turkeshi与Sierant的理论工作表明局域噪声足够强时该保护会失效，并预言了临界相干噪声角α_c≈1.14处的相变。此前该相变缺乏实验验证，本文旨在首次在真实硬件上观测并刻画该转变。

Abstract: Random unitaries scramble quantum information into highly nonlocal degrees of freedom, thereby providing protection from local noise. However, as demonstrated by Turkeshi and Sierant (Phys. Rev. Lett. 132, 140401, 2024 and arXiv:2603.00235), this protection may fail once local noise grows sufficiently strong. These works describe a phase transition in a noisy random encoding-decoding circuit model between an error-protecting phase, in which encoded information is recoverable, and an error-vulnerable phase, in which encoded information is lost, with the transition occurring at a critical coherent noise angle $α_c \approx 1.14$. Here, we present the first experimental observation of this transition, conducted on IBM's ibm_pittsburgh Heron r3 device for system sizes of up to $N = 26$ qubits. We detect this transition via three order parameters: fidelity, Rényi-2 entanglement entropy, and stabilizer Rényi-2 entropy. For the latter two, we employ randomized measurement protocols to construct estimators, and for all three, we rely on zero-noise extrapolation and M3 SPAM error mitigation. Inputting noiseless simulated data into the same model yields $α_c \approx 1.05$, implying that the discrepancy between experiment and theory is due to our encoding circuits, which are not deep enough to be truly Haar-random. Abstract: Passive loss removes probability from a system and therefore seems unable to improve an unconditional target. This is one T gate more per circuit than the best measurement-free variant that keeps one clean ancilla. For randomized circuits that do not measure, this is within a factor of eight of optimal up to an additive constant, even with extra qubits, and when each random circuit computes a Boolean function exactly, closing the factor asymptotically is equivalent to determining the leading constant in the T-count of exact multi-controlled Toffoli gates. Finally, the oracle of the indicator of a random set of fixed density needs exponentially many T gates at constant error, while that of an affine subspace of the same density needs a constant number, and extra qubits reduce the exponential cost only quadratically.

Abstract: A coherent sum of quantum circuits introduces interference that can alter the training signal. Beyond this lower bound, we derive exact loss and gradient formulas that quantify the additional contributions from interference between branches. One of these terms depends on relative coefficient phases and vanishes when the branch ensemble is invariant under multiplication by $i \mathbb{I}$; a four-qubit matchgate example shows that it can be nonzero. Conclusion: 有效恢复噪声由噪声强度、物理位置及加解密协议结构共同决定。

Abstract: We investigate noise propagation in encrypted quantum cloning by formulating the protocol in terms of effective recovery channels. For local single-qubit completely positive trace-preserving noise, a Pauli-basis expansion of the Kraus operators reveals a strong dependence on the location of the physical channel. Noise on an auxiliary signal qubit leaves the reduced recovered state unchanged, whereas noise on an auxiliary key qubit induces a Pauli-diagonal effective channel on the recovered qubit. For the core qubits, the error propagation additionally depends on the parity of the number of entangled pairs. In particular, for noise on the core key qubit with odd parity, distinct physical Pauli sectors merge into the same effective sector, converting isotropic depolarizing noise into an anisotropic recovery channel. We also relate Werner-type resource imperfections to local depolarizing noise and distinguish distributed product depolarization from genuine global depolarization. The results demonstrate that the effective recovery noise is governed not only by noise strength, but also by its physical location and by the structure of the encryption-decryption protocol.

Abstract: Rapid preparation of a target quantum state and reset to the many-body ground state are essential for quantum information processing. Guided by this insight, we design a reset protocol in which a finite-duration local drive activelysuppresses the relatively slow-decaying components. For the parameters studied, this reduces the time to reach a fixed residual-excitation threshold by up to approximately 25% compared with free decay. To prepare a target Dicke state, we introduce a two-step protocol that initially leaves one emitter undriven before restoring the full drive that stabilizes the target. This local control redistributes spectral weight into the more subradiant modes, driving them closer to their target amplitudes, which reduces the preparation time by up to approximately 45% while consuming less driving energy than continuous driving. Finally, we show how directional waveguide coupling shifts collective modes toward a preferred boundary through the non-Hermitian skin effect, providing guidance for selecting the preferred site for local operations. We term this fundamental dependence of the relaxation dynamics on the system's chirality the ``non-Hermitian Skin-Mpemba effect.'' These results demonstrate how selective local control can accelerate state preparation and reset in open quantum systems.

Abstract: Heralded atom loss is a dominant noise channel in neutral atom quantum computing platforms, and losses at a two-qubit gate are strongly correlated: when one atom is lost, its gate partner is often lost too. This work shows how to exploit this correlation to construct a better decoder, focusing on [[144,12,12]] Gross code syndrome extraction circuits. Given a loss readout, the possible pairings of the lost qubits according to shared gates form a graph whose maximum matchings carry almost all of the conditional probability mass. Our maximal-matching-mixture (MMM) decoder builds a per-readout decoding hypergraph as a weighted mixture over exactly these loss scenarios. ### [65] [Schur$\unicode{x2013}$Weyl Reduction of Finite-Copy Precision Bounds for Multiparameter Quantum Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38299)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In multiparameter quantum estimation, incompatible measurements can impose trade-offs among estimation errors. Understanding the precision attainable with a finite number of jointly measured copies is therefore important, but direct optimization becomes costly as the tensor product space grows exponentially. For independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) copies, the quantum statistical model is invariant under permutations of the copies. We exploit this symmetry through Schur--Weyl duality to remove permutation multiplicity spaces while using their dimensions as weights. This gives a reduced statistical model equivalent to the original under arbitrary collective measurements. On this reduced model, we derive an exact conic formulation of the tight Cramér--Rao bound and a semidefinite program for its Nagaoka--Hayashi (NH) relaxation. For fixed single-copy dimension, the reduced operator representation grows polynomially rather than exponentially with the number of copies. To approximate the tight bound, we use an exact quadratic identity to correct finite-ray dual solutions and obtain rigorous upper and lower bounds. For fixed copy number and parameter dimension, sufficiently fine meshes admit feasible primal points, and dual solutions with bounded objective gaps have uniformly bounded compact-search radii. These results yield quadratic convergence in the projective covering radius. Numerical examples illustrate the quadratic scaling of the mesh correction term and the computational reach of the reduced NH formulation.

Specifically, when a cost angle is varied, the relevant geometry is encoded by the contact region in the interaction line graph. At fixed circuit depth on graphs of bounded degree, the number of distinct circuit settings required for the corresponding derivative collapses from $O(m)$ to $O(1)$. This shows that dynamical locality is not merely a constraint on correlation propagation, but also a computational resource that enables exact gradient evaluation with fewer circuit settings in quantum optimization.

Abstract: The quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) is a paradigmatic variational quantum algorithm for combinatorial optimization problems, yet on current quantum devices the available qubit count and achievable circuit depth remain the main bottlenecks to its practical application. Divide-and-merge reduces the number of variables encoded in a single quantum circuit, while contraction based on coarse graining extends the effective range of shallow circuits, but existing work has not addressed both challenges simultaneously. We propose symmetry-preserving hierarchical QAOA (SP-HQAOA), in which parity sector contraction preserves the zero-field Ising structure of the effective objective at every contraction step, thereby addressing both within a single algorithm. We further prove that this reduction is the unique one with this property. Successive contractions further decouple the maximum leaf block size $B$ from the qubit limit $Q$ per quantum call, so every quantum circuit uses at most $Q$ qubits even when $B>Q$. We also establish a deterministic repair-risk bound on the possible loss in optimal value caused by each sector restriction, and the resulting bounds can be summed across contraction steps and levels of the recursive hierarchy. With depth $p=1$, SP-HQAOA attains $99.4\%$ to $99.7\%$ of the exact optimum on $N=40$ random regular MaxCut leaf blocks and, with at most $10$ qubits per circuit, $96\%$ of the classical reference for $N=500$. SP-HQAOA demonstrates the capacity to use shallow quantum circuits to address combinatorial optimization problems whose variable count far exceeds the qubit limit of a single quantum call, and may enable practical quantum optimization advantages in the near term.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) provides a unifying framework for quantum algorithms by applying the polynomials of quantum signal processing (QSP) to block-encoded matrices. We connect this framework with spectral analysis, design and topological protection in condensed matter physics. Extending QSVT beyond matrices to possibly infinite-dimensional operators (operator QSVT), we establish the following. First, it transforms every block of the operator by the QSP polynomials; no singular value decomposition is needed. Second, a spectral mapping theorem determines the full spectrum of the QSVT operator, including continuous spectra, from the embedded operator through the QSP polynomial, allowing spectral analysis without translation invariance. Third, the spectral mapping turns QSP into spectral design: the QSP phases can stretch, fold and separate the bands and place the eigenvalues. Finally, the kernel sectors that conventional QSVT usually sets aside carry a topological index, invariant under the choice of QSP phases, local perturbations and gap-preserving deformations, which guarantees topologically protected states. These results unify two quantum walks: Szegedy's walk from quantum algorithms and the split-step walk from condensed matter physics. Their kernel sectors encode the graph's Euler characteristic and the difference of bulk winding numbers, respectively. For the split-step walk, QSP breaks chiral symmetry but not the topological protection, and can move the protected state into a continuous band to form a bound state in the continuum (BIC).

</details>

Abstract: We quantify the extra Bayes risk of locating a quantum change point and estimating its amplitude with one measurement. The joint and marginal optimizations use the full record and the same joint prior, with both parameters unknown. Ideal steps exhibit certified conflict at finite signal strength, including in phase records of sixteen qubits, and recover both informative marginal optima at strong signal. For finite offset prior width, a retained quantum boundary region serves position, and the remaining suffix serves amplitude. Physical recovery holds when strength grows more slowly than the square root of record length. Known smooth rises also admit leading recovery in the coherent experiment as strength grows and width shrinks. For the quintic profile, we establish two residual cost scaling laws. The residual cost is $Θ(s^{-2})$ as $s\to\infty$. Taking the strong signal limit at fixed $w$ yields a limiting cost of $Θ(w^3)$ as $w\to0$. In the resolved local model, quadratic position loss admits a common optimum within the covariant class. These results link onset geometry and decision loss to Bayesian measurement compatibility in quantum change point estimation.

Abstract: Spectral constraints can alter the observable manifestation of quantum geometry without changing the local eigenspace. Relativistic electron vortices provide a sharp example: the spin orbit interaction redistributes spin and orbital angular momentum, yet its magnetic contribution differs between localized three-dimensional wave packets and stationary monochromatic beams. We resolve this discrepancy by showing that fixing the energy replaces ambient momentum derivatives in position-weighted current moments by shell-tangent derivatives and derive a Hermitian selection rule for first-order observables in momentum-dependent internal subspaces. Its extension-independent form applies beyond Dirac systems, including a broad class of antisymmetric first moments of scalar-charge velocity currents. For the free positive-energy Dirac doublet, the magnetic correction is an exact non-Abelian Berry-curvature contraction with a pointwise saturable quantum-metric bound. Changing the relative phase between the two $σ_z$ spinor components switches this correction from zero in a conserved transverse-helicity sector to a finite value in a radial coherent superposition. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子态可被经典高效描述，即使最大纠缠也无量子优势，因此量子优势需要magic这一超越纠缠的量子资源。然而尚缺乏对一般多体态magic程度的系统评估工具，也不清楚magic在热化和动力学演化下的命运。

Method: 发展一套评估一般多体态magic程度（偏离Pauli算子乘积稳定子态的程度）的通用框架；将其应用于横场Ising模型、一般自旋链、蜂窝格Kitaev量子自旋液体（关注子区域形状依赖的阈值），并研究量子淬火后子区域magic的平衡化，以及引入非局域magic概念刻画随空间分离的magic衰减；最后设计与中子散射对应的实验见证方案。

Result: 在横场Ising模型中，magic随温度发生"热突然死亡"，阈值随系统尺寸饱和，且发生在纠缠死亡之后；一般自旋链和Kitaev自旋液体中得到类似结论，后者阈值对子区域形状有复杂依赖。量子淬火下，一般自旋链子区域magic通常先于纠缠死亡，与热轨迹相反，可用非magic态的几何结构解释。空间分离情形下非局域magic亦表现出突然死亡。并给出用中子散射观测frame-robust magic体积律的方法。

Conclusion: magic作为独立于纠缠的量子资源，在不同物理轨迹下呈现出与纠缠不同的"突然死亡"行为，其阈值与几何结构密切相关；该框架可统一刻画多体magic，并提供在量子材料中通过中子散射进行实验见证的可行途径。

Abstract: Magic, a quantum resource distinct from entanglement, measures departure from states stabilized by products of Pauli operators. Such stabilizer states can be maximally entangled yet admit efficient classical description: quantum advantage requires magic. We develop a powerful framework to evaluate the degree of magic in generic many-body states. We use it to study the fate of magic under thermal and dynamical evolution. In the transverse field Ising model, we find a thermal sudden death of magic that saturates with system size, and occurs after the death of entanglement. We reach similar conclusions in generic spin chains, and in the Kitaev quantum spin liquid on the honeycomb lattice, where the threshold shows an intricate dependence on subregion shape. We then study how magic equilibrates after a quantum quench. In quenches of generic spin chains, the magic of a subregion typically dies before entanglement, contrary to thermal trajectories. An explanation is given in terms of the geometry of non-magical states. As a third type of trajectory, we examine the fate of magic with separation with an appropriate notion of non-local magic and establish a sudden death. Finally, we show how to experimentally witness a volume law of frame-robust magic in quantum materials by using neutron scattering.

</details>

Abstract: A uniformly accelerated observer perceives the Minkowski vacuum as a thermal bath, so acceleration acts as a noise channel on correlations shared with an inertial partner. Using negativity, mutual information, and all six pairwise quantum discords, we find that the entanglement and discord between the inertial observer and the accessible mode vanish together at infinite acceleration, leaving only one bit of classical correlation. These results show that the fate of quantum correlations in noninertial frames depends on the structure of the Minkowski-Rindler map.

Abstract: Understanding how local perturbations influence global quantum dynamics is important for analyzing and designing large-scale quantum systems. Existing quantum centrality measures often rely on static correlations or entanglement and do not directly capture perturbation propagation under unitary evolution. We formulate controllability allocation scoring for quantum input-output dynamics governed by the Schrodinger equation. Using the multiobservable quantum-control Gramian, we introduce virtual local Hamiltonian perturbations along a nominal trajectory and decompose the terminal-output response into channel-wise Gramian contributions. A simplex-constrained log-determinant optimization combines these contributions to define a volumetric controllability allocation score (V-CAS), specifying an optimal allocation of virtual input intensities among local Hamiltonian perturbation directions. The score measures their cooperative dynamical influence and depends on the output and trajectory. We characterize the output Gramian's range and minimum-energy properties and derive a drift-independent sufficient condition ensuring score uniqueness for almost every terminal time at which the problem is feasible. For quantum many-body systems, we develop a matrix-product-state and time-evolving-block-decimation implementation that avoids explicitly constructing exponentially large state-transition matrices. We also derive an error bound propagating Trotter and MPS-truncation errors to the discrete output Gramian. Numerical experiments show that the score captures finite-time influence patterns distinct from those identified by static correlation- and entanglement-based measures and can inform target-independent control-channel preselection.

</details>

Abstract: Electrically spin-injected ensembles of InGaAs/GaAs quantum dots emit circularly polarized single photons, with the emission helicity determined by the spin polarization of the injected electrons. The observations indicate that spin injection controls not only the helicity of the emitted photons but also higher-order temporal correlations of the emission. The behavior is phenomenologically captured by inserting both a variable excitation mechanism and a set of multi-emitter collapse operators within a stochastic wave function evolution. The platform thus opens a route to electrically driven, spin-controlled single and multi-photon Fock-state sources and provides a testbed for spin-conditioned multi-emitter dynamics in solid-state quantum light sources.

Abstract: Non-Markovianity is commonly characterized through information-theoretic signatures of memory. In a complementary continuous-time setting, we show using random telegraph dephasing that memory accumulated during the evolution can enhance capacity retention relative to a matched Markovian reference under the same control class. These results identify non-Markovian memory as an operational resource for quantum-battery capacity.

Abstract: Efficient control of quantum devices requires the knowledge of their environmental noise, yet the commonly measured power spectrum does not fully characterize non-Gaussian fluctuations of realistic environments. Here we show that exceptional points (EPs) in the noise-averaged dephasing response can expose observable signatures of non-Gaussian statistics, distinguishing environments with identical lower-order statistics. We also introduce a theoretical model that describes dephasing environments in terms of identical independent noise sources while preserving the power spectrum. Given a calibrated single-source model, the largest EP order extracted from the measured dephasing response determines the number of sources and predicts the higher-order statistics of the combined noise. We illustrate an atomtronic readout that maps distinct EP signatures onto an evolving ensemble-averaged atomic interference pattern. Our results connect non-Hermitian dynamics to environmental noise statistics, providing a route to characterize noise in quantum devices used in computing, metrology, and precision measurement.

</details>

Abstract: Coherent errors leave stabiliser-code-encoded magic states in superpositions of syndrome sectors. The removed magic is positive for every non-Clifford error: adding a non-stabiliser state to any state multiplies one plus the robustness by a (tightly- bounded) factor above one. Abstract: Grover-Mixer QAOA (GM-QAOA) is a promising quantum framework for constrained combinatorial optimization. First, a parameterized quantum circuit is trained to prepare this superposition, using an entropy-based cost function that favors a uniform distribution over feasible bitstrings. The trained circuit is then embedded in GM-QAOA, where it provides both the initial state and, together with its inverse, yields the Grover mixer. The resulting method is evaluated on two representative benchmark problems: the Maximum Weight Independent Set (MWIS) problem and the Multidimensional Knapsack Problem (MKP). While the results demonstrate consistent improvements over standard QAOA for MWIS, only marginal gains are observed for MKP, with the advantage diminishing as the problem size increases. The reduced performance on MKP reflects the growing difficulty of training the variational mixer, likely due to increasingly challenging optimization landscapes. Overall, these findings highlight both the potential of variational Grover mixers and the trade-off between their performance gains and the additional resources required for their training.
