TL;DR: 提出受绝热量子计算启发的VQA混合量子训练框架，用于纠缠检测；将优化问题建模为QUBO以借助量子退火，并用参数空间离散化与分层分割方案，初步结果与经典神经网络及常规VQA训练方法对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠检测是量子信息科学中的基础性问题，兼具经典与量子两类研究路径；现有VQA训练方法存在优化困难，作者希望借助绝热量子计算/量子退火的能力改进VQA训练，并借此激发学界对该方法有效性与适用性的讨论。

Method: 提出一种绝热启发的VQA优化策略：对参数空间进行离散化，并结合分层分割（hierarchical segmentation）方案；将优化问题形式化为二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）模型，以便利用量子退火求解；以纠缠检测任务作为验证场景。

Result: 给出初步结果，并与经典神经网络和传统VQA训练技术进行对比；摘要未给出具体定量结论，仅表明用于引发后续研究与讨论。

Conclusion: 该绝热启发式、QUBO化的混合量子训练框架为VQA训练与纠缠检测提供了新思路，其有效性与适用性尚需进一步实验验证与学界讨论。

Abstract: This paper introduces a hybrid quantum framework for training Variational Quantum Algorithms (VQAs) aimed at addressing the fundamental problem of entanglement detection using adiabatic quantum computing. This problem is of foundational significance in quantum information science and has been approached through both classical and quantum methodologies. We propose an adiabatic-inspired optimization strategy for training VQAs, which combines a discretization of the parameter space with a hierarchical segmentation scheme. The optimization problem is formulated as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) model to leverage the capabilities of quantum annealing. Preliminary results are compared against classical neural networks and conventional VQA training techniques. The findings aim to stimulate further investigation and discussion within the scientific community regarding the efficacy and applicability of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [121] [Probabilistic imaginary-time evolution via compiled diagonal representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39600)
*Hengyue Li,Huazhe Lou,Yang Li,Mingyu Huang,Yusheng Yang,Jianyong Sheng,Jian Sun,Shenggang Ying*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于编译式近似对角表示的概率虚时演化（PITE）方案：离线优化基变换，在线电路结构与门数不随虚时变化，且同一编译表示可跨输入态与演化时间复用，数值测试显示在同等精度阈值下在线开销低于逐项 Pauli 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚时演化（如基于 Pauli 分解的逐项方法）在线成本随虚时与哈密顿量项数增长，电路深度大、门数多，重复调用时开销难以摊薄，亟需降低在线资源消耗并提升复用性。

Method: 1) 离线编译哈密顿量的近似对角表示，优化基变换以获得稀疏对角形式；2) 在该表示下设计概率虚时演化（PITE）在线电路，通过非幺正操作的测量-后选择实现虚时传播；3) 推导成功概率增强的判据条件；4) 以数值实验与逐项 Pauli 方法在共同精度阈值下对比在线成本，并分析近似误差与离线制备成本对总体收益的影响。

Result: 在线电路结构与每次尝试的门数与虚时无关；同一编译表示可复用于不同输入态和不同演化时间；给出成功概率增强的条件；数值测试表明在相同精度阈值下在线成本低于逐项 Pauli 方法；并定量考察了近似误差与离线制备开销对净收益的权衡。

Conclusion: 编译式近似对角化表示可将 PITE 的高成本部分转移至离线阶段，实现在线开销与虚时解耦及跨任务复用，总体收益取决于近似精度与离线成本的平衡，为资源受限场景下的虚时模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce a probabilistic imaginary-time evolution scheme based on a compiled approximate diagonal representation of the Hamiltonian. A basis transformation is optimized offline. For a fixed compiled representation, the online circuit structure and gate count per attempt are independent of the imaginary time, and the same representation can be reused across different input states and evolution times. We derive a condition for enhanced success probability. Numerical tests show reduced online cost relative to termwise Pauli-based methods at a common accuracy threshold. We also examine how approximation errors and offline preparation costs affect the overall benefit.

</details>


### [122] [Detection and Inhibition of Decoherence Through Collisions: A dynamical approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39657)
*Sagnik Chakraborty,Srijon Ghosh,Federico Amato,Gniewomir Sarbicki,Dariusz Chruściński,Rosario Lo Franco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于碰撞模型提出两种"与噪声无关"的量子态保护协议：其一在无需完整噪声先验知识下保护量子比特态，其二在类似条件下保护逻辑量子比特，可稳健守护任意单比特态与纠缠态并支持任意比特数，提升量子架构的可靠性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统本质脆弱、易受环境噪声侵扰，量子态保持是实现实用量子技术的核心难题；现有保护方案通常依赖对噪声动力学的完整先验知识，限制了实际适用性。

Method: 采用碰撞模型（collisional model）框架，设计两种对噪声类型不作假设（noise-agnostic）的量子态保护协议：协议一对单量子比特态在缺乏噪声动力学完整先验信息时实现保护；协议二在类似条件下保护编码后的逻辑量子比特。

Result: 两种协议可对任意单比特态与纠缠态在一般噪声过程下提供稳健保护；并具备保护任意数量量子比特态的能力，从而增强新兴量子架构的可靠性、鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了所提框架作为实用量子信息处理中颇具前景的量子态保护途径。

Abstract: Quantum systems are intrinsically fragile and highly vulnerable to environmental noise, making the preservation of quantum states one of the fundamental challenges in realizing practical quantum technologies. We propose two noise-agnostic quantum state protection protocols based on a collisional model. The first protocol preserves quantum states of qubits without requiring complete prior knowledge of the underlying noise dynamics, and the second even protects a logical qubit under similar circumstances. These protocols provide robust protection for arbitrary single-qubit and entangled states against general noise processes. Furthermore, their capability to safeguard quantum states with an arbitrary number of qubits enhances the reliability, robustness, and scalability of emerging quantum architectures. These results establish the proposed framework as a promising approach for practical quantum information processing.

</details>


### [123] [Error-Corrected Quantum Optimization via Rydberg Atom Array Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39670)
*Andrew Byun,Berend Klaver,Arinjoy De,Pedro Ildefonso,Davit Khachatryan,Alexei Bylinskii,Michael Schuler,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Rydberg原子嵌入中的"额外"原子（gadget/超原子）并非开销，而是天然的经典纠错码；据此提出Cluster解码器，在QuEra Aquila上对107原子系统恢复精确优化解，平均成功率≳8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rydberg原子量子优化器原生只解决几何图上的最大独立集问题；处理一般组合优化问题需借助gadget嵌入以克服有限范围相互作用，而这不可避免地引入辅助原子，通常被视为资源开销。作者意图证明这种"开销"实为可利用的内禀纠错资源。

Method: 1）证明基于Parity Architecture的Rydberg嵌入天然构成一种经典纠错码，并推广至其他基于Rydberg阻塞的嵌入；2）利用超原子（superatom）子结构中的精确Rydberg激发数约束作为零硬件成本的局部错误综合征，形成严格非线性码；3）设计精确定制的Cluster解码器，结合局部约束与gadget结构纠正bit-flip错误；4）在不同布局几何下做数值模拟并计算错误阈值；5）在QuEra Aquila QPU上进行实验验证。

Result: 数值上，Cluster解码器在多种布局几何下取得错误阈值 p_th ≳ 26%；实验上，在QuEra Aquila QPU上对多达107个原子的系统，平均精确解恢复成功率≳8%，即使裸硬件与标准贪心后处理均找不到解。

Conclusion: Rydberg原子阵列嵌入不仅是优化问题的硬件映射，而是一种协同设计的量子优化架构——嵌入、计算与纠错源自同一物理结构，"辅助"原子从冗余转变为纠错资源，显著提升优化解的成功率。

Abstract: Rydberg atom quantum optimizers naturally encode the maximum independent set problem on geometric graphs. However, to solve general combinatorial optimization problems on more complex topologies, an embedding is required to overcome the limited-range native interactions. Such embeddings use gadgets, specific arrangements of atoms, which inevitably introduce ancillary atoms. Here, we show that this apparent overhead instead provides an intrinsic resource for error correction. Specifically, we demonstrate that the Parity Architecture-based Rydberg embedding naturally forms a classical error-correcting code, a principle that extends to other Rydberg blockade-based embeddings. Within superatom substructures, exact Rydberg-excitation-number constraints provide local error syndromes at no additional hardware cost and give rise to a strictly nonlinear code. To correct bit-flip errors, we introduce the precisely tailored Cluster decoder, which exploits these local constraints and the underlying gadget structure. Numerically, this approach achieves an error threshold of $p_{\text{th}}\gtrsim26 \%$ across different layout geometries. Experimentally, on the QuEra Aquila QPU, the Cluster decoder recovers exact optimization solutions with an average success rate of $\gtrsim8\%$ for systems of up to 107 atoms, even when bare hardware and standard greedy post-processing find none. A Rydberg atom array embedding is therefore not merely a hardware mapping of an optimization problem, but a co-designed architecture for quantum optimization in which embedding, computation, and error correction emerge from the same physical structure.

</details>


### [124] [Optimized Dynamical Decoupling for Spin Ensembles with Large Inhomogeneities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39693)
*Rajan Paul,Genko Genov,Oliver Schmid,Shinobu Onoda,Yutaka Kobayashi,Junichi Isoya,Fedor Jelezko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作系统比较了大量动态解耦(DD)协议在常温NV色心自旋系综中的相干保护与量子传感性能，发现采用对幅度和频率非均匀性鲁棒的快速绝热通道(RAP)脉冲并结合相位变化的最优序列，相较标准矩形脉冲的XY8可将存储时间提升约一个数量级并改善信号对比度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋系综在量子传感与量子技术中前景广阔，但其相干性保护困难；常规DD序列在系综中因幅度/频率非均匀性与控制误差而性能下降，需要寻找更鲁棒的DD方案。

Method: 在常温下的负电荷NV色心系综中，对大量DD协议进行对比实验，评估其在相干保护与量子传感中的表现，并据此识别出基于快速绝热通道(RAP)脉冲（对幅度与频率非均匀性鲁棒）并结合其相位变化的最优序列。

Result: 与广泛使用的、基于标准矩形脉冲的XY8相比，最优序列实现了更高的信号对比度和约一个数量级的存储时间提升；并将其应用于弱振荡场的改进传感。

Conclusion: 基于RAP脉冲加相位变化的最优DD协议能显著提升自旋系综的相干保护与传感性能；虽然演示基于金刚石NV色心，但该协议可推广至其他自旋系综及具有高幅度/频率非均匀性的系统。

Abstract: Spin ensembles show increasing promise for highly efficient quantum sensing and quantum technologies. However, protecting their coherent properties remains a significant challenge. Dynamical decoupling (DD) typically employs a sequence of fast pulses that aim to reduce the effect of unwanted system-environment interactions. However, the performance of the DD sequences typically suffers in spin ensembles, e.g., due to amplitude and frequency inhomogeneities and control errors. In this work, we compare the performance of an extensive number of DD protocols for coherence protection and quantum sensing in an ensemble of negatively charged NV centers under ambient conditions. We identify the optimal approach that uses rapid adiabatic passage pulses, which are robust to amplitude and frequency inhomogeneity, in combination with the variation of their phases. We experimentally demonstrate an improved signal contrast and an order-of-magnitude increase in memory time with the optimal sequence compared to the widely used XY8 with standard rectangular pulses. We also apply our approach for improved sensing of a weak oscillating field. While our particular demonstration is with NV centers in diamond, the optimized protocol is generally applicable to other spin ensembles and to systems with high amplitude and/or frequency inhomogeneities.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum Query Complexity of Tournament Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39702)
*Pragya Arora,Udit Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究锦标赛解（Copeland集、uncovered集、top cycle、Condorcet赢家、strong king、最大出度顶点）的量子查询复杂度，证明top cycle可达Õ(N)、Condorcet赢家Õ(√N)、strong king与最大出度顶点均为Θ(N^{3/2})，并给出最大出度顶点Θ(N^{3/2})下界的自包含新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情形下各锦标赛解均需Θ(N²)查询，图像"一致"而无区分度；作者希望在量子查询模型下揭示不同锦标赛解之间的复杂度层次差异，并填补部分解（尤其top cycle）量子复杂度的空白。

Method: 采用量子查询模型，每条比较结果需通过查询获得。上界：对top cycle先求哈密顿路径，再用分治法定位构成top cycle的前缀；Condorcet赢家利用量子搜索/计数加速。下界：借助Ambainis敌手方法，将计数问题归约为锦标赛问题，得到strong king与最大出度顶点的Θ(N^{3/2})界，并重新给出Gilani等人结果的自包含证明。

Result: top cycle的量子查询复杂度为Õ(N)；Condorcet赢家为Õ(√N)；strong king为Θ(N^{3/2})，与最大出度顶点相同；并给出最大出度顶点Θ(N^{3/2})下界的独立自包含证明。

Conclusion: 量子查询显著降低了部分锦标赛解的复杂度，并使不同解之间呈现出经典情形所没有的复杂度分层；敌手方法与计数归约为锦标赛问题下界提供了统一框架。

Abstract: A tournament solution records a set of winners from a tournament, that is, from the outcomes of all pairwise comparisons among $N$ alternatives. We study the quantum query complexity of computing tournament solutions when the outcome of each comparison has to be learned by a query. Classically, the picture is uniform: the Copeland set, the uncovered set and the top cycle all require $Θ(N^2)$ queries, and so does finding a single vertex of maximum out-degree. We show that the quantum query complexity of the top cycle can be computed with $\tildeΘ(N)$ queries, and a Condorcet winner can be found with $\tilde Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries. Finding a strong king requires $Θ(N^{3/2})$ queries, the same as finding a vertex of maximum out-degree. We also give a self-contained alternative proof of the known $Θ(N^{3/2})$ bound for finding a vertex of maximum degree, established by Gilani, Mande, Patro, and Raja. Our lower bounds reduce counting problems to tournament problems using Ambainis's adversary method. For the top cycle, we find a Hamiltonian path and locate the prefix forming the top cycle by divide and conquer.

</details>


### [126] [Maximum-Entropy Ensemble of Quantum Subsystems with a Fixed Average State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39712)
*Charlie Shakeshaft,Christopher David White,Harry J. D. Miller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明固定系综平均下最大熵混合态的本征值密度渐近收敛于关联Wishart系综(CWE)，给出自由乘性卷积公式，并应用于横场Ising模型与随机POVM测量后的纠缠熵Page曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Haar随机二分态约化密度矩阵对应固定迹Wishart系综，其本征值密度服从Marchenko–Pastur律；作者希望为受固定系综平均约束的最大熵混合态寻找类似的普适本征值密度形式，并刻画测量诱导的混合态系综及其纠缠性质。

Method: 将最大熵混合态的本征值密度与CWE联系，用自由乘性卷积求密度；对含热平均态的TFIM导出对MP律的高温领头阶修正；分析纯化态上的随机POVM测量，论证所得混合态系综渐近等价于CWE并给出数值验证；计算测量后系统与辅助体的纠缠熵及有限温度Page曲线。

Result: 最大熵混合态本征值密度渐近收敛于CWE密度，可由自由乘性卷积公式给出；TFIM热态得到MP律的高温领头阶修正；随机POVM测量后的系综渐近等价于CWE并有数值证据；给出TFIM中测量后平均纠缠熵的有限温度Page曲线。

Conclusion: 固定系综平均下的最大熵混合态具有类Wishart普适本征值密度，随机POVM测量可映射为CWE，从而统一刻画热态与测量诱导混合态的谱与纠缠熵。

Abstract: The reduced density matrices of Haar-random bipartite states form a fixed-trace version of the Wishart ensemble of random matrices, with an asymptotic eigenvalue density given by the Marchenko--Pastur (MP) law. We establish a similar universal form for the maximum-entropy distribution of mixed states subject to a fixed ensemble average, arguing that its eigenvalue density converges asymptotically to that of the correlated Wishart ensemble (CWE). The density is thus obtained by a free multiplicative convolution formula, which we apply to the transverse-field Ising model (TFIM) with a thermal average state to derive a high-temperature leading-order correction to the MP law. We then consider measurement by a random POVM on the purification of the mixed reference state. We show that the resulting mixed-state ensemble is asymptotically equivalent to the CWE, and provide confirming numerical evidence. We return to the TFIM example to compute the entanglement of the system with the ancilla after measurement, and give finite-temperature Page curves for the average entanglement entropies.

</details>


### [127] [Non-Asymptotic Bounds via the Nussbaum-Szkoła Mapping for Quantum Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39784)
*Jorge Lizarribar-Carrillo,Gonzalo Vazquez-Vilar,Tobias Koch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于 Nussbaum-Szkoła 映射为非对称量子假设检验提出新的上下界，统一恢复大偏差、中偏差、小偏差等渐近区间的可达性与逆定理结果，并在小码长下准确逼近最优量子误差权衡函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子假设检验界要么依赖技术复杂的信息谱论证，要么固定前因子、在非渐近区间适用性受限；且不同渐近区间（大/中/小偏差）通常需分别处理，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 利用 Nussbaum-Szkoła 映射将量子态假设检验问题转化为经典分布问题，据此构造统一的上下界，从而可部分直接借用经典结果；并将所得界与基于保真度和信息谱方法的现有界进行数值比较。

Result: 所得上下界统一覆盖全部主要渐近区间（大偏差、中偏差、小偏差）的逆定理与可达性结果；在小分组长度下对最优量子误差权衡函数给出准确近似；数值比较显示其紧致性优于基于保真度与信息谱的现有界。

Conclusion: Nussbaum-Szkoła 映射为量子假设检验提供了一个统一且紧致的界框架，兼具理论统一性与非渐近实用性。

Abstract: Quantum hypothesis testing concerns the discrimination between quantum states. This paper introduces novel lower and upper bounds for asymmetric quantum hypothesis testing based on the Nussbaum-Szkoła mapping. Together, these bounds provide a unified recovery of both converse and achievability results across all major asymptotic regimes, including large-, moderate-, and small-deviations. Unlike existing bounds, which either rely on technically involved information-spectrum arguments or suffer from fixed prefactors and limited applicability in the non-asymptotic regime, the proposed bounds arise from a unified framework and enable, in some cases, the direct use of classical results. It is further demonstrated that these bounds provide accurate approximations to the optimal quantum error trade-off function at small blocklengths. Numerical comparisons with existing bounds, including those based on fidelity and information spectrum methods, highlight their improved tightness.

</details>


### [128] [Digital-Analog Quantum Neural Network with Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39847)
*Zongyuan Ge,Daili Li,Zewen Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在数字-模拟量子神经网络（DA-QNN）框架中引入幂律长程Ising相互作用可提升混合量子算法效率，长程拟设在更少层数或更短演化时间下即可达到短程拟设的性能，并在无监督相检测中与短程拟设形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单比特数字操作与模拟量子演化相结合的协议可绕开大量两比特纠缠门，是有前景的变分算法与混合量子模拟途径；本文旨在探究模拟块中相互作用范围（长程耦合）对这类混合算法效率、表达力与可训练性的影响。

Method: 以DA-QNN为代表框架，在模拟块中采用幂律相互作用Ising模型产生纠缠，系统分析横场与演化时间的影响，核心考察相互作用范围如何支配拟设的表达力与可训练性；在有监督图像分类、多体相检测及无监督相检测任务上进行基准测试。

Result: 有监督图像分类与多体相检测中，长程拟设以更少DA-QNN层数或更短演化时间匹配短程拟设性能，归因于非局域耦合带来的快速信息传播；该资源削减降低了经典优化开销并简化实验实现。无监督相检测中长程拟设并非在所有区间占优，但两种拟设提供互补特征，可共同重构完整相图。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用平台是推进实用化、大规模数字-模拟量子计算的重要资源。

Abstract: Protocols combining single-qubit digital operations with analog quantum evolutions bypass large numbers of two-qubit entangling gates, offering a promising approach to variational algorithms and hybrid quantum simulation. Focusing on the digital-analog quantum neural network (DA-QNN) as a representative framework, this work demonstrates that incorporating long-range interactions enhances the efficiency of such hybrid algorithms. Within the analog blocks, an Ising model with power-law interactions is employed to generate entanglement, and this protocol is benchmarked across supervised and unsupervised learning tasks. Beyond discussing the effects of the transverse field and evolution time, the primary analysis focuses on how the interaction range governs the expressibility and trainability of the ansatz. For supervised image classification and many-body phase detection, long-range ansatzes match the performance of their short-range counterparts with fewer DA-QNN layers or shorter evolution times, owing to the rapid information propagation across the system enabled by nonlocal couplings. This resource reduction alleviates the classical optimization overhead and eases experimental implementation. In unsupervised phase detection, although the long-range ansatz does not outperform its short-range counterpart in every regime, the two ansatzes provide complementary signatures that jointly reconstruct the complete phase diagram. These findings establish long-range interacting platforms as a compelling resource for advancing practical, large-scale digital-analog quantum computing.

</details>


### [129] [Distributed Inner Product Estimation of Continuous-Variable Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39912)
*Kun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对能量受限的连续变量量子态，提出三种连续变量分布式内积估计（CV-DIPE）协议，给出截断误差界与最坏情况样本复杂度保证，并比较三类协议的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式玻色与量子光学系统制备的未知态难以用高效经典描述刻画，也无法进行联合测量，因此需要仅凭本地测量数据的内积估计方法来支持基准测试。

Method: 针对自然类能量受限连续变量态（含纠缠态与非高斯态），设计了基于协同位移宇称测量、协同Haar测量和独立零差阴影（homodyne shadows）的三种CV-DIPE协议，并推导任意多模输入下的截断误差界与最坏情况样本复杂度。

Result: 建立了精确内积的截断误差界与最坏情况样本复杂度保证；误差界主导项表明协同Haar测量最优，但其全局实现实验难度较大。

Conclusion: 三种协议均能从本地测量数据给出分布式连续变量系统内积的认证估计，在性能与实验可行性之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Continuous-variable distributed inner product estimation (CV-DIPE) compares unknown states prepared by distant bosonic and quantum optical systems. It supports benchmarking without efficient classical descriptions or joint measurements. We develop three CV-DIPE protocols for the natural class of energy-constrained continuous-variable quantum states, based on coordinated displaced parity, coordinated Haar measurements, and independent homodyne shadows. For arbitrary multimode inputs in this class, including entangled and non-Gaussian states, we establish truncation error bounds and worst-case sample complexity guarantees for the exact inner product. The leading terms of these bounds favor coordinated Haar measurements, whose global implementation is experimentally challenging. The three protocols provide certified inner product estimates for distributed continuous-variable systems from local measurement data.

</details>


### [130] [Bath-state (In)dependence of Non-Markovianity Measures in Quadratic Open Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39970)
*Mirko Consiglio,Ilia Khomchenko,Tony J. G. Apollaro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究费米子二次开放量子系统中各非马尔可夫性度量的浴态依赖性，证明BLP与LPP度量与浴态无关，而基于纠缠、可除性与熵的度量依赖温度与化学势，并在自由费米子链模型中数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当系统与环境时间尺度接近时马尔可夫近似失效，需用多种非马尔可夫性度量刻画，而这些度量预期随环境参数变化；本文旨在厘清各度量对浴态（温度、化学势）的依赖关系。

Method: 选取费米子二次开放量子系统，解析分析BLP、LPP、RHP（基于纠缠与基于可除性）及MSV熵度量的浴态依赖性；将浴建模为自由费米子链，推导单费米格点的精确时间局域主方程并进行数值计算。

Result: BLP与LPP度量与浴态无关；RHP（纠缠型与可除性型）及MSV熵度量通过温度和化学势敏感依赖储层态。数值结果证实上述结论，且BLP与LPP仅在束缚态阈值处严格为零，此时浴敏感度量仍为有限值。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫性度量的浴态依赖性并非普适：不同度量对浴态信息敏感程度不同，选用度量时须考虑其是否反映环境状态；束缚态阈值处BLP/LPP与浴敏感度量的差异揭示了度量间本质区别。

Abstract: Open quantum system dynamics fail to fulfill the conditions of the Markov approximation when the environment and system timescales are close to each other. Such behavior of these systems can be characterized by several non-Markovianity measures, which are expected to vary with the environment parameters. In this work, we consider a fermionic quadratic open quantum system, establishing bath-state independence of the Breuer--Laine--Piilo and the Lorenzo--Plastina--Paternostro non-Markovianity measures. However, our analytical results reveal that the entanglement-based and divisibility-based Rivas--Huelga--Plenio measures, as well as the entropic Megier--Smirne--Vacchini measure, remain sensitive to the reservoir state, through both its temperature and its chemical potential. Modeling the reservoir as a free-fermionic chain, we derive an exact time-local master equation for a single fermionic site in contact with it. Our numerical results for this model confirm these findings and show that the BLP and LPP measures vanish identically only on the bound-state threshold, where the bath-sensitive measures nevertheless remain finite.

</details>


### [131] [Identifying Logical Operations from Syndrome Histories for qLDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40052)
*Yizhuo Tan,Shifan Xu,Yongshan Ding,Jakub Szefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究仅凭综合历史（syndrome histories）及经典测量结果识别容错量子计算中所执行的逻辑操作，提出精确推断方法与可扩展近似方法（可处理未知电路故障与理想测量随机性），在[[18,4,2]]与[[72,12,6]]双变量自行车码上验证：在电路级噪声、10^-5至10^-3错误率下，单条逻辑指令识别准确率超94%，基于自同构的逻辑电路超99%，三类指令混合电路达78.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 综合历史通常仅用于检错纠错，但其可能泄露所执行逻辑操作的信息；作者希望将综合的作用从错误解码扩展到“逻辑操作/电路识别”，并揭示这种信息泄露的潜在安全含义。

Method: 建立仅用综合历史或结合其他经典测量结果的逻辑操作识别框架：开发精确推断方法及可扩展近似推断，二者均考虑未知电路故障与理想测量随机性；在[[18,4,2]]和[[72,12,6]] BB码上，针对自同构、逻辑Pauli测量、T注入及三者组合的逻辑电路，在多种噪声模型与错误率下评估识别性能，并分析由测量信息不足/不可区分、故障时刻及近似推断引起的识别歧义。

Result: 电路级噪声、错误率10^-5~10^-3下单条逻辑指令识别准确率>94%；基于自同构的逻辑电路>99%；三类指令组合电路达78.32%；并定位了识别歧义的主要来源（测量信息不足或不可区分、故障时序、近似推断误差）。

Conclusion: 综合历史可超越错误解码，用于识别容错量子计算中实际执行的逻辑操作与电路；这也意味着对综合历史及相关经典测量结果的访问可能无意中泄露所执行计算的信息，具有安全性/隐私层面的启示。

Abstract: Syndrome histories are normally used to detect and correct errors in fault-tolerant quantum computation, but they can also reveal information about the logical operations executed during computation. We study logical-operation identification using syndrome histories alone or together with other classical measurement outcomes. We develop an exact inference method and a scalable approximation that account for unknown circuit faults and ideal measurement randomness. We evaluate automorphisms, logical Pauli measurements, T injections, and logical circuits combining these instructions on \([[18,4,2]]\) and \([[72,12,6]]\) bivariate bicycle (BB) codes under multiple noise models and error rates. A single logical instruction can be identified with over 94\% accuracy under circuit-level noise with error rates between \(10^ {-5} \) and \(10^{-3}\). We also achieve over 99\% accuracy for automorphism-based logical circuits and 78.32\% for logical circuits combining all three instructions. We further analyze identification ambiguities arising from insufficient or indistinguishable measurement information, fault timing, and approximation inference. Together, these results extend the role of syndromes beyond error decoding to identifying executed logical operations and circuits during fault-tolerant quantum computation. They also suggest that access to syndrome histories and related classical measurement outcomes may unintentionally reveal information about the executed computation.

</details>


### [132] [Dissipative controller as a hidden source of errors in quantum gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40072)
*Serhat C. Kadıoğlu,Saulo V. Moreira,Mark T. Mitchison,Anthony Kiely*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子控制中控制场的涨落噪声导致的错误与宏观控制器产生的熵（耗散）之间存在权衡关系：在快速、高保真控制这一实验最相关区域，抑制控制错误通常需以宏观耗散为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统一般由经典器件控制，而经典器件必然向环境耗散能量；由涨落-耗散定理，这会引入控制场的噪声。作者希望定量厘清“控制错误”与“宏观控制器熵产生”之间的关系，并给出在具体最小模型下的刻画。

Method: 构建一个具体的最小模型，在快速控制与高保真的实验相关参数区间内分析错误与耗散的关系；并通过微扰计算验证：对简单门实现计算门保真度，以及在Landau-Zener模型中借助“绝热捷径”实现态转移并计算其保真度。

Result: 在所考察的区域内发现耗散与控制错误之间的权衡（tradeoff），且该权衡在门保真度与Landau-Zener绝热捷径态转移保真度的微扰计算中均得到体现。

Conclusion: 结果定量表明：量子控制中的错误抑制通常伴随宏观耗散，而这种耗散是抑制经典控制器涨落所必需的。

Abstract: Quantum systems are typically controlled by classical devices that necessarily dissipate energy to the surrounding environment. By the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, this implies noise in the control field produced. We investigate the relationship between the resulting errors and the entropy produced by the macroscopic controller, working in the context of a concrete minimal model. In the most experimentally relevant regime of fast control and high fidelity, we find a tradeoff between dissipation and control errors. These findings are exemplified by perturbative calculations of the gate fidelity for simple gate implementations and of the fidelity of state transfer via shortcuts to adiabaticity in the Landau-Zener model. Our results quantitatively demonstrate that suppressing errors in quantum control typically comes at the cost of macroscopic dissipation, which is needed to tame fluctuations in the classical controller.

</details>


### [133] [Distributed Inner-Product Estimation for Continuous-Variable Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40141)
*Jun Tang,Congcong Zheng,Xutao Yu,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出仅用本地零差测量与经典通信的连续变量分布式内积估计（CV-DIPE）协议，在平均每模能量有界下给出有限样本保证与所需副本数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式内积估计（DIPE）是量子信息基础任务，但多模连续变量（CV）系统的无限维希尔伯特空间带来额外困难，缺少可实现的测量方案与显式样本复杂度分析。

Method: 在共享相位设置下仅做本地零差测量，配合经典通信；在已知每模平均能量界下推导有限样本置信区间与充分副本数；利用能量约束给出平方反比的截断偏差界，并借助高斯结构将尾部控制强化为指数衰减。

Result: 得到估计完整内积至给定制精度与置信度的显式有限样本保证；高斯结构使误差随精度呈更优标度；对高斯与非高斯态数值模拟，展示截断偏差与统计误差的权衡及其对模数和能量的依赖。

Conclusion: 该协议使相互独立的CV平台能够彼此基准化态制备，即使双方态均无可处理的经典描述。

Abstract: Distributed inner product estimation (DIPE) is a fundamental task in quantum information, aiming to estimate the inner product between two unknown quantum states prepared on distributed quantum platforms. For multimode continuous-variable (CV) systems, the infinite-dimensional Hilbert space poses an additional challenge. In this work, we develop a CV-DIPE protocol using only local homodyne measurements at shared phase settings and classical communication. Under a known bound on the mean energy per mode, we derive explicit finite-sample guarantees and sufficient copy counts for estimating the full inner product to prescribed accuracy and confidence. The energy constraint yields an inverse-square truncation-bias bound, while Gaussian structure strengthens the tail control to exponential decay and improves the resulting accuracy scaling. Numerical simulations with Gaussian and non-Gaussian states illustrate the balance between truncation bias and statistical error and its dependence on mode number and energy. Our results enable independent CV platforms to benchmark their state preparations against one another, even when neither state admits a tractable classical description.

</details>


### [134] [Reversible photorefractive attack in continuous-variable quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40155)
*Junxuan Liu,Yuchao Liu,Qingquan Peng,Piao Tan,Tao Wang,Peng Huang,Anqi Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在系统层面实验证明：对本地本振CV-QKD的铌酸锂IQ调制器施加中等强度蓝光可逆地降低调制方差，据此构造的攻击可造成定标失配、高估信道透过率与低估额外噪声，从而在真实密钥率非正时仍输出正的密钥率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD具有信息论安全性，但器件不完美带来实现层面的漏洞。CV-QKD中发射端漏洞的安全后果缺乏实验证据，尤其是可逆/可恢复型光折变效应能否被用于攻击尚不清楚，因此作者开展系统级实验研究。

Method: 在local-local-oscillator CV-QKD系统中实验研究一种可逆光折变攻击：用中等强度蓝光照射铌酸锂IQ调制器，测量调制方差与估计额外噪声的变化及其撤光后的恢复行为；阐明物理机制，并设计利用"恢复过程"破坏调制定标的攻击；通过接收端测量验证定标失配导致的透过率高估与额外噪声低估，并据此计算密钥率。

Result: 蓝光照射将调制方差压低至稳定值，而估计额外噪声无显著上升；撤光后方差恢复。基于受扰参数的密钥率计算给出正速率，而正确定标下的真实密钥率非正。此外，恢复过程使先前操纵在事后检查中更难被识别。

Conclusion: 可逆光折变响应可被用于破坏CV-QKD调制定标，这是首个系统级实验证据；量子发射端的可逆变化构成实际安全威胁，需要纳入同时考虑光学扰动与随后恢复过程的安全性分析与防护策略。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) offers information-theoretic security, but device imperfections introduce implementation vulnerabilities. Experimental evidence of the security consequences of transmitter vulnerabilities in continuous-variable QKD (CV-QKD) remains limited. Here, we experimentally investigate a reversible photorefractive attack in a local-local-oscillator CV-QKD system. Moderate blue-light illumination reduces the modulation variance of a lithium-niobate IQ modulator to a stable value without appreciably increasing estimated excess noise. The variance recovers after illumination is removed. We elucidate the underlying physical mechanisms and develop an attack that exploits recovery to compromise modulation calibration. Receiver measurements confirm that the resulting calibration mismatch leads to overestimated channel transmittance and underestimated excess noise. Key rate calculations based on measured parameters yield a positive estimated rate when the correctly calibrated rate is nonpositive. Recovery can also make the preceding manipulation harder to identify through later inspection. This work provides the first system-level experimental evidence that a reversible photorefractive response can be exploited to compromise modulation calibration in CV-QKD. These findings highlight reversible changes in quantum transmitters as a practical security concern, motivating security analyses and protection strategies that account for both optical perturbations and subsequent recovery.

</details>


### [135] [Inevitability of the Petz transpose map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40158)
*Jacopo Surace,Chenxu Li,Francesco Buscemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对所有先验与信道定义的量子回溯（retrodiction）规则，若满足对合性、序列与并行复合一致性、以及与酉反转相容，则唯一等于Petz转置映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Bayes规则由先验与随机模型唯一确定回溯条件概率；量子情形下是否存在同样普适且唯一的回溯规则尚不明确，本文旨在给出公理化刻画并解释Petz转置映射在量子理论各领域反复出现的根源。

Method: 在有限维全矩阵代数间的信道框架下，将输入输出空间限制于参考态的支撑；对同时定义于任意先验与信道的回溯指派施加四条要求——对合性、序列复合一致性、并行复合一致性、与酉反转一致——进行公理化推导。

Result: 满足上述要求的回溯指派必与Petz转置映射重合；去掉对合性后，其余要求刻画出一族"双旋转Petz映射"（doubly rotated Petz maps）。概率混合下的仿射性与对参考态的特征平方根依赖均为定理推论而非额外假设。

Conclusion: Petz转置映射是唯一与这些一致性要求相容的Bayes规则量子类比，从而统一解释了其在量子理论中看似不同领域的普遍出现。

Abstract: Bayes' rule is central to classical statistical inference: given a prior and a stochastic model, it determines the corresponding retrodictive conditional probabilities. In quantum theory, however, the question of whether an equally general rule of retrodiction exists and, if so, whether it is uniquely determined remains nontrivial. Here we prove that a retrodiction assignment defined for all priors and channels, which takes each predicted output state back to its prior, must coincide with the Petz transpose map if it is involutive, respects sequential and parallel composition, and agrees with unitary inversion. The theorem holds for channels between finite-dimensional full matrix algebras, with the input and output spaces restricted to the supports of the corresponding reference states. In particular, affinity under probabilistic mixtures and the characteristic square-root dependence on the reference states follow from these requirements rather than being postulated. Without involutivity, the remaining requirements characterize a family of doubly rotated Petz maps. Our result thus explains why the Petz transpose map arises in seemingly different areas of quantum theory: it is the unique quantum analogue of Bayes' rule compatible with these consistency requirements.

</details>


### [136] [Detuning-assisted cavity enhancement in spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40216)
*Subhasish Guha,Sourav Das,Francesco Albarelli,Aiman Khan,Elnaz Darsheshdar,Adam D. Wright,Animesh Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将腔频从跃迁处失谐，可把偶极矩估计精度提升至共振情形上限的约两个数量级，且损耗无法抹除此优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔增强光谱虽被广泛使用，但通常默认腔与跃迁共振为最优。作者质疑这一直觉，探索失谐是否能进一步提升参数估计精度。

Method: 以偶极矩估计为目标参数，对比腔共振与失谐两种配置；分析经典连续波探针与脉冲单光子探针两类情形，并在存在损耗的开放腔模型中求最优失谐与最优极化激元分支；将增强归因于光型与物质型极化激元间的信息重分布。

Result: 失谐相对共振可获得最高约100倍的精度提升（约两数量级），在连续波与单光子脉冲探测下均成立；损耗决定应选取光型还是物质型分支；该增强仍可用常规光电探测与时间相关单光子计数实现。

Conclusion: 共振并非腔增强偶极矩估计的最优条件，失谐通过极化激元信息再分配提供显著增益，且方案在损耗与现有探测技术下可实施，为腔增强精密测量提供了新的优化自由度。

Abstract: Cavity-enhanced spectroscopy is a widely used experimental technique. We show that detuning the cavity frequency from the transition of interest can enhance the precision for dipole moment estimation by up to two orders of magnitude beyond the improvement when the cavity is resonant with the transition. We show this for both classical continuous-wave (CW) and pulsed single-photon probes, even in the presence of losses. We trace this enhancement to a detuning-controlled redistribution of information between the light- and matter-like polaritons, with losses determining the optimal branch. We show that this enhancement remains accessible using conventional photodetection and time-correlated single-photon counting.

</details>


### [137] [Bounds for General $(r,δ)$-Quantum Locally Recoverable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40273)
*Shuowei Ma,Fei Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究一般（无纯性/稳定子假设、恢复块可任意重叠）的$(r,\delta)$-量子局部可恢复码，给出Singleton型上界、纯码参数界扩展与三类达到界的码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有qLRC界多依赖纯性、稳定子结构或恢复块互不相交（partition）假设，缺乏对一般量子子空间与任意重叠恢复块的统一刻画，且纯码界的适用条件受限。

Method: 利用多重擦除恢复的选择引理（selection lemma）推导一般量子子空间的Singleton型上界；通过整数优化给出显式余项修正（最多收紧$\delta-2$）；对纯码采用局部解耦与熵论证，将Galindo等的参数界推广到任意纯量子子空间；并移除若干纯码界的分区假设、优化预算以获进一步余项修正。

Result: $\delta=2$时一般界退化为Golowich–Guruswami的单擦除界；纯码界由含Euclidean/Hermitian对偶的稳定子码推广到任意纯子空间；并分析三类达到界的码族：重叠块且$d=\delta$的纯码（既严格改进纯码界又同时达到纯码界与一般界）、不相交块达到一般界的不纯码、以及重叠块且$d>\delta$的既有纯码族（同时达到所有界）。

Conclusion: 在无纯性/稳定子与任意重叠恢复块的一般框架下建立了更紧的qLRC参数界，统一并强化了现有单擦除界与纯码界，并通过三类极优码族验证了界的紧致性与相容性。

Abstract: We study general $(r,δ)$-quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) without assuming purity or a stabilizer structure, and with arbitrarily overlapping recovery blocks. Using a selection lemma for multiple-erasure recovery, we establish a Singleton-like upper bound for general quantum subspaces. When $δ=2$, this bound reduces to the general single-erasure bound of Golowich and Guruswami. An integer optimization yields an explicit remainder correction that tightens the bound by up to $δ-2$. For pure codes, local decoupling and entropy arguments extend the parameter bound of Galindo et al., originally obtained for pure stabilizer codes constructed from Euclidean or Hermitian dual-containing classical codes, to arbitrary pure quantum subspaces. We also remove the partition assumption from several existing pure-code bounds for disjoint recovery blocks and obtain a further remainder correction by optimizing the budget. Finally, we analyze three families attaining these bounds: pure codes with overlapping recovery blocks and $d=δ$, which exhibit both a strict improvement of the pure-code bound and simultaneous equality in the pure-code and general bounds; impure codes with disjoint recovery blocks attaining the general bound; and an existing family of pure codes with overlapping recovery blocks and $d>δ$, which simultaneously attains all these bounds.

</details>


### [138] [QDMin: Coherent Quantum Minimum Finding for Nuclear Deformation Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40320)
*Abhishek,P. Arumugam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QDMin（Quantum Deformation Minimum Finding）量子算法，用相干集体坐标寄存器和按需基态能量阈值预言机，在参数化哈密顿量族中搜索核集体势能面极小值，避免逐点构建能量景观；四点算例单轮Grover将真实极小值概率从0.25提升至0.921。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定位核集体势能面（collective landscape）极小值通常需要大量相互耦合的多体计算，逐点扫描能量景观代价高昂。

Method: 构建由集体坐标参数化的哈密顿量族，配以相干集体坐标寄存器与按需基态能量阈值预言机（oracle），提供量子最小寻找所需的谓词，无需预先逐点构建能量景观；在schematic pairing-plus-multipole模型上实现，搜索过程中用线路生成的能量估计代替预言机。测试四参数单坐标算例与4×4四极-八极场双坐标扩展算例。

Result: 四参数算例中一轮Grover使真实极小值概率由0.25升至0.921；4×4四极-八极双坐标算例将完整线路扩展到两坐标，但其极小值在零次Grover迭代的轮次中首次被采样；最坏情形分支制备保真度为0.744，低于设定目标。

Conclusion: QDMin在非结构化模型下继承O(√N_q)的候选查询缩放；总体优势取决于多体预言机成本，尚未确立。当前瓶颈是相干基态访问（分支制备保真度不足）。

Abstract: Locating the minimum of a nuclear collective landscape can require many interacting many-body calculations. QDMin (Quantum Deformation Minimum Finding) searches a parameterized Hamiltonian family with a coherent collective-coordinate register and an on-demand ground-energy threshold oracle. The oracle supplies the predicate required for quantum minimum-finding without first constructing the energy landscape point by point. We implement it for a schematic pairing-plus-multipole model using circuit-generated energy estimates during the search. In a four-point calculation, one Grover round raises the probability of the true minimum from 0.25 to 0.921. A $4\times4$ quadrupole--octupole-field calculation extends the complete circuit to two coordinates, although its minimum is first sampled in a round with zero Grover iterations. Its worst-case branch-preparation fidelity of 0.744 falls below the declared target, identifying coherent ground-state access as the current bottleneck. QDMin inherits expected $O(\sqrt{N_q})$ candidate-query scaling in the unstructured model; total advantage depends on the many-body oracle cost and remains unestablished.

</details>


### [139] [Subsystem Spacetime Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40357)
*Esther Xiaozhen Fu,Aranya Chakraborty,Daniel Gottesman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构造子系统时空码（subsystem spacetime code），用其刻画Clifford电路的故障距离；通过瞬时稳定子群的掩蔽性质显式构造并分类其规范群，证明其恰为对电路无害的故障集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码为容错Clifford电路的分析与设计提供了有力框架，但缺乏能直接刻画电路（尤其是动力学码与组合式电路）故障距离的统一工具，需要识别电路在何处损失距离以及组合后距离如何变化。

Method: 构造子系统时空码；利用瞬时稳定子群（instantaneous stabilizer groups）的掩蔽（masking）性质显式构造并分类其规范群；将规范群元素与对电路无害的故障相对应；据此定位动力学码丢失距离的环节，并分析由较小组件复合而成时故障距离的变化规律。

Result: 规范群可显式构造并分类，且恰好等于对电路无害的故障集合；由此可识别动力学码距离损失的位置，并刻画电路组合时故障距离的变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析与设计容错电路提供了通用工具，并可能用于构造新的动力学码（dynamical codes）。

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes provide a powerful framework for analyzing and designing fault-tolerant Clifford circuits. In this work, we construct the subsystem spacetime code and use it to characterize the distances of Clifford circuits. We show that its gauge group can be explicitly constructed and classified using the masking properties of the instantaneous stabilizer groups and consists precisely of faults that are harmless to the circuit. We use this framework to identify where a dynamical code loses distance and to analyze how the fault distance changes when circuits are composed from smaller components. Our results provide a versatile tool for analyzing and designing fault-tolerant circuits, with potential applications to the construction of new dynamical codes.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum communication: a discrimination approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40358)
*Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用状态分辨方法研究无辅助噪声量子信道通信，基于纠缠恢复与状态分辨的操作对偶，用Petz恢复映射构造纠缠生成码与显式解码器，误差界以Petz-Rényi相干信息表达，对乘积源在低于相干信息的速率下呈指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无辅助量子信道通信的可靠编码与显式解码器构造及其有限码长误差界仍需更清晰的刻画；作者希望借助纠缠恢复与状态分辨之间的操作对偶，获得原理简洁且可显式实现的传输方案。

Method: 采用状态分辨（state discrimination）视角处理噪声信道下的无辅助量子通信：由纠缠恢复与状态分辨的操作对偶出发，构造纠缠生成码，并将解码器显式地取为Petz恢复映射，从而推导出对任意信道使用次数均成立的误差界，并用Petz-Rényi相干信息表述该界。

Result: 所得误差界对每个信道使用次数n均有效（非渐近），且以Petz-Rényi相干信息为参数；对乘积源，误差随码长呈指数衰减，可达速率低于相干信息。

Conclusion: 状态分辨与纠缠恢复的对偶为无辅助量子通信提供了统一且可显式构造的编码—解码框架，给出有限码长误差保证与基于Petz-Rényi相干信息的指数收敛速率，为接近相干信息的可实现量子通信提供依据。

Abstract: We develop a state discrimination approach to unassisted quantum communication through a noisy quantum channel. Starting from the operational duality between entanglement recovery and state discrimination, we construct entanglement-generation codes with explicit decoders constructed from Petz recovery maps. The resulting error bounds hold for every number of channel uses and are expressed in terms of Petz-Rényi coherent information. For product sources, they yield exponential error decay at rates below coherent information.

</details>


### [141] [Rapid mixing for Gibbs samplers for quantum spin systems with all-to-all interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40361)
*Nicola Bertoni,Giacomo De Palma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究任意相互作用图（含全连接）上两体局域哈密顿量的Gibbs态制备，基于量子Dobrushin条件与一阶量子Wasserstein距离给出收缩估计，得到对数混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间局域性为具几何结构的系统提供强有力工具，但全连接模型需处理任意自旋对之间的相互作用，缺乏几何或图增长假设时如何保证快速热化是核心问题。

Method: 采用满足KMS细致平衡的Lindblad动力学；引入经典Dobrushin条件的量子类比以控制局域扰动对其余系统的影响；利用一阶量子Wasserstein距离量化扰动在动力学下的传播，并在由每格点最大总相互作用强度控制的高温区导出收缩估计。

Result: 收缩估计不依赖空间几何或图增长假设，混合时间在系统尺寸与误差容限倒数上均为对数级，且对所有初始态一致成立。

Conclusion: 量子Dobrushin型条件结合Wasserstein收缩是分析无几何约束（含全连接）量子多体系统热化的有效框架，可给出统一的快速混合保证。

Abstract: Understanding when quantum many-body systems rapidly converge to thermal equilibrium is a central problem in quantum statistical mechanics. While spatial locality provides powerful tools for systems with an underlying geometry, fully connected models require estimates that accommodate interactions between every pair of spins. We study Gibbs-state preparation for two-local quantum Hamiltonians on arbitrary interaction graphs, including fully connected systems, via Lindblad dynamics that satisfies Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) detailed balance. Our approach is based on a quantum analogue of the classical Dobrushin condition, which controls the influence of local perturbations on the rest of the system. Using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order $1$, we quantify how these perturbations propagate under the dynamics and derive contraction estimates in a high-temperature regime controlled by the maximum total interaction strength per site. These estimates require no assumptions on spatial geometry or graph growth and yield mixing times logarithmic in the system size and the inverse error tolerance, uniformly over all initial states.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum Computing through Computational Imaging: Representations, Measurement, and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40393)
*George Kourousias,Sergio Carrato,Roberto Pugliese,Panagiotis Dimitrakis,Alberto Carini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 一篇面向计算成像/工程读者的教学性论述，用四个区分澄清"成像模型与量子态的代数对应≠计算方法"：振幅编码的输入与完整标签编码不同、复场干涉导致需互补测量推断未知相位、联合振幅重排为矩阵连接SVD与纯态纠缠、四像素分割中目标函数变为代价算符与采样线路（QAOA一层降均值能量但非最优标签概率反超最优）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出，成像模型与量子态之间的代数对应关系本身并不指定任何计算方法；真正决定"能返回什么"的是输入访问方式、测量方式与解码方式。因此需要为计算成像与工程背景读者建立一套概念区分，避免把态表示误当作可用的完整读出与经典可模拟性。

Method: 提出并展开四个区分：(1) 区分输入值的振幅编码与完整候选标签的振幅编码，说明振幅存储不提供全阵列读出；(2) 由复场干涉引出未知相位推断，用互补测量消除局部Fisher信息无法检测的模糊性；(3) 将联合振幅重排为矩阵，把奇异值分解与纯态纠缠联系起来，并区分Schmidt秩与高效经典可模拟条件；(4) 在四像素分割中将目标转化为代价算符与采样线路，用一层QAOA与均匀采样对比，并以IBM与IQM归档测量说明六变量局部边界决策。方法上仅依赖复线性代数与概率，配合可复现数值示例与"Seeing Amplitudes"浏览器实验平台。

Result: 四像素分割中，一层QAOA将平均能量从均匀采样的1.675降至0.686，但使非最优标签比最优标签更可能（0.484对0.365）；归档的IBM与IQM测量另用于演示六变量局部边界决策；低秩态重构与若干量子算法被纳入端到端资源核算。

Conclusion: 每种表示形式都应对应其允许的估计或决策以及该输出所需的资源；只有同时计入输入访问、测量与解码的资源账目，代数对应才具有计算意义。文章自包含并提供交互实验室，适合作为成像与工程读者的入门性方法梳理。

Abstract: An algebraic correspondence between an imaging model and a quantum state does not specify a computational method: input access, measurement and decoding determine what can be returned. This exposition develops four distinctions for computational-imaging and engineering readers. It separates amplitude encodings of input values from encodings of complete candidate labelings, explaining why amplitude storage does not provide full-array readout. Complex-field interference motivates unknown-phase inference: complementary measurements resolve ambiguities that local Fisher information alone does not detect. Reshaping joint amplitudes into a matrix connects singular-value decomposition to pure-state entanglement, while distinguishing Schmidt rank from conditions for efficient classical simulation. In a four-pixel segmentation, an objective becomes a cost operator and a sampling circuit. One layer of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) lowers mean energy from $1.675$ to $0.686$ relative to uniform sampling, yet makes a nonoptimal labeling more probable than the optimum 0.484 versus 0.365. Archived IBM and IQM measurements illustrate a separate six-variable local boundary decision. Low-rank state reconstruction and selected quantum algorithms connect these distinctions to an end-to-end resource account. The derivations and reproducible numerical references assume complex linear algebra and probability, without prior quantum mechanics. The accompanying Seeing Amplitudes browser laboratory supports interactive exploration; the article remains self-contained and connects each representation to the estimate or decision it permits and the resources that output requires.

</details>


### [143] [Fault-Tolerant Heisenberg-Limited Quantum Metrology with Bosonic Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40405)
*Daniel G. Li,Lorcán O Conlon,Yu-Xin Wang,Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Twesh Upadhyaya,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于玻色探针的容错量子传感协议，在退相位噪声下估计位移，通过最优误差矫正码与重复编码量子比特寄存器、玻色宇称测量与重置实现探针噪声消除，使Fisher信息在约γ_A^{-(d+1)/2}的探测时间内达到NT²标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子传感可利用纠缠与相干演化实现海森堡极限标度，但环境噪声与硬件误差破坏敏感量子态相干性；已有容错传感方案多针对量子比特系统，而许多实际传感器需要玻色探针，故需构建玻色容错传感协议。

Method: 在平均光子数约束下构造最优纠错码；用受控位移将探针与重复编码的量子比特寄存器耦合；通过重复的玻色宇称测量与重置去除探针噪声而不擦除所存逻辑相位；分析电路级比特翻转噪声下的逻辑失效阶数。

Result: 距离d的码将逻辑失效抑制至p^{(d+1)/2}阶；对衰变率γ_A、总传感时间T与平均光子数预算N，在小残余信号下Fisher信息在约γ_A^{-(d+1)/2}的探测时间内标度为NT²，显著优于非容错方案仅在O(γ_A^{-1})总时间内实现海森堡标度。

Conclusion: 玻色容错传感可在含噪条件下延长海森堡极限标度的有效探测时间，为实用玻色型量子传感器提供可扩展的鲁棒性方案。

Abstract: Quantum sensors have been shown to enable more precise measurements than classical sensors. By using entanglement and coherent evolution, the estimation error can be made to scale better in interrogation time, photon count, and other resources, achieving Heisenberg-limited scaling. However, environmental noise and hardware errors typically destroy the coherence of the sensitive quantum states. This motivates the use of fault-tolerant techniques to create more robust quantum sensing protocols. Such fault-tolerant quantum sensing approaches have been shown to enhance sensitivity for qubit systems, but many practical sensors require bosonic probes. In this paper, we propose a fault-tolerant protocol using bosons to estimate displacements in the presence of dephasing noise. We construct an optimal error-correcting code under a mean photon-number constraint. Controlled displacements couple the probe to a repetition-encoded qubit register, while repeated boson-parity measurements and resets remove probe noise without erasing the stored logical phase. Under circuit-level bit-flip noise, a distance-$d$ code suppresses logical failures to order $p^{(d+1)/2}$. We show that, for a decay rate $γ_A$, a total sensing time $T$ and a mean photon number budget $N$, and for sufficiently small residual signals, the Fisher information scales as $NT^2$ over interrogation times of order $γ_A^{-(d+1)/2}$. This is a significant improvement over the non-fault-tolerant approach that achieves Heisenberg scaling only for a total time $\mathcal O(γ_A^{-1})$.

</details>


### [144] [Structural Bottlenecks in QAOA Mixer Design: Transport and Causal Reach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40424)
*Jasjyot Singh Gulati,Tharrmashastha SAPV*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文从"构型空间输运"和"有限深度因果可达"两个结构性瓶颈分析浅层QAOA混合器设计，Part I给出混合器依赖的一阶目标界与非微扰上包络，Part II通过问题图对齐的二体相互作用扩充横向场混合器以提升MaxCut性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合器选择是QAOA的核心设计决策，但混合器的不同结构性局限无法由单一指标刻画，缺乏对不同瓶颈的分解式理解。

Method: Part I：在小β与小γ互补区域推导一阶混合器依赖目标界，利用图拉普拉斯恒等式证明连通正则混合器图上两式严格等价并归约为代价加权Dirichlet能量；再由逐层ℓ1扩散导出非微扰上包络与必要扩散条件。Part II：对MaxCut在横向场混合器上加入问题图对齐的二体相互作用，构造k-hop混合器（深度p时因果半径≤(k+1)p），并对环、圆梯子、高围长正则二分图数值测试X+XX、X+YY与X的比较。

Result: 连通正则混合器图下小β与小γ两区域的首阶表达式经图拉普拉斯恒等式严格等价，同归约为代价加权Dirichlet能量；ℓ1扩散给出代价分布相关的必要条件。数值上X+XX与X+YY在同深度下优于标准X混合器；X+YY在深度p接近X在深度2p约一个近似比点以内。等可达的XX与YY表现不同，说明满足结构能力并不保证性能。

Conclusion: 输运与因果可达是两个互补且不可由单一指标替代的结构瓶颈；结构可达性是性能的必要而非充分条件，二者共同为混合器设计提供结构性指导。

Abstract: The choice of mixer is a central design decision in the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), yet different structural limitations of a mixer need not be captured by a single metric. We identify two complementary structural bottlenecks for shallow QAOA: configuration-space transport, which limits how strongly probability can concentrate on high-quality solutions, and finite-depth causal reach, which limits which parts of a problem instance can influence a local objective term. In Part I, we derive first-order mixer-dependent objective bounds in complementary small-$β$ and small-$γ$ regimes. For connected regular mixer graphs, the two leading-order expressions are exactly equivalent through a graph-Laplacian identity and reduce to the same cost-weighted Dirichlet energy. We then derive a non-perturbative upper envelope from the layer-wise $\ell_1$ spreading of the mixer unitary, yielding a cost-distribution-dependent necessary spreading condition for attaining a target approximation ratio. In Part II, we study the locality bottleneck for MaxCut by augmenting the transverse-field mixer with problem-graph-aligned two-local interactions. The resulting $k$-hop construction has causal radius at most $(k+1)p$ at depth $p$; for the $k=1$ architectures studied numerically, this gives radius $2p$. Across cycles, circular ladders, and high-girth regular bipartite graphs, $X{+}XX$ and $X{+}YY$ improve the best-found approximation ratios over the standard $X$ mixer at equal depth, while $X{+}YY$ at depth $p$ comes within about one approximation-ratio point of plain $X$ at depth $2p$ in the reach-matched cases examined. The unequal performance of the equal-reach $XX$ and $YY$ augmentations shows that satisfying a structural capability does not by itself guarantee performance. Together, the results provide complementary structural insights for mixer design.

</details>


### [145] [A universal gauge structure in the photon content of light-matter systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40446)
*Arka Dutta,Marcus Kollar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在单模光-物质模型截断希尔伯特空间后，规范变换保持能谱不变但基态光子数依赖规范。作者证明该依赖服从精确代数定律：$\langle a^\dagger a\rangle_\lambda = \langle a^\dagger a\rangle_0 + C\lambda + Q\lambda^2$，其中 $C=\langle G p\rangle$、$Q=\langle G^2\rangle$，其全局最小值为规范不变量，并对量子Rabi与Dicke模型给出正值结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范变换下能谱不变而基态光子数却依赖规范参数，这一矛盾使基态光子数的物理意义长期存在争议。作者希望厘清这种规范依赖的结构，并找出一个规范无关、可赋予物理意义的量来刻画基态的"残余光子含量"。

Method: 考虑规范变换表现为对与物质算符 $G$ 耦合的场作位移的情形，将基态光子数表示为规范参数 $\lambda$ 的函数并解析推导其精确形式。系数被识别为关联函数 $C=\langle G\,p\rangle$（$G$ 与场动量 $p$ 的关联）和 $Q=\langle G^2\rangle$。再对所有规范取最小值构造规范不变量，并在量子Rabi模型、含能级劈裂的Rabi与Dicke模型（含热力学极限）中分析该最小值及取最小值的规范。

Result: 基态光子数严格为 $\lambda$ 的二次多项式 $\langle a^\dagger a\rangle_\lambda=\langle a^\dagger a\rangle_0+C\lambda+Q\lambda^2$；其对所有规范的最小值规范不变，且在量子Rabi模型中仅由基态能量决定。该最小值对含能级劈裂的Rabi与Dicke模型严格为正，故任何规范下都存在残余光子。弱耦合下最小光子数对应的规范为Jaynes-Cummings规范；对Dicke模型，在热力学极限下该结论对所有耦合强度均成立。

Conclusion: 基态光子数的规范依赖具有精确二次代数结构，其规范不变的最小值是刻画基态光子含量的合理物理量；该量在Rabi/Dicke模型中恒正，并在弱耦合（及Dicke热力学极限全耦合）下由Jaynes-Cummings规范实现。

Abstract: After truncating the matter Hilbert space of a single-mode light-matter model, gauge transformations leave the spectrum invariant whereas the ground-state photon number is gauge dependent, hence, its meaning remains disputed. We show that this gauge dependence obeys an exact algebraic law whenever the gauge transformation acts as a displacement of the field coupled to a matter operator $G$. Namely, the ground-state photon number is a quadratic polynomial in the gauge parameter $λ$, $\langle a^{\dagger} a\rangle_λ$ $=$ $\langle a^{\dagger} a\rangle_{0}$ $+$ $Cλ$ $+$ $Qλ^2$. Here the coefficients are the correlator $C=\langle G\,p\rangle$ of $G$ and the field momentum $p$, and $Q=\langle G^2\rangle$. Its minimum over all gauges is naturally gauge-invariant, and for the quantum Rabi model it is determined by the ground-state energy alone. We show that it is strictly positive for the quantum Rabi and Dicke models with level splitting, so that a residual photon content survives in every gauge. At weak coupling the gauge of minimal photon number is the Jaynes-Cummings gauge, and for the Dicke model it remains so at all couplings in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [146] [Process matrices on generalised theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40448)
*Matt Wilson,James Hefford,Timothée Hoffreumon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将过程矩阵框架推广到一大类广义物理理论，用"对经典探针的自然性"重新公理化时空语境，刻画了时空语境可由过程矩阵表示的理论，并给出超映射与过程矩阵两种高阶过程观点一致的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原过程矩阵框架主要针对量子理论定义不定因果序，缺乏在更一般的广义概率理论中的统一表述，且时空语境（spacetime context）的公理化基础与超映射、过程矩阵两种高阶过程视角之间的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 以组合式（compositional）范畴视角，用"对经典探针的自然性"对时空语境进行简洁的重新公理化，该公理精确对应非语境性指派，并兼容凸性与局部实验室内粗粒化；据此刻画哪些理论中的抽象时空语境可 admit 过程矩阵表示，并分析超映射与过程矩阵观点一致的条件。

Result: 给出了时空语境非语境性、凸性与粗粒化兼容的精确公理化；干净地刻画了允许过程矩阵表示的理论类；并确定超映射与过程矩阵观点一致的精确条件，经典理论、量子理论及类 Boxworld 理论均满足这些条件。

Conclusion: 过程矩阵框架可自然推广至广义物理理论，时空语境的自然性公理化为其提供统一基础，且在经典、量子与类 Boxworld 等广泛理论中两种高阶过程形式体系相互一致。

Abstract: We extend the process-matrix framework for defining indefinite causal orders to a broad class of generalised physical theories. We give a simple re-axiomatisation of spacetime contexts in terms of naturality over classical probes, which precisely captures non-contextual assignment together with compatibility with convexity and coarse-graining within local laboratories. Using this compositional perspective, we give a clean characterisation of theories in which abstract spacetime contexts admit a process-matrix representation, and we pin down the precise conditions (satisfied by classical theory, quantum theory, and a Boxworld-like theory) under which the supermap and process-matrix perspectives on higher-order processes coincide.

</details>


### [147] [Trajectory sharpening under quantum reference frame transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40452)
*Matteo Fadel,Giulia Gubitosi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文将负质量参照系下的轨迹锐化效应解释为量子参考系变换，用有符号质量自由粒子玩具模型建立EPR型可观测量与一对对易关系型可观测量的对应，并给出锐度的单配性与分布式刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子参考系（QRF）变换描述了同一物理系统在不同量子观察者眼中的图景，但其与具体实验现象的联系尚不清楚。本文旨在为连续变量系统的QRF变换提供可操作、与实验相关的解释。

Method: 采用有符号质量自由粒子的极简玩具模型：将探测系统与有效负质量参考系关联所得的轨迹锐化效应，诠释为向负质量系统参考系的QRF变换；把导致该效应的EPR型可观测量识别为一对相互对易的关系型可观测量，并证明在瞬时QRF变换下它们映射为分属不同子系统的局域算符；进一步用动力学QRF变换复现相应关系型轨迹（变换后的关系型位置显含时间）；最后引入QRF不变的锐度度量。

Result: 瞬时QRF变换下关系型可观测量对应到不同子系统的局域算符；动力学QRF变换复现该关系型轨迹且关系型位置显含时间；由QRF不变锐度度量导出单配关系，表明轨迹不能同时对两个不同负质量参考系任意锐化；锐度可转而分布于跨两个物理系统的集体参考模式中，即使任一单独组分都不能提供任意锐的参考。

Conclusion: 轨迹锐化效应可自然理解为负质量参考系下的QRF变换，锐度是可分配而非局域于单一组分的资源，从而为连续变量系统的QRF变换给出了与实验相关的操作化诠释。

Abstract: Quantum reference frame (QRF) transformations relate the description of a physical system to different quantum observers, but their connection to concrete experimental phenomena remains largely unclear. Here we show that the trajectory-sharpening effect obtained by correlating a probed system with an effective negative-mass reference admits a natural interpretation as a QRF transformation to the frame of a negative-mass system. Using a minimal toy model of free particles with signed masses, we identify the Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen-type observables responsible for this effect with a pair of commuting relational observables that, under the instantaneous QRF transformation, are mapped to operators local to distinct subsystems. We then show that the corresponding relational trajectory is reproduced by the dynamical QRF transformation, where the transformed relational position acquires an explicit time dependence. We introduce QRF-invariant sharpness measures for relational trajectories and derive monogamy relations showing that a trajectory cannot be made simultaneously arbitrarily sharp with respect to two distinct negative-mass frames. We further show that trajectory sharpness can instead reside in a collective reference mode encoded across two physical systems, even when neither constituent alone provides an arbitrarily sharp reference, so that sharpness can be distributed rather than localized in a single constituent frame. These results provide an operational, experimentally relevant interpretation of QRF transformations for continuous-variable systems.

</details>


### [148] [Quantum-Walk Inspired Spectral Metrics for Evaluating QAOA Mixers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40459)
*Jasjyot Singh Gulati,Tharrmashastha SAPV*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从量子行走混合理论出发，提出两种与代价无关的谱启发式指标，用于刻画QAOA混合器（mixer）哈密顿量的拓扑结构，并在多种图拓扑上验证其与实际QAOA性能的一致性及局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA混合器对探索指数级解空间至关重要，但混合器哈密顿量的选择通常由惯例、实现或线路约束决定，缺乏考虑图拓扑结构的判据。为此作者希望建立一个拓扑层面、与代价无关的谱度量，用以指导混合器的选择与比较。

Method: 作者受量子行走混合理论启发，定义两个互补的谱启发式：(1) 混合器哈密顿量 H_M 的归一化最小特征值间隔；(2) 混合器酉算符 U_M(β)=e^{-iβH_M} 的长时间平均最小特征值间隔。两者对 H_M 的正数缩放均保持不变，分别提供哈密顿量层面与酉算符层面的谱结构刻画，作为混合器在QAOA演化中引起振幅重新分布的动力学指标。作者对完全图、超立方体、环和平衡二维环面推导精确表达式与渐近界，并与经验QAOA性能进行比较。

Result: 所研究的几类拓扑在这些指标上呈现较大的谱间隔差异。对随机置换不变（permutation-invariant）代价哈密顿量，实测QAOA性能排序与谱刻画大体一致；但对结构化的一维和二维 Ising QUBO，该排序不再成立，说明混合器与代价的匹配（mixer-cost alignment）变得重要。

Conclusion: 研究表明，拓扑层面、与代价无关的谱启发式既是选择QAOA混合器的有用工具，也存在明显局限：其有效性依赖于代价哈密顿量是否为结构化，混合器-代价的耦合不可忽视。

Abstract: QAOA mixers play an important role in enabling exploration of the exponentially large solution space of binary optimization problems. However, the choice of mixer Hamiltonian is typically guided by convention and implementation or circuit constraints, rather than by a graph-topology-aware criterion. In this work, inspired by quantum-walk mixing theory, we propose two complementary spectral heuristics for characterizing QAOA mixer topologies. The first is the normalized minimum eigenvalue separation of the mixer Hamiltonian $H_M$, while the second is the long-time averaged minimum eigenvalue separation of the mixer unitary $U_M(β)=e^{-iβH_M}$. Both quantities are invariant under any positive rescaling of the mixer Hamiltonian and provide Hamiltonian- and unitary-level characterizations of the mixer's spectral structure. We use them as heuristic indicators of the mixer dynamics relevant to amplitude redistribution during QAOA evolution.
  We derive exact expressions and asymptotic bounds for the complete graph, hypercube, cycle, and balanced 2-D torus, revealing a broad spectral separation between the studied topology classes. We then compare these separations with empirical QAOA performance. For random permutation-invariant cost Hamiltonians, the observed performance ordering is broadly consistent with the spectral characterization. In contrast, for structured 1-D and 2-D Ising QUBOs, this ordering breaks down, indicating that mixer-cost alignment can become important. These results illustrate both the usefulness and the limitations of topology-level, cost-agnostic spectral heuristics for QAOA mixer selection.

</details>


### [149] [Lattice moments and quantum designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40481)
*Jonathan Conrad,Kristian Holm*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明幺模GKP（Gottesman–Kitaev–Preskill）分布系综不构成rigged量子3-设计，并量化导致该失效的replica缺陷，方法为自包含地重新推导辛格上的二阶矩公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验GKP码态系综在随机性/伪随机性意义上的设计阶数，明确其不能达到rigged 3-设计的原因与偏差大小。

Method: 对幺模GKP分布系综，在辛格（symplectic lattice）上以自包含方式重新推导二阶矩公式，并由此计算replica缺陷量，用以检验rigged设计条件。

Result: 该系综不满足rigged量子3-设计的条件；其偏离程度可由推导出的replica缺陷定量刻画。

Conclusion: 幺模GKP系综的随机性弱于rigged 3-设计，二阶矩分析（辛格二阶矩公式）提供了判定与量化该缺陷的统一工具。

Abstract: We show that the ensemble of unimodular Gottesman$-$Kitaev$-$Preskill distributions fails to form a rigged quantum $3$-design and quantify the replica defect that determines this failure. This is established by rederiving a second moment formula for symplectic lattices in a self-contained manner.

</details>


### [150] [Fast and accurate neural triage decoding of ultra-high-rate quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40490)
*Hong-Ye Hu,Zhenlai Wang,Binzhao Luo,Tao Xiang,Chen Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"神经分诊解码"(neural triage decoding)框架，利用综合征一致性自适应分配神经计算量，在高码率qLDPC码上达到近最优算法解码器的精度，同时将解码延迟降低约1–2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高码率qLDPC码可显著降低容错量子计算的物理量子比特开销（码率>1/2，约千个数据比特规模），但其实际应用亟需兼具高精度与低延迟的解码器。现有高码率qLDPC解码器精度高但计算延迟大，而神经网络解码器尚未在高码率qLDPC码上得到验证。

Method: 提出神经分诊解码框架：以综合征一致性作为判据自适应分配神经计算。(1) 局部神经消息传递网络快速预测纠错；(2) 若仍有残余综合征违例，则增加迭代；(3) 对剩余rare样本启用"多样化"阶段搜索一致解。同时给出对精确逐比特边缘解码器的严格分析，揭示与码距无关的综合征不一致误差底（error floor），并考察网络对误差先验失配的鲁棒性。

Result: Triage解码器可解码编码数百个逻辑量子比特的超高码率qLDPC码（含lifted-product码与Kasai码），精度与近最优算法解码器相当（state-of-the-art），解码延迟降低约1–2个数量级。分析表明精确逐比特边缘解码器存在不依赖码距的综合征不一致误差底。此外，神经网络解码器对失配的误差先验具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应神经解码是实现近期快速、精确且可扩展的量子纠错的有前景路径。

Abstract: Quantum error correction is essential for fault-tolerant quantum computing. High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes have emerged as a promising approach to reducing the substantial physical qubit overhead of quantum error correction, enabling encoding rates exceeding $1/2$ in blocks of approximately one thousand physical data qubits. However, their practical implementation requires decoders that combine high accuracy with low latency to keep pace with quantum hardware. Existing decoders of high-rate qLDPC codes can achieve high accuracy but often incur substantial computational latency, whereas neural network decoders have yet to be demonstrated for high-rate qLDPC codes. Here, we introduce \emph{neural triage decoding}, a framework that uses syndrome consistency to adaptively allocate neural computation. A local neural message-passing network rapidly predicts error corrections, additional iterations reduce residual syndrome violations, and a rarely invoked diversification stage searches for consistent corrections to the remaining shots. We demonstrate that the \texttt{Triage} decoder can decode ultra-high-rate qLDPC codes encoding a few hundred logical qubits, including lifted-product and Kasai codes, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy comparable to the near optimal algorithmic decoders while reducing decoding latency by approximately one to two orders of magnitude. Our framework is inspired by a rigorous analysis of the exact bitwise marginal decoder, revealing a distance-independent syndrome-inconsistent error floor. In addition, we show the robustness of neural network decoders to mismatched error priors. Our results establish adaptive neural decoding as a promising route towards fast, accurate, and scalable quantum error correction in the near term.

</details>


### [151] [Using domain walls to benchmark fast quantum anneals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40504)
*Shushmi Chowdhury,Asa Hopkins,Nick Chancellor,Viv Kendon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用D-Wave快速退火在短于相干时间的退火下观测到畴壁的正弦平方量子分布；最短退火下呈现新的U型并据此提出以畴壁尾部伸展度作为平台无关的相干时间与噪声指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在反平行边界条件下受挫一维Ising自旋链中，理想情形下畴壁分布应为量子情形的正弦平方（粒子在盒中）分布或经典情形的均匀分布，但含随机缺陷的实际器件中此前观察到两者皆非的U型分布。作者希望厘清该U型的物理来源，并区分量子相干与经典随机场/热平均的贡献。

Method: 以反平行边界的一维受挫Ising自旋链为模型体系，比较理想量子和经典预期分布。使用D-Wave新近的快速退火功能，将退火时间缩短到相干时间以下，直接探测相干量子动力学，并分析不同退火时长下的畴壁分布形状（包括最短退火时长下的分布形态）。

Result: 在短于相干时间的退火下观察到符合预期的正弦平方畴壁分布，表明恢复了对相干量子行为的探测；而在此前含随机场的经典模型中，随机场平均与热平均的组合产生U型分布。对于可用的最短退火时间，动力学尚未达到正弦平方形状，而是在链中部呈现另一种U型轮廓。

Conclusion: 畴壁分布形状可作为区分相干量子动力学与随机场/热平均经典效应的诊断手段；在该最短退火区制下，畴壁分布尾部的伸展范围可作为平台无关的度量，同时给出相干时间与噪声水平的特征信号。

Abstract: We consider the distribution of domain walls arising in ground states of frustrated 1-dimensional Ising spin chains with anti-parallel boundary spins. For ideal chains, a sine-squared particle-in-a-box distribution of domain walls is expected in the quantum setting, and a uniform distribution expected classically. However, for chains with random imperfections, prior work [Chancellor et al., npj Quantum Inf. 8, 73, 2022] found that the distribution in a physical implementation corresponded to neither. Instead, the domain wall distribution forms a U-shaped profile arising from a classical model with random fields, where the combination of averaging over these fields and thermal averaging produces the characteristic U-shape. With D-Wave's newer fast anneal feature, we access anneal times shorter than the coherence time and observe the expected coherent quantum behavior in the form of a sine-squared distribution. For the shortest available anneal time, the dynamics have not yet reached the sine-squared shape and instead display a different U-shape in the middle of the chains. This motivates using the extent of the domain wall distribution tail in this regime as a platform independent metric that provides a signature of both coherence time and noise levels.

</details>


### [152] [Grow, Compress, and Perturb: A Hybrid Quantum-Classical Pathway to Scalable Quantum Chemistry on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40561)
*Hugo D. Nogueira,Siwar Badreddine,Olivier Adjoua,Diata Traore,Guillaume Michel,Jean-Philip Piquemal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出无哈密顿量梯度的分层VQE框架VULCAN，通过变分双括号流方程动态生成拟设算符，结合线性规模的最小完备池、整层同步追加、增长-压缩方案与主导行列式子空间投影，显著降低测量与比特成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE及其变体是量子计算电子结构计算的主要候选方法，但基于梯度的算符选择需大量哈密顿量梯度测量，限制了可扩展性。

Method: VULCAN（变分增建、分层压缩、自适应N体微扰）：用变分双括号流（VDBF）方程动态生成拟设算符；采用线性缩放的极小完备池（比QEB池小4-12倍）；整层同时追加以完全消除哈密顿量梯度测量；用高效态重叠测量的“先增长后压缩”策略控制线路深度；将哈密顿量投影到主导行列式子空间以减少比特需求；并以GPU加速的随机Epstein-Nesbet（ENPT2）微扰校正作为后处理实现提前终止。

Result: 在最多32个逻辑比特的高性能模拟上，对氢链、BeH2和H2O仅用批量ADAPT-VQE测量成本的5-20%即达到化学精度；对32比特C3H6（批量ADAPT-VQE无法达到化学精度），单层VULCAN加随机ENPT2校正即可达标。

Conclusion: VULCAN通过去除梯度测量、分层构造与子空间约简，在保持化学精度的同时大幅降低测量与比特开销，随机ENPT2后处理支持在达到化学精度时提前终止，提升了VQE类方法的可扩展性。

Abstract: While Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) variants are among leading candidates for electronic structure calculations on quantum computers, gradient-based operator selection limits their scalability. To solve this, we introduce the Hamiltonian-gradient-free VULCAN (Variational Upbuilding, Layered Compression, Adaptive N-body perturbation) layered VQE framework. Our method dynamically generates ansatz operators using Variational Double Bracket Flow (VDBF) equations. Combined with a linearly scaling minimal complete pool, it creates a pool 4-12 times smaller than QEB (Qubit-Excitation-Based) pools. By appending entire layers simultaneously, Hamiltonian gradient measurements are completely eliminated. Circuit depth is managed via a grow-then-compress scheme using efficient state-overlap measurements, and qubit requirements are reduced by projecting the Hamiltonian onto a dominant-determinant subspace. Using high-performance emulation up to 32 logical qubits, we achieve chemical accuracy at just 5-20% of the measurement cost of batched ADAPT-VQE for hydrogen chains, BeH2 and H2O. For the 32-qubit C3H6, where batched ADAPT-VQE does not reach chemical accuracy, a single VULCAN layer combined with a GPU-accelerated stochastic Epstein-Nesbet (ENPT2) correction does. This stochastic ENPT2 post-processing step enables early termination at chemical accuracy.

</details>


### [153] [Algebraic strong-to-weak symmetry breaking and partial decodability in the surface code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40567)
*Stephen W. Yan,Yimu Bao,Sagar Vijay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 相干噪声可在三角晶格表面码中诱导出部分可译码的临界相，其对偶为Z2顺磁体中的代数型强弱自发对称破缺(SWSSB)相；作者用SO(n)非线性sigma模型在n→1复本极限下统一描述，并给出解码保真度与对偶保真度关联子的普适预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干(非Pauli)噪声下的量子纠错与混合态序不被标准Pauli噪声框架覆盖；作者希望统一理解"可译码相变"与混合态中SWSSB的对应关系，并厘清相干噪声相较Pauli退相干的"非经典"本质。

Method: 通过Wegner对偶联系两个模型：(1)三角晶格表面码受单比特Pauli-Z旋转后接综合征测量；(2)Z2顺磁体受强对称退相干。构造弱耦合SO(n)非线性sigma模型，在复本极限n→1下解析求解。

Result: 增大旋转角使系统从可译码相进入部分可译码临界相，对偶侧从强对称相进入代数型Z2 SWSSB相。解析得到普适特征：最优Pauli解码保真度在热力学极限下趋于仅依赖长宽比的普适函数；对偶保真度关联子呈普适幂律衰减，二者对应sigma模型中twist算符的关联。大规模数值模拟验证了这些预言。并论证代数SWSSB相无法由任何Pauli退相干信道实现。

Conclusion: 相干噪声可产生Pauli噪声无法实现的量子信息恢复与混合态序；通过初始态中相距很远的Z2磁通插入探测量子态敏感性，揭示了不同Z2荷轨迹间长程持续的相干性，体现了该相的"非经典"特征。

Abstract: Coherent noise can give rise to unconventional regimes of quantum information recovery and mixed-state order. We study these phenomena in two settings related by Wegner duality: the triangular-lattice surface code subject to single-qubit Pauli-$Z$ rotations followed by syndrome measurements, and a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ paramagnet subject to strongly symmetric decoherence. Increasing the rotation angle drives a transition from a decodable phase to a \emph{partially decodable} critical phase in the code, and from a strongly symmetric phase to a phase with \emph{algebraic} $\mathbb{Z}_2$ strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) in the dual paramagnet. We develop a common description of these phenomena in terms of a weakly coupled $\mathrm{SO}(n)$ nonlinear sigma model in the replica limit $n\to1$. Taking this replica limit analytically, we derive universal signatures of the critical phase: an optimal Pauli decoding fidelity that approaches a universal function of the system's aspect ratio in the thermodynamic limit, and a dual fidelity correlator that decays as a universal power law. These observables relate to correlations of twist operators in the sigma model description. Large-scale numerical simulations confirm these analytical predictions. We also argue that the algebraic SWSSB phase cannot be realized by any Pauli dephasing channel acting on the $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ paramagnet. We probe this "non-classical" character through the output state's sensitivity to well-separated $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ fluxes inserted in the initial state, which reveals persistent coherence between distinct $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ charge trajectories at long distances.

</details>


### [154] [On the Quantum Chromatic Gap II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40571)
*Lorenzo Ciardo,Antoine Mottet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了 Khot 的 d-to-1 博弈的强形式伪心灵感应猜想：存在经典值任意小但具有完美有限维量子策略（且测量上下文半径任意大）的 d-to-1 标签覆盖博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确认作者先前 [SODA'26] 提出的关于 d-to-1 博弈强伪心灵感应形式的猜想，并给出量子色数间隙 χ_q=3 而经典色数任意大的首个改进。

Method: 先构造“拉伸”的 Mermin–Peres 魔方（通过拉开方程并用长方程线连接以增大测量上下文半径同时保持经典不可满足性），再施加并行重复降低经典值，用 Dinur–Guruswami–Khot–Regev 的 PCP 构造将其转化为伪心灵感应的多层 Kneser 超图，最后输入 Fei–Minzer–Wang 的超图染色到 4-to-1 博弈归约，证明该归约的量子完备性并保持上下文半径。

Result: 对任意 d≥4、ε>0、k∈N，存在经典值小于 ε 却具有使用有限维纠缠、测量上下文半径至少为 k 的完美量子策略的 d-to-1 标签覆盖博弈；并据此得到 χ_q=3 而 χ 任意大的图。

Conclusion: 这是自 Brassard–Cleve–Tapp [PRL'99]（给出 χ 在 χ_q 中指数增长）以来量子色数间隙的首次改进。

Abstract: We confirm a conjecture of the first author [SODA'26] on a strong form of pseudo-telepathy for Khot's $d$-to-$1$ games: for every $d\geq 4$, every $ε>0$, and every $k\in\mathbb N$, there is a $d$-to-$1$ label-cover game of classical value less than $ε$ that admits a perfect quantum strategy using finite-dimensional shared entanglement with measurement contexts of radius at least $k$. As a consequence, there exist graphs whose quantum chromatic number $χ_q$ is $3$ and whose classical chromatic number $χ$ is arbitrarily large. This is the first improvement on the quantum chromatic gap since the work of Brassard, Cleve, and Tapp [Phys. Rev. Lett.'99], which produced graphs for which $χ$ grows exponentially in $χ_q$.
  To prove the conjecture, we proceed as follows. We start with a ``stretched'' version of the Mermin--Peres magic square, obtained by spacing out its equations and linking them with additional long equation wires. This operation increases the radius of the initial measurement contexts of a perfect quantum strategy for the game, while preserving classical unsatisfiability. We then apply parallel repetition to make the classical value small, and we turn the system into a pseudo-telepathic multi-layered Kneser hypergraph via a PCP construction of Dinur, Guruswami, Khot, and Regev [STOC'03]. Finally, we feed the resulting hypergraph into the reduction of Fei, Minzer, and Wang from hypergraph colouring to $4$-to-$1$ games, we establish quantum completeness of this reduction, and we show that it preserves context radius.

</details>


### [155] [What Can Be Computed with Indefinite Causal Structure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40573)
*Omri Shmueli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在过程矩阵（process matrix）形式化框架下研究不定因果序的计算能力：证明一般过程矩阵计算可在多项式时间内求解所有 NP 问题（生成通道酉、满足纯化公设），并给出纯过程矩阵下唯一汇定向（unique-sink orientation）的多项式时间算法，从而表明不定因果序可带来超越 BQP 的计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：当量子操作不必遵循确定的因果顺序时，量子计算是否会变得更强大。作者希望在 Oreshkov 等人的过程矩阵形式化与"电路生成过程矩阵"的计算模型下，严格刻画不定因果序的计算复杂度地位，并厘清其与 BQP 的关系及酉性、纯性等物理约束的影响。

Method: 采用过程矩阵形式化，将过程矩阵视为由量子电路简洁生成，算法可访问其生成的通道，计算代价以生成电路规模衡量。构造两类显式且高效可构造的酉生成器：(1) 一般过程矩阵情形，所构造的有效过程满足 Araújo 等人的纯化公设，生成通道为酉但生成电路非酉；(2) 纯过程矩阵情形，构造面向唯一汇定向问题的多项式时间算法。

Result: 在一般过程矩阵设定下，多项式时间算法足以求解 NP 中的每个问题；在纯过程矩阵设定下，给出唯一汇定向问题的多项式时间算法，该问题涵盖优化领域若干长期未决的计算问题。

Conclusion: 若唯一汇定向不存在量子多项式时间算法，则结论表明：即使在保持酉性的前提下，过程矩阵形式化中的不定因果序也能提供超越 BQP 的计算能力，说明因果结构的不确定性本身是一种可被利用的计算资源。

Abstract: Can quantum computation become more powerful when operations do not need to follow a definite causal order? We study this question in the process matrix formalism of Oreshkov et al. (Nature Communications, 2012), which allows quantum operations to be connected without a fixed global order. In the computational model, a quantum circuit succinctly describes a process matrix, and the algorithm is given access to its generated channel, with computational cost measured by the size of the generating circuit.
  We give polynomial-time algorithms in two settings. First, general process matrix computation suffices to solve every problem in $\textbf{NP}$. The valid processes used by our algorithm satisfy the purification postulate of Araújo et al. (Quantum, 2017), and their generated channels are unitary, although their generating circuits are not. Second, requiring the process matrices themselves to be pure, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for unique-sink orientation, a general problem capturing several longstanding computational problems in optimization. Our unitary generators are constructed explicitly and efficiently. Assuming that unique-sink orientation has no quantum polynomial-time algorithm, our result shows that indefinite causal order can provide computational power beyond $\textbf{BQP}$ within the process matrix formalism, even while preserving unitarity.

</details>


### [156] [Classical communication over quantum channels: does entanglement help in nature?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40585)
*Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一般的Schur加权块熵不等式，证明所有相位协变信道（含多能级振幅阻尼、广义退相位、阻尼-退相位信道）不存在纠缠辅助的经典通信优势，并顺带重新导出"所有qubit信道无纠缠优势"的已有结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典通信中跨信道独立使用纠缠码字能否提升经典通信速率是一个长期猜想问题；Hastings等虽否证了"所有信道无优势"的猜想，但所需信道维度极大，暗示许多物理信道或所有低维信道可能无纠缠优势。作者受广义振幅阻尼信道近期进展启发，希望在更广的信道族上界定这一边界。

Method: 提出一般的Schur加权块熵不等式（Schur-weighted block entropy inequality），并将其应用于相位协变信道族，证明其经典容量在有无纠缠辅助下相同；该族涵盖多能级振幅阻尼信道、广义退相位信道与阻尼-退相位信道，并作为副产品重获qubit信道的结论。

Result: 证明所有相位协变信道均无纠缠辅助经典通信优势，覆盖多能级振幅阻尼、广义退相位、阻尼-退相位信道；并独立地重新得到"所有qubit信道无纠缠优势"的已有结果。

Conclusion: 纠缠对经典通信的优势并非普遍存在：在相位协变这一大类物理相关信道（含全部qubit信道）中不存在优势，支持"多数物理信道无纠缠优势"的判断；Schur加权块熵不等式可作为统一分析工具。

Abstract: Can entangling codewords across independent uses of a quantum channel improve the rate of classical communication? An old conjecture states that there is no such entanglement advantage for all channels. Although Hastings's result and follow-up works disproved this conjecture, it turns out that enormous dimension is required to construct a channel where entanglement enhances classical communication. This indicates that there might be no entanglement advantage for many physical channels, or for all low dimensional channels. Motivated by recent progress on (generalized) amplitude damping channels, we propose a general Schur-weighted block entropy inequality, and show that there is no entanglement advantage for all phase-covariant channels including multi-level amplitude damping channels, generalized dephasing channels, and damping-dephasing channels. As a by-product, we also recover the recently established result that there is no entanglement advantage for all qubit channels.

</details>


### [157] [Toric codes all the way down: decoupling translation-invariant topological CSS codes in higher dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40592)
*Mingxin He,Yifei Wang,Hao Song,Yingfei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文刻画了高于二维的平移不变p元拓扑CSS码在超晶格上可解耦为高形式环面码堆叠的充要条件，指出除各超选择层上平移作用平凡外，唯一残余障碍是层间"粘合"，并证明有限激发类型情形下额外粗粒化p^{m-1}可消除粘合，同时给出显式SUM/SWAP电路构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维拓扑CSS码具备横截非Clifford门、单次译码等容错量子计算优势，但缺乏系统理论。解耦（用有限深度局域CSS保持Clifford电路将其映射为高形式环面码堆叠）是统一理解此类码的有效途径，然二维已有判据，高维情形因激发按维度分层并可能"粘合"而尚不清楚。

Method: 对平移不变的p元（p为素数）CSS码，要求电路与超晶格的平移对易。以激发按维度的超选择分层及层间粘合（gluing）为不变量，构造可实现解耦的有限深度局域CSS保持Clifford电路（允许加辅助比特）。

Result: 证明解耦的充要条件：粗粒化到超晶格后，平移在每一层上作用平凡且粘合消失。对每层激发类型有限、非零层数为m的码，一旦各层平移平凡，再沿各方向粗粒化因子p^{m-1}即可消除粘合。物理上同一判据决定码在有限环面上是否编码最大数量逻辑qudit。并给出由SUM（qudit CNOT）与SWAP门构成的显式解耦电路算法。

Conclusion: 高维平移不变拓扑CSS码的解耦障碍完全由"各层平移平凡性+层间粘合"刻画，粘合可通过有限粗粒化消除，并提供了可构造的电路算法，为高维拓扑码的容错性质与逻辑容量分析建立了系统判据。

Abstract: Topological Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes in more than two spatial dimensions offer features useful for fault-tolerant quantum computation, such as transversal non-Clifford gates and single-shot decoding, but a systematic understanding of them is still lacking. One approach is to decouple a code: map it to a stack of higher-form toric codes by a finite-depth local CSS-preserving Clifford circuit, possibly after adding ancillas. For translation-invariant $p$-ary CSS codes, where $p$ is prime ($p=2$ for qubits), we require the circuit to commute with the translations of a superlattice. In two dimensions, such a decoupling exists once these translations act trivially on every anyon type. In higher dimensions, excitations are sorted by their dimension into superselection layers, which can remain glued together even when translations act trivially on each layer. We show that this gluing is the only remaining obstruction: a code can be decoupled on a superlattice exactly when, after coarse-graining to it, translations act trivially on every layer and the gluing vanishes. Moreover, for a code with finitely many excitation types in each layer and $m$ nonzero layers, once translations act trivially on every layer, further coarse-graining by a factor of $p^{m-1}$ in every direction removes the aforementioned gluing. Physically, the same criterion determines when the code encodes the maximal number of logical qudits on a finite torus. Finally, we give an explicit algorithm that constructs the decoupling as a circuit of SUM (qudit CNOT) and SWAP gates.

</details>


### [158] [Optimal detuning and gate time for strongly driven single-qubit gates beyond the rotating-wave approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40595)
*Alejandro D. Bendersky,Sergio S. Gomez,Rodolfo H. Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在弱耦合旋转波近似（RWA）之外，利用二阶有效Magnus-Taylor哈密顿量理论分析单量子比特门的不保真度；通过类似Bloch-Siegert的振幅依赖频率失谐与操作时间重标定恢复RWA门条件并修正参数，使门不保真度降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RWA仅为弱耦合提供闭式门参数；为加速动力学而增大驱动振幅时，被忽略的反旋转项及其引起的共振移动（Bloch-Siegert shift）使RWA设计的门失准，因此需要研究超越RWA的门保真度并给出修正方案。

Method: 采用驱动频率倒数二阶截断的有效Magnus-Taylor哈密顿量进行理论分析；对驱动频率施加振幅依赖的Bloch-Siegert型失谐，并对操作时间进行重标定，从而以修正后的参数恢复RWA门条件；并用薛定谔方程数值解验证。

Result: 数值求解薛定谔方程表明，所提出的修正可将门不保真度降低数个数量级，并扩展了可用振幅范围。

Conclusion: 振幅依赖的Bloch-Siegert型失谐与时间重标定能有效补偿反旋转项效应，在超越RWA下实现高保真单量子比特门，可用于更快的驱动方案。

Abstract: Single-qubit quantum gates are implemented as the time-controlled unitary evolution of a two-level system under harmonic driving. The rotating-wave approximation (RWA) provides closed-form gate parameters for weak coupling. Larger amplitudes, desirable to speed up the dynamics, make the RWA-designed gates inaccurate because of the neglected counter-rotating term, which also shifts the resonance (Bloch-Siegert shift). Here we study theoretically the infidelity of gates beyond the RWA, using the effective Magnus-Taylor Hamiltonian through second order in the inverse of the driving frequency. By detuning the driving frequency by an amplitude-dependent shift of Bloch-Siegert type and rescaling the operation time, the RWA gate conditions are recovered with corrected parameters. Numerical solutions of the Schrodinger equation show that these corrections lower the gate infidelity by several orders of magnitude, extending the range of usable amplitudes

</details>
