### [159] [Optimal Diamond Distance Estimation for Unitary Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40623)
*Jinge Bao,Qisheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出用 $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$ 次对酉信道及其逆的查询、以加性误差 $\varepsilon$ 估计两个未知 $d$ 维酉信道间菱形距离的量子算法，查询复杂度最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱形距离刻画两量子信道单次使用时的最优区分概率，是信道区分的核心度量；但对未知信道的菱形距离估计缺乏已知的高效算法与紧致复杂度刻画。

Method: 设计量子算法，借助对两个未知 $d$ 维酉信道及其逆 $U, U^{-1}, V, V^{-1}$ 的查询，将菱形距离估计转化为可相干访问的酉算子比较问题，实现加性误差 $\varepsilon$ 的估计。

Result: 查询复杂度为 $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$，与 Jeon 和 Oh 给出的下界匹配，故算法在查询复杂度上达到最优。

Conclusion: 该工作给出估计酉信道菱形距离的最优查询复杂度算法，闭合了该问题的复杂度刻画，表明误差与维度权衡下的 $\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon$ 标度为本质极限。

Abstract: The diamond distance between two quantum channels determines the optimal probability of distinguishing them from a single use. We give a quantum algorithm that estimates the diamond distance between two unknown $d$-dimensional unitary channels to within additive error $\varepsilon$, using $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$ queries to the unitary channels and their inverses. The query complexity is optimal, as it matches the lower bound due to Jeon and Oh (npj Quantum Inf. 2026).

</details>


### [160] [Left-Handed Transmission Lines for the Dynamical Casimir Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40624)
*Valentin E. Salari,Fernando C. Lombardo,Paula I. Villar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了由SQUID磁通调制端接的左手超导传输线中的动力学卡西米尔效应，并将其与同等元件的右手线及共面波导进行对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 左手传输线具有非零下带边与高频稀疏模式等独特谱结构，其在动力学卡西米尔效应中的耦合与模式占据行为尚不清楚，需与右手线及常规共面波导作系统对比。

Method: 构建串联电容与并联电感组成的左手超导传输线，端接磁通调制的SQUID；推导量子哈密顿量，证明两模式间耦合在SQUID处分解为各自模式振幅之积，并结合左手谱结构与耦合的模式依赖分析模式布居及增长率；对比同等元件的右手线及端接同一SQUID的长共面波导，考察双模泵浦、共振线交叉等情形。

Result: 同等元件下，左手耦合随频率增长，在由单胞电感与电容决定的交叉泵浦频率之上超过右手线；仅左手线在右手带边以上存在共振。双模泵浦时弱耦合模式可"借用"强耦合模式的耦合；共振线交叉可使三或四个模式被布居但不提高增长率。与共面波导相比，左手线耦合相近，却能将产生的粒子保留在目标模式，而近等间距波导将粒子散布到多对模式并需更高泵浦能量。

Conclusion: 左手传输线的谱结构与模式依赖耦合共同决定模式布居选择性与增长速率，使其在特定泵浦条件下优于右手线与共面波导，具备模式选择性强的动力学卡西米尔粒子产生优势。

Abstract: Left-handed (LH) superconducting transmission lines, built with series capacitors and shunt inductors instead of the series inductors and shunt capacitors of a conventional right-handed (RH) line, have a spectrum with a nonzero lower band edge and modes that become sparse at high frequencies. We study the dynamical Casimir effect in a LH line terminated by a flux-modulated SQUID and compare it with an RH line built from the same elements and with a long coplanar waveguide terminated by the same SQUID. We derive the quantum Hamiltonian and show that the coupling between two modes factorizes into their amplitudes at the SQUID. Together, the LH spectrum and the mode dependence of this coupling determine which modes are populated and how fast. With equal elements, the LH coupling grows with frequency and exceeds the RH one above a crossover pump frequency set by the cell inductance and capacitance, and only the LH line has resonances above the RH band edge. Pumping two modes at once lets a weakly coupled mode borrow coupling from a strongly coupled one, while crossings of resonance lines populate three or four modes without increasing the growth rate. Compared with the coplanar waveguide, the LH line has a similar coupling but keeps the created particles in the target mode, whereas the nearly equidistant waveguide spreads them over many pairs and needs more pump energy.

</details>


### [161] [Locality in Dissipative Ground-State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40628)
*Qicheng Tang,Ruhanshi Barad,Xueda Wen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究自由费米子局域哈密顿量的基态能否由局域耗散制备，提出动量空间解析性判据并给出no-go定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨局域耗散能否制备局域哈密顿量的基态这一基本问题，聚焦自由费米子体系，厘清目标哈密顿量的谱结构如何决定耗散制备的局域性。

Method: 采用含线性跳跃算符的二次Lindbladian进行耗散基态制备；对平移不变目标，利用傅里叶对应将空间局域性转化为动量空间解析性，推导指数局域线性跳跃算符存在的完整谱判据，并构造带代数衰减关联的二次能带接触模型说明高维的额外阻碍。

Result: 得到指数局域线性跳跃算符存在性的完整谱判据；证明变号费米面交叉排除指数局域线性算符的no-go定理；该交叉在一维是唯一阻碍，高维则存在额外阻碍（以两个二次能带接触模型为例，基态关联仅代数衰减）；并表明短程基态关联虽非必要但足以保证局域耗散制备，且不依赖平移不变性。

Conclusion: 目标哈密顿量的谱结构决定了耗散基态制备的局域性；变号费米面交叉与高维能带接触构成本质障碍，而短程关联是局域可制备性的充分条件。

Abstract: Can the ground state of a local Hamiltonian be prepared by local dissipation? We address this question for free-fermion Hamiltonians, using a quadratic Lindbladian with linear jump operators for the dissipative ground-state preparation. For translation-invariant targets, Fourier correspondence relates spatial locality to momentum-space analyticity, allowing us to derive a complete spectral criterion for the existence of exponentially local linear jump operators that prepare the ground state. This criterion leads to a no-go theorem that, sign-changing Fermi-level crossings exclude exponentially local linear jump operators for preparing the ground state. While these crossings are the complete obstruction in one dimension, additional obstructions generally arise in higher dimensions. We illustrate these additional obstructions using two quadratic-band-touching models with algebraically decaying ground-state correlations. We further show that short-range ground-state correlations, although not necessary, are sufficient for local dissipative preparation even without translation invariance. Our results establish how the spectral structure of the target Hamiltonian governs the locality of dissipative ground-state preparation.

</details>


### [162] [Entanglement diagnostics of stochastic-resetting steady states: theory and measurement on superconducting qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40638)
*Jakob Murauer,Sabine Tornow,Manas Kulkarni,Eli Barkai,Satya N. Majumdar,Monalisa Singh Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究随机重置下横场Ising链的纠缠：解析求解双比特情形，并在IBM超导处理器上实验验证；揭示重置历史"分辨/平均"两种熵之差（Jensen gap）等于子系统对"距上次重置时间"的Holevo信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重置可将量子系统驱动至无限受幺正演化无法达到的非平衡定态，纠缠性质依赖于重置历史是被分辨还是被平均。作者希望厘清这一区别在具体多体模型中的表现，并给出实验可检验的预言。

Method: 在横场Ising链中，对双比特给出闭式解，对至多12比特进行精确数值计算；在IBM超导处理器上测量重置平均前后的熵与concurrence；用单参数去极化模型解释concurrence抑制。

Result: 双比特闭式解显示能级间的相位匹配产生共振，其在定态中的可见度由重置态的对称性决定；硬件上两种熵（重置平均前后）在无拟合参数下高度吻合无噪声预言，而concurrence在多数测量中低于预言，去极化模型以单一拟合参数可重现其压低。两熵之差即纠缠熵的Jensen gap，等于子系统保留的关于"距上次重置时间"的Holevo信息；Bell态重置下该信息在半链切割处随链长增长而无明显饱和。

Conclusion: 随机重置下"历史平均"与"历史分辨"纠缠的差异具有清晰的信息论含义（Holevo信息/Jensen gap），且因重置时刻经典采样记录，轨迹平均熵无需后选择即可获得，相较monitored circuits避免了指数稀有测量记录采样难题。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting drives quantum systems into nonequilibrium stationary states unreachable under uninterrupted unitary evolution, and their entanglement depends on whether the reset history is resolved or averaged over. We study this interplay in the transverse-field Ising chain, deriving closed-form results for two qubits and evaluating chains of up to 12 qubits exactly. In the two-qubit system, these closed-form results show that phase matching between energy eigenstates produces resonances, and their visibility in the stationary state is determined by the symmetry of the reset state. Measurements on an IBM superconducting processor show the entropies evaluated before and after the reset average closely following noiseless predictions with no fitted parameter, while concurrence lies below prediction across most of the hardware measurements. The two diagnostics respond differently because they read different components of the same reconstructed state, and a depolarizing model captures the concurrence suppression with one fitted parameter. The difference between the two entropies, i. e., the Jensen gap of entanglement entropy, equals the Holevo information that a subsystem retains about the time since the last reset. With a Bell-state reset, this information grows with chain length across a half-chain cut over the sizes studied, without visible saturation at the smaller reset probabilities. Since reset times are sampled and recorded classically, the trajectory-averaged entropy, and with it the Jensen gap, is obtained without postselection, in contrast to monitored circuits, where trajectory-resolved entanglement requires sampling exponentially rare measurement records.

</details>


### [163] [Comparison of entangling strategies for unitary synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40642)
*Jonas Wunder,Hendrik Weimer,Ricardo Costa de Almeida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作构建通用工具集，用酉合成作为与具体应用无关的基准，评估由原生Mølmer–Sørensen多比特门构成的门的集合的性能，并厘清增加工程复杂度与算法运行实际收益之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 囚禁离子平台具备长相干时间、近似全连通及多比特门潜力，但需要弄清提升工程复杂度（更灵活的门集合）是否带来运行量子算法的实际改进。

Method: 开发多功能工具集，以酉合成作为应用无关的基准目标，对由原生Mølmer–Sørensen多比特门组成的门集合进行性能评估。

Result: 识别出使多比特门具有优势的具体硬件要求。

Conclusion: 多比特门的实际优势依赖特定硬件条件，工程复杂度与算法性能提升需权衡。

Abstract: Trapped-ion quantum computers are promising as a platform for realizing quantum computational advantage. They promise long coherence times, near all-to-all connectivity, and potential multiqubit gates as distinguishing advantages compared to competing platforms. Regarding these advantages, it is crucial to understand the trade-offs between increasing engineering complexity and actual improvements in running quantum algorithms with a more flexible gate set. In this work, we investigate this question and develop a versatile toolset to evaluate the performance of gate sets composed of native Mølmer--Sørensen multiqubit gates. To this end, we employ unitary synthesis as an application-agnostic benchmark target and identify specific hardware requirements for multiqubit gates to be advantageous.

</details>


### [164] [Reconstructing hidden observables via quantum snapshot thermometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40652)
*Jie Ren,Andrew Hallam,Luheng Zhao,Huanqian Loh,Zlatko Papić*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"量子快照测温术"：利用局域平衡与预先标定的平衡态物态方程，从单一测量基下的投影快照中提取隐藏的守恒密度分布，进而定量重建能量前沿、扩散常数与守恒电荷分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影快照是量子模拟器的标准读出手段，但只能直接探测在测量基下对角的可观测量的期望值，粒子和能量等守恒密度剖面在实验中往往不可见。若为每个可观测量更换测量基或做量子态层析，代价高且难以扩展，因此需要一种仅用固定测量基就能获取水动力学信息的读出方法。

Method: 以局域平衡假设和独立标定的平衡态物态方程为先验，将单基投影快照中的局域统计与平衡态密度—能量关系联立，反演出温度/化学势场，从而重建守恒密度剖面。通过数值模拟验证该方法，并分析对称性导致的读出简并，说明利用空间结构先验可消解歧义。

Result: 数值实验表明：可恢复能量前沿并提取扩散常数；能区分自旋输运与能量扩散；可揭示在初始测量中不可见的守恒电荷分布。同时识别出对称性所施加的读出歧义，并证明基于空间结构的先验知识可将其消除。

Conclusion: 固定基快照测温在无需额外测量基和量子态层析的前提下，提供了超越输运指数的定量水动力学信息，为量子模拟器提供了低成本、易扩展的读出与分析方案。

Abstract: Projective snapshots are a standard readout tool for quantum simulators, but they directly probe only observables that are diagonal in the measurement basis. We show how local equilibration and an independently calibrated equilibrium equation of state can reveal otherwise hidden conserved-density profiles. We demonstrate numerically that such ``quantum snapshot thermometry'' can recover energy fronts and a diffusion constant, distinguish spin transport from energy spreading, and reveal conserved-charge profiles invisible in the initial measurements. We also identify symmetry-imposed ambiguities in the readout and show how prior knowledge of the spatial structure can resolve them. Fixed-basis snapshot thermometry thus provides quantitative hydrodynamic information beyond transport exponents, bypassing additional measurement bases and quantum state tomography.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [A photonic-integrated optomechanical mid-infrared detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37771)
*Guillermo Arregui,Amirali Arabmoheghi,Alessio Zicoschi,Xinru Ji,Scott E. Kenning,Marco Golino,Shivaprasad U. Hulyal,Yi Xia,Sunil A. Bhave,Tobias J. Kippenberg*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将高Q氮化硅纳米机械谐振器（trampoline结构）与氮化硅光子集成回路单片集成，实现微瓦级光功率室温下噪声等效功率3.4 pW/√Hz的热辐射探测器，并用于FTIR光谱测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米机械热探测器接近基本灵敏度极限时依赖自由空间或光纤耦合干涉仪，阻碍了紧凑化部署与阵列化扩展，需要以集成光子学方式替代这一瓶颈。

Method: 将受张应力的氮化硅trampoline谐振器（带阻抗匹配铂吸收体）与下方氮化硅光子集成微谐振腔色散耦合，通过跟踪谐振频率变化实现热辐射探测。

Result: 室温、微瓦级光功率下，对3–12 μm宽带热辐射的噪声等效功率为3.4 pW/√Hz（折算入射功率）；折算吸收功率时，距基本热涨落极限仅差1.5倍。在商用FTIR光谱仪中分辨聚合物薄膜2–20 μm振动指纹，入射功率低约一个数量级即达到内置热释电探测器精度。

Conclusion: 集成光机械架构突破了干涉仪耦合瓶颈，为多路复用光机械焦平面阵列提供可行路径。

Abstract: Tracking the resonance frequency of a mechanical oscillator is the basis of atomic force microscopy, mass sensing and thermometry. Nanomechanical thermal detectors exploit this principle to sense absorbed radiation, most effectively with tensile-stressed resonators, whose tension converts thermal expansion into a large frequency shift and dilutes dissipation. Approaching their fundamental sensitivity limit, however, has required free-space or fibre-coupled interferometers, hindering compact deployment and scaling to arrays. Here we remove this bottleneck by monolithically integrating a high-Q nanomechanical resonator with a silicon nitride photonic integrated circuit. A tensile-stressed silicon nitride trampoline with an impedance-matched platinum absorber is dispersively coupled to a photonic integrated microresonator beneath it. At room temperature and with microwatt-level optical power, the detector reaches a noise-equivalent power of 3.4 pW/$\sqrt{\rm Hz}$ for broadband thermal radiation (3-12 $μ$m), referred to incident power. Referred to absorbed power, this is within a factor of 1.5 of its fundamental thermal fluctuation limit. Using our detector in a state-of-the-art commercial Fourier-transform infrared spectrometer, we resolve the vibrational fingerprint of a polymer film across 2-20 $μ$m and reach the precision of the built-in pyroelectric detector with about an order of magnitude lower incident power. This architecture opens a route to multiplexed optomechanical focal-plane arrays.

</details>


### [166] [An Eigenmode Expansion Framework for Bi-Directional Multimode Nonlinear Mixing Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37846)
*Salvador Poveda-Hospital,Vincent Girouard,Yves-Alain Peter*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将参量非线性过程纳入本征模式展开（EME）框架，构建统一仿真方法，可同时处理经典非线性过程及其量子对应（自发参量下转换、自发四波混频）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂光子系统的仿真常依赖本征模式展开法与散射矩阵追踪分层介质中的电场，但该框架此前仅适用于线性问题，缺乏对参量非线性产生的建模能力，限制了其在非线性光子器件设计中的应用。

Method: 在本征模式展开框架内形式化描述参量非线性过程，将非线性极化作为源项耦合进散射矩阵表述，从而在分层介质中追踪各模式电场并计算非线性产生。该方法统一覆盖经典非线性过程与量子对应过程（SPDC、SFWM）。

Result: 所提方法能够在多种光子系统中仿真非线性产生，既给出经典非线性过程的场演化，也能描述自发参量下转换与自发四波混频等量子过程。

Conclusion: 本征模式展开框架可自然扩展至参量非线性领域，为复杂光子系统中经典与量子非线性效应的统一建模与设计提供了一种通用仿真途径。

Abstract: An approach to simulating complex photonic systems is the eigenmode expansion method, in which scattering matrices are used to model and track the electric field in layered media. In this work, we formulate parametric nonlinear processes within the eigenmode expansion framework, enabling the simulation of nonlinear generation in varying photonic systems. This new simulation method includes both classical nonlinear processes and their quantum counterparts, including spontaneous parametric down-conversion and spontaneous four-wave mixing.

</details>


### [167] [Automated identification of quasi-BIC modes in disordered resonator chains using spatial field envelopes and resonance descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37967)
*Vadim A. Porvatov,Kliment V. Semushev,Ravshanjon Kh. Nazarov,Nikolai A. Vlasov,Alexander I. Solomonov,Zarina F. Kondratenko,Ekaterina E. Maslova*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Structural disorder turns bound states in the continuum (BICs) into quasi-BIC resonances and mixes them with spectrally close eigenmodes, so the target mode must be identified anew in every disorder realization. We develop an automated procedure for this task and test it on a chain of 50 ceramic disks, where disorder reorders the standing-wave eigenmodes at the edge of the BIC band: at disorder amplitude $σ=0.05$, the highest-quality-factor ($Q$) mode is the target in only 45\% of realizations, and above $σ\approx0.06$ the highest-frequency mode is no longer the target. The target is identified from the spatial envelope of its field and its resonance parameters by an ensemble of a convolutional--recurrent neural network and two gradient-boosted decision-tree models. On 299 held-out realizations, the ensemble selects the target in 82.6\% of cases, against 60.9\% and 49.5\% for the highest-frequency and highest-$Q$ rules, and in 69.5\% of cases for $σ\ge0.09$, where the best of these rules reaches 57.6\%. The mean $Q$ of the selected modes follows that of the manually labeled modes ($\langle Q\rangle\proptoσ^{k}$, $k=-0.80\pm0.05$ versus $-0.86\pm0.04$). After adaptation on 50 labeled realizations, the workflow identifies the quasi-BIC of a Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ bar metasurface in 98--100\% of labeled cases, including a lossy phase where $Q$ does not distinguish the mode.

</details>


### [168] [Drift-induced nonreciprocity in image-polariton-mediated dipole emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38348)
*Christian Nicolaisen Hansen,Eduardo J. C. Dias,Christos Tserkezis,Joel D. Cox*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究漂移偏置石墨烯/hBN异质结构中的非互易等离激元，揭示hBN声子极化激元耦合可显著调控非互易响应，且镜像极化激元结构在保持Fizeau位移的同时实现两倍光场限域与~40%左右不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电流偏置提供无需磁场的纳米尺度非互易光传播途径，但石墨烯漂移偏置体系中与hBN声子极化激元耦合如何影响非互易响应、以及能否兼顾强限域与Fizeau位移尚不清楚。

Method: 采用随机相位近似（RPA）与玻尔兹曼输运方程刻画漂移偏置石墨烯的非局域光学响应；对比石墨烯/hBN与石墨烯-薄hBN间隔层-金衬底构成的镜像极化激元结构，分析极化激元色散随漂移的方向性移动；以交叉光学态密度（CDOS）量化偶极-偶极相互作用的非互易性。

Result: 载流子漂移引起极化激元色散显著的方向性位移，与hBN声子极化激元的耦合强烈修饰非互易响应；镜像极化激元结构中石墨烯极化激元限域提升至约两倍，同时保持可比的Fizeau位移；非互易色散带来定向近场发射与非互易偶极-偶极相互作用，左右不对称度最高约40%。

Conclusion: 漂移偏置石墨烯中的镜像极化激元可作为紧凑、电可调的纳米尺度量子发射器定向耦合平台。

Abstract: Electrical current bias offers a magnet-free route to nonreciprocal light propagation at the nanoscale. Here, we theoretically explore drift-induced nonreciprocity in graphene heterostructures containing hexagonal boron nitride (hBN), where graphene plasmons interact with hBN phonon polaritons in the infrared. Both the random-phase approximation and the Boltzmann transport equation are used to characterize the nonlocal optical response of drift-biased graphene. We compare graphene on hBN with an image-polaritonic structure comprising graphene, a thin hBN spacer, and a gold substrate. Carrier drift produces pronounced directional shifts of the polariton dispersion, while coupling to hBN phonon polaritons strongly modifies the nonreciprocal response. In the image-polaritonic structure, the graphene polaritons become up to twice as confined while retaining a comparable Fizeau shift. The nonreciprocal polariton dispersion produces directional near-field emission and nonreciprocal dipole-dipole interactions, the latter quantified through the cross density of optical states, reaching a left-right dissymmetry of up to ~40%. Our results identify image polaritons in drift-biased graphene as a compact, electrically tunable platform for directional coupling between quantum emitters at the nanoscale.

</details>


### [169] [Confocal ellipsoidal displaced Gregorian structure for stand-off millimeter-wave imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38403)
*Mohammad Hossein Koohi Ghamsari,Mehdi Ahmadi-Boroujeni*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种共焦椭球移位格里高利（CEDG）双反射面构型，用于毫米波/太赫兹驻留成像系统，可抑制光遮挡并支持物方扫描，全波仿真得分辨率约1.5 cm、景深约49.7 cm，3 m处最大视场40 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在毫米波与太赫兹驻留成像系统中，反射式结构相比折射式具有更低的频率相关损耗与像差，是光学分系统的优选方案；但传统格里高利结构的次镜会造成光遮挡，且不利于物方扫描，需要一种兼顾低遮挡与扫描能力的小型低成本构型。

Method: 设计共焦椭球移位格里高利双反射面结构：主镜为标准椭球面，将出射光线聚焦于焦平面；次镜为改进型面以减小遮挡并提升成像性能。给出逐步构造流程、解析研究与闭式设计公式；采用光线追迹仿真评估遮挡，用全波仿真验证分辨率与景深；通过次镜倾斜与馈源横向位移研究扫描性能与驻留距离调谐。

Result: 光线追迹表明CEDG结构的遮挡较标准格里高利结构降低；全波仿真得分辨率1.5 cm、景深49.7 cm，与理论预测吻合良好；利用次镜倾斜与馈源位移，在3 m距离处获得最大视场40 cm，满足现代实用驻留成像需求；并验证了通过馈源横向位移可调谐驻留距离。

Conclusion: 该紧凑、低成本的双反射面构型在遮挡抑制、扫描能力与驻留距离调谐方面表现良好，具备用于实用毫米波与太赫兹驻留成像系统的潜力。

Abstract: Reflective-based structures are favorable candidates as the optical section of millimeter-wave and terahertz (THz) stand-off imaging systems due to decreasing frequency-dependent losses and aberrations compared to refractive-based counterparts. In this paper, we propose a simple confocal ellipsoidal displaced Gregorian (CEDG) dual-mirror configuration for stand-off imaging systems that exhibits superior performance in terms of mitigating optical blockage and enabling object scanning. The proposed structure is composed of a standard elliptical main mirror, which focuses the emanated rays on the focal plane, and a modified secondary mirror (SM) that reduces the optical blockage effect and improves the imaging performance of the structure. A detailed step-by-step procedure is proposed for constructing the structure, followed by an analytical study and deriving closed-form design equations. By performing ray tracing simulations, it is observed that the optical blockage of the proposed CEDG structure is reduced compared with the standard Gregorian. Also, the resolution and the depth-of-focus (DoF) are obtained at 1.5 cm and 49.7 cm in full-wave simulations, respectively, demonstrating good agreement with the theoretical predictions. Moreover, the scanning performance of the CEDG structure is investigated by utilizing the tilting of the SM and feed point displacement, presenting a maximum field-of-view (FoV) of 40 cm at a distance of 3 m, which is acceptable for modern practical stand-off imaging systems. Finally, the capability of the proposed structure for tuning the stand-off distance by lateral displacement of the feeding source is verified. As a result, the proposed compact, low-cost configuration shows potential for practical mm-wave and THz stand-off imaging systems.

</details>


### [170] [Engineering Canalized Near-Field Radiative Heat Transfer between Fixed Nanoparticles with Twisted $α$-MoO$_3$ Trilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38583)
*Yang Hu,Pablo Alonso-González*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在固定实验室坐标系（颗粒轴与底层晶向）下，通过调控α-MoO₃三层结构的内部扭转角，优化后的三层结构近场辐射热功率达双层最大值的2.84倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲范德华晶体中的通道化声子极化激元为纳米尺度热流调控提供途径；作者希望在固定实验室几何（颗粒轴与底层晶向固定）条件下，量化内部扭转自由度对近场辐射热传递（NFRHT）的额外调控能力。

Method: 以已建立的扭转α-MoO₃三层平台为对象，在保持总厚度与双层相等的前提下扫描两个内部扭转角；计算指定纳米颗粒对之间的NFRHT，并借助动量空间格林张量诊断与实空间能量密度分布图分析机制。

Result: 最优三层结构辐射功率为双层最大值的2.84倍，内部扭转构型平均功率为1.51倍；第二内部扭转坐标扩大了强传递构型集合，并在单独变化任一内部角时呈现局域角度容差；增强源于更强的朝向接收端散射与更优的通道对准；所选构型在颗粒共振与长距离下仍保持增强耦合。

Conclusion: 内部扭转构型自由度是在固定实验室坐标系几何下额外工程化辐射热传递的有效手段。

Abstract: Canalized phonon polaritons in twisted van der Waals crystals offer a route to control nanoscale thermal energy flow. Building on the established twisted $α$-MoO$_3$ trilayer platform, we examine how internal twist angles modify near-field radiative heat transfer (NFRHT) between a prescribed nanoparticle pair while both the nanoparticle axis and the bottom-layer crystallographic orientation remain fixed. Under this constraint and at equal total $α$-MoO$_3$ thickness, the optimized trilayer reaches 2.84 times the bilayer maximum radiative power, while its mean power over the sampled internal twist configurations is 1.51 times the bilayer value. The second internal twist coordinate also enlarges the set of configurations supporting strong transfer, with local angular tolerance when either internal angle is varied separately. Momentum-space Green-tensor diagnostics and real-space energy-density maps associate the enhancement with stronger receiver-directed scattering and improved channel alignment. Selected trilayer configurations retain enhanced coupling across the sampled family of nanoparticle resonances and at long distances. These results quantify the additional internal configurational freedom available for engineering radiative heat transfer under a fixed laboratory-frame geometry.

</details>


### [171] [Maxwell's equations are universal for sparse linear algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38913)
*Yannick Augenstein*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文证明任意多项式条件数的稀疏线性系统都可编译为一块三维无损各向同性介电腔体，其时间谐波响应即编码解；结构体积与读写开销约为 $\tilde O(m^{3/2})$，系数仅需 $O(\log m)$ 比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学与波动模拟计算已被用于矩阵求逆与积分方程求解，但被动结构究竟能表示哪些问题、在计入编码、精度、条件数与读出后代价如何，尚不清楚。作者希望给出一个严格的表达能力与复杂度刻画。

Method: 将稀疏线性系统嵌入三维介电腔体（有界介电常数的无损各向同性材料），令其时间谐波响应编码解；在连续 Maxwell 方程上给出证明，并针对有限差分求解所用的 Yee 离散给出计算机辅助验证版本。

Result: 对 $m$ 个非零系数，结构体积及写入/读出代价均为 $\tilde O(m^{3/2})$，系数只需 $O(\log m)$ 比特；任何近似满足 Maxwell 方程的场都解码为近似解。反向解读表明，电磁仿真在相同时开销下与一般稀疏线性代数一样难：足够快的通用 Maxwell 求解器可导出更快的稀疏线性求解器。

Conclusion: 被动波动物理结构可通用地表示多项式条件数稀疏线性系统，且从复杂度上电磁仿真与稀疏线性代数等价（相差 $\tilde O(m^{3/2})$ 开销），为模拟计算的能力边界与求解器复杂度提供了双向结论。

Abstract: Optical and wave-based analog computing has re-emerged as a route to accelerating linear algebra, with metamaterials and photonic circuits shown to invert matrices and solve integral equations. However, it has remained unclear which problems such passive structures can represent, and at what cost, once encoding, precision, conditioning and readout are accounted for. Here, we show that every polynomially conditioned sparse linear system can be compiled into a three-dimensional dielectric -- a cavity filled with lossless, isotropic material of bounded permittivity -- whose time-harmonic response encodes its solution. For $m$ nonzero coefficients, the volume of the structure and the cost of writing it down and reading out the answer scale as $\tilde O(m^{3/2})$, coefficients require only $O(\log m)$ bits, and every field that approximately satisfies Maxwell's equations decodes to an approximate solution. We prove the construction for the continuum equations and give a computer-assisted version for the Yee discretization underlying finite-difference solvers. Read in reverse, it implies that electromagnetic simulation is, up to this overhead, as hard as general sparse linear algebra: a sufficiently fast general-purpose Maxwell solver would yield a faster sparse linear solver.

</details>


### [172] [Volumetric Nanophotonics for Enhanced Scintillation and Remote Light Outcoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39079)
*Simo Pajovic,Marius Jürgensen,Sachin Vaidya,Johnathon P. Gales,Joshua Chen,Steven E. Kooi,Sion Richards,Isabel H. B. Braddock,Karen Mamian,William Michaels,Shaul Katznelson,Chris D. Armstrong,Ido Kaminer,Marin Soljačić,Mikael C. Rechtsman,Charles Roques-Carmes*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文利用飞秒激光直写在无机闪烁体（YAG:Ce、YAG:Yb）体内部加工2.5D空气孔方阵（近亚波长周期、深度达数百微米），实验证明体相纳米光子图案可使闪烁发光增强约3.5倍，并首次演示"远程出光耦合"新功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纳米光子图案化多局限于二维表面，虽能提升闪烁体出光效率，但光子实际在体相中产生、传输与吸收，仅靠表面结构难以实现对闪烁过程的全链条调控，因此需要将纳米光子结构引入材料体相。

Method: 采用直接飞秒激光直写技术，在YAG:Ce与YAG:Yb无机闪烁体内部制备空气孔方形晶格；周期接近亚波长（相对峰值发射波长），深度可达数百微米，占体相厚度较大比例。在高能激发下测量发光亮度与发射增强随结构参数的标度关系，并考察非直接激发区域图案的出光行为。

Result: 体相图案区域在高能激发下亮度提升最高约3.5倍，其发射增强趋势与已确立的纳米光子标度律一致；同时观察到"远程出光耦合"现象——未被直接激发的纳米光子图案可输出在闪烁体其他区域产生的光。

Conclusion: 该单片式纳米加工方法将纳米光子闪烁体从表面拓展至体相，是实现基于光子学设计的闪烁过程全面调控的重要一步，对医学成像与粒子探测等应用具潜在价值。

Abstract: Nanophotonic patterning is a promising approach to enhancing light extraction in scintillator materials, with the potential to improve performance in applications such as medical imaging and particle detection. While 2D surface patterns have been successful in improving scintillator performance, the true potential of nanophotonic patterning lies in the bulk, where photons are emitted, transported, and absorbed. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the enhancement and control of scintillation using 2.5D volumetric nanophotonic patterns. Using a direct femtosecond laser writing approach, we nanofabricate square lattices of air holes into the bulk of inorganic scintillators (YAG:Ce and YAG:Yb), achieving nearly subwavelength periods compared to the peak emission wavelength and depths up to hundreds of microns, which fill a large fraction of the full thickness of the bulk. Under high-energy excitation, the patterns appear up to ~3.5$\times$ brighter and demonstrate emission enhancement trends consistent with well-established nanophotonic scaling laws. We also report a novel functionality we call "remote outcoupling," in which the nanophotonic patterns emit light that is generated in another region of the scintillator, without being directly excited themselves. Our monolithic nanofabrication approach extends nanophotonic scintillators into the bulk, which is an important step towards total control over scintillation using photonic design.

</details>


### [173] [Nonreciprocal coupling enables tunable exceptional-point response in non-Hermitian circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39157)
*Xiaoyin Sha,Chao Ma,Yuqi Jiang,Zhiqin Jiang,Junan Lai,Xiaoke Gao,Tianyu Dong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种回旋器介导的非互易耦合非厄米电路，在保持EP条件的前提下连续调节前/后向耦合强度，实现EP构型库与频率劈裂幅度的连续调控，从而在不改变平方根标度的条件下增强EP传感响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EP对弱微扰呈现平方根响应，但响应幅度由构型依赖的前因子决定，且该前因子通常较小，限制了EP传感灵敏度。已有方法难以在保持EP特性的同时调节前因子，缺少额外的设计自由度。

Method: 构建含回旋器介导耦合的非互易非厄米电路系统；非互易耦合下前向与后向耦合强度可独立变化而保持其乘积不变，从而维持EP条件并生成一族连续EP构型；通过调节耦合不对称性控制给定绝对电阻微扰引起的谱劈裂；结合理论预测、电路仿真与反射谱实验测量进行验证。

Result: 该系统支持连续的一族EP构型；给定绝对电阻微扰产生的谱劈裂可通过调节耦合不对称性被增强；频率劈裂幅度可连续调控，而平方根依赖关系保持不变；理论、仿真与实验反射谱测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 非互易耦合为工程化与增强EP诱导的微扰响应提供了另一条途径，可在不改变其分支点标度特性的情况下提升响应幅度，为EP传感设计增加新的自由度。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) exhibit characteristic square-root spectral responses to weak perturbations, with response magnitudes governed by configuration-dependent prefactors that are often relatively small. Modulating these prefactor while preserving the exceptional characteristics offers an additional degrees of freedom for engineering non-Hermitian sensing responses. Here, we report a nonreciprocal non-Hermitian circuit system with gyrator-meditated coupling that supports a continuous family of EP configurations. Unlike reciprocal coupling, which typically yields a unique EP configuration for balanced gain and loss, nonreciprocal coupling allows the directional forward and backward coupling strengths to be varied while maintaining their product and thus preserving the EP condition and yielding a library of EPs. This additional freedom enables continuous control of the frequency-splitting magnitude without modifying the square-root characteristic dependence on absolute perturbation strength. We show that the spectral splitting induced by a given absolute resistance perturbation can be enhanced by appropriately adjusting the coupling asymmetry. Theoretical predictions, circuit simulations, and experimental reflection-spectrum measurements consistently confirms this tunable response. Our results establish nonreciprocal coupling as an alternative approach for engineering and enhancing EP-induced perturbative responses without changing their characteristic branch-point scaling.

</details>


### [174] [Spatial-Mode Selection for Local Mineral Absorption Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39221)
*Qipeng Qian,Jialiang Jin,Shihao Wang,Yuntao Qian*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于空间模式解复用（SPADE）的量子启发测量框架，在固定响应泊松测量下通过模式选择抑制背景、联合反演目标吸收带深度与共享背景谱，在等时仿真中将深度RMSE较全像素直接成像降低14%–26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小矿物区域的吸收带深度需要与周边区域定量比较，但衍射将目标光与背景混合；传统光谱拟合只能分离平均贡献，无法去除已记录的背景光子噪声。

Method: 构建量子启发测量框架：用空间模式解复用（SPADE）在探测前保留目标吸收响应并降低背景接受度；将有限矿物区域几何与参考光谱同估计精度相联系；给出与损耗相关的设计条件，判断在固定响应泊松测量且场景参数未知时增加SPADE曝光是否优于直接成像（DI）；对初始DI观测与两个模式端口进行联合推断，同时估计目标深度与未知的空间共享背景谱。

Result: 在包含定位不确定度、对准时间、定标与探测器成本的等时仿真中，相对于同背景自由度的全像素DI，深度RMSE在五种吸收强度下降低14%–26%。结果依赖给定的目标特征形状与几何，目标连续谱斜率被拟合、曲率固定。

Conclusion: 该分析将光学选择性与参数模糊性、采集成本相分离，为局部矿物光谱中的空间模式接收器设计提供定量依据。

Abstract: Absorption-band depth measures the fractional decrease in mineral reflectance below its local continuum. Recovering this feature strength for a small mineral region supports quantitative comparisons between regions, but diffraction mixes its light with the surroundings. Conventional spectral fitting can separate mean contributions; it cannot remove background photon noise already recorded. We develop a quantum-inspired measurement framework that uses spatial-mode demultiplexing (SPADE) to retain the target absorption response while reducing background acceptance before detection. The framework connects finite mineral-region geometry and reference spectra to estimation precision. A loss-dependent design condition identifies when adding SPADE exposure improves on direct imaging (DI) under fixed-response Poisson measurements with unknown scene parameters. Joint inference from initial DI observations and both mode ports estimates target depth together with an unknown, spatially shared background spectrum. In equal-time simulations that include localization uncertainty, alignment time, calibration, and detector costs, this acquisition reduces depth root-mean-square error (RMSE) by 14\%--26\% across five absorption strengths relative to all-pixel DI with the same background freedom. These results use a supplied target feature shape and geometry, with target continuum slope fitted and curvature fixed. The analysis separates optical selectivity from parameter ambiguity and acquisition cost, providing a quantitative basis for spatial-mode receiver design in local mineral spectroscopy.

</details>


### [175] [Focal-Plane Extreme Adaptive Optics for Deep Super Resolution Imaging and Nanolithography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39340)
*Jinxin Liu,Jiahao Niu,Runxin Zeng,Kaining Yang,Ziyu Li,Xiaodong Guan,Jing Ren,Kebin Shi,Yue Wang,Shan Zhao,Yujie Sun,He Sun*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: FP-ExAO 通过直接感知与控制焦平面复振幅场，替代传统相位共轭校正，在深层散射介质中实现快速、大视场、纳米级超分辨成像与光刻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光学异质介质中，超分辨成像与纳米光刻受高阶、空间变化的相位和振幅像差严重限制，深度处性能急剧下降，传统相位共轭 AO 难以校正耦合的相位–振幅像差及空间变化像差。

Method: 提出焦平面极端自适应光学（FP-ExAO）：直接传感并控制焦平面复场（末端场，涵盖全系统累积像差）；采用状态空间建模直接关联变形镜指令与焦场，避免逐面傅里叶光学显式建模；结合信息论最优探测与闭式控制实现快速场估计与校正；级联变形镜用于校正耦合的相位–振幅及空间变化像差。

Result: 3D SMLM：脑组织成像深度达 160 μm、肾组织超 60 μm，在 75 μm×75 μm 校正视场内保持约 18 nm 定位精度，视场扩大 16 倍，校正速度比传统 AO 快十倍以上；STED 纳米光刻：160 μm 光刻胶深度处最细线宽由未校正的 251.1 nm 降至 88.8 nm。

Conclusion: FP-ExAO 确立了面向深层纳米尺度光学读出与写入的新自适应光学范式，满足 3D 深层组织光学连接组重建与定量超分辨病理学的成像需求。

Abstract: Super resolution optical imaging and nanolithography enable nanoscale observation and fabrication, but high order, spatially varying phase and amplitude aberrations in optically heterogeneous media severely limit their performance at depth. Here we introduce focal-plane extreme adaptive optics (FP-ExAO), which moves beyond conventional phase conjugate correction by directly sensing and controlling the focal-plane complex field. This terminal field captures aberrations accumulated throughout the optical system. A state space formulation directly links deformable mirror commands to the focal field, avoiding explicit plane by plane Fourier optics modelling, while information theoretically optimal probing and closed-form control enable rapid field estimation and correction. Cascaded deformable mirrors further enable FP-ExAO to correct coupled phase-amplitude and spatially varying aberrations. In three-dimensional (3D) single molecule localization microscopy (SMLM), FP-ExAO enables imaging to 160 um depth in brain tissue and beyond 60 um in kidney tissue, while maintaining approximately 18 nm localization precision across a 75 um x 75 um corrected field, with a 16-fold larger field of view and more than tenfold faster correction than conventional AO methods. These capabilities meet the imaging requirements for 3D deep tissue optical connectome reconstruction and quantitative super resolution pathology. In stimulated emission depletion (STED) nanolithography, FP-ExAO reduces the minimum written linewidth from 251.1 nm without correction to 88.8 nm at a depth of 160 um in photoresist. FP-ExAO establishes a new adaptive optics paradigm for deep nanoscale optical readout and writing.

</details>


### [176] [Label-free Monitoring of Nucleoid Dynamics of Bacteria for Drug Screening Using Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39496)
*Gauresh Raj Jassal,Jiaqi Li,Lei Feng,Nicholas G. Bravo-Frank,Hao Wang,Jiarong Hong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出全息显微（HM）作为荧光显微（FM）的紧凑、低成本、免标记替代方案，通过相位反演生成类荧光对比度来量化细菌类核响应，实现无标记药筛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光显微在细菌药筛中需标记，存在光漂白与光毒性，且设备昂贵复杂，限制长期活细胞成像与高通量应用，需要免标记、非特异性的替代手段。

Method: 采用正则化逆全息体层重建（RIHVR）框架从HM图像提取相位图，再对重建相位进行反演生成类荧光对比度，其中亮反相位区域对应FM所捕获的细菌类核内容；跨多种菌种、生长阶段、药物处理及处理后时间点验证该映射关系。

Result: HM相位图同时包含全细胞折射率信息与内部类核相关对比度，可同步量化细胞边界、细胞长度、类核尺寸与类核占位率，并随时间追踪药物诱导的形态与类核组织变化；信号稳定，适合长期活细胞成像。

Conclusion: 将免标记相位成像与类核尺度生理读出相连接，为自动化、高通量且实验流程更简化的细菌药筛检测铺平道路。

Abstract: We introduce holographic microscopy (HM) as a compact, low-cost, label-free, non-specific alternative to fluorescence microscopy (FM) for quantifying bacterial physiological responses during drug screening. Using the regularized inverse holographic volumetric reconstruction (RIHVR) framework, we extract phase maps from HM images and then invert the reconstructed phase to generate fluorescence-like contrast, in which bright inverted-phase regions correspond to bacterial nucleoid content captured by FM signals. We show that this relationship holds across multiple bacterial species, growth stages, drug treatments, and post-treatment time points, demonstrating that HM can consistently capture nucleoid associated structural information without fluorescent labeling. Because HM phase images contain both whole-cell refractive-index information and internal nucleoid-associated contrast, they enable simultaneous quantification of cell boundaries, cell length, nucleoid size, and nucleoid occupancy. These measurements allow drug-induced changes in bacterial morphology and nucleoid organization to be monitored over time. Unlike FM, which can suffer from photobleaching and may perturb live cells through phototoxicity, HM relies on refractive-index variations and maintains stable signal quality for long-term live-cell imaging. By linking label-free phase imaging to nucleoid-scale physiological readouts, this work establishes a path toward automated, high-throughput, and experimentally simpler bacterial drug-screening assays.

</details>


### [177] [Near-Field Engineering of Silica-Shelled Plasmonic Nanoparticles toward Enhanced Near-Infrared Fluorescence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39525)
*Eleonora G. Radaelli,Stavros Athanasiou,Robin E. Schaublin,Olivier J. F. Martin,Markus Niederberger,Fatemeh Kiani*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 结合STEM-EELS与边界元模拟，系统研究金纳米棒、金双锥与银纳米棒的近红外等离激元近场随二氧化硅壳层厚度的分布，给出用于荧光增强的形貌与亚10 nm壳层设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外荧光分子量子产率低，限制其在传感与成像中的应用；利用同波段等离激元共振的纳米颗粒可增强荧光，但需要对近场实现精确的纳米尺度调控。

Method: 合成金纳米棒（AuNRs）、金双锥（AuBPs）和银纳米棒（AgNRs）三类可调近红外等离激元共振的各向异性纳米颗粒并包覆二氧化硅作为介电间隔层；采用扫描透射电镜-电子能量损失谱（STEM-EELS）高分辨成像结合边界元电磁模拟，表征与计算近场随壳层厚度的衰减，并评估几何、材料、荧光分子内量子产率及荧光-金属间距对增强因子的影响。

Result: 薄壳层即可使大部分近场延伸到介电层之外：AuNRs为4.4 nm，AuBPs为3.1 nm；AgNRs需7.0 nm壳层以抑制氧化同时保持近场可及性。模拟进一步给出最佳壳层厚度及荧光增强对颗粒几何、材料、内量子产率和间距的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 为等离激元纳米结构中颗粒形貌与亚10 nm二氧化硅壳层的优化提供了实用设计准则，可用于近红外荧光增强体系构建。

Abstract: Near-infrared (NIR) fluorescent molecules often suffer from low quantum yields, limiting their applications in sensing and imaging. Their fluorescence can be enhanced by coupling with plasmonic nanoparticles exhibiting resonances in the same spectral region. However, this approach requires precise nanoscale control over the plasmonic near-field. Here, we combine scanning transmission electron microscopy with electron energy-loss spectroscopy (STEM-EELS) and boundary element electromagnetic simulations to engineer silica-coated anisotropic plasmonic nanoparticles. We synthesised three types of nanoparticles, gold nanorods (AuNRs), gold bipyramids (AuBPs), and silver nanorods (AgNRs) with tunable NIR plasmonic resonances. Silica serves as a dielectric spacer, enabling precise control over the distance between the nanoparticle surface and a potential molecular emitter. To assess their suitability for fluorophore attachment, we investigated the dependence of the electric near-field on silica shell thickness. High-resolution STEM-EELS mapping revealed that thin silica shells (4.4 nm for AuNRs and 3.1 nm for AuBPs) allow most of the near-field to extend beyond the dielectric layer. For AgNRs, a thicker shell of 7.0 nm was required to prevent oxidation while maintaining near-field accessibility. Electromagnetic simulations further evaluated the optimal shell thickness and the dependence of fluorescence enhancement on particle geometry, material, fluorophore internal quantum yield, and fluorophore-metal separation. Together, these results provide practical design guidelines for optimizing particle morphology and sub-10 nm silica shells in plasmonic nanostructures for fluorescence enhancement.

</details>


### [178] [Symmetry-governed nonlinear metasurface simultaneously enhances circular dichroism, helicity purity, and Q-factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39565)
*Dmitrii Gromyko,Wenhe Jia,Chengfeng Pan,Xuan Mao,Wenduo Chen,Zhen Xu,Shutao Zhang,Hongtao Wang,Bochang Wu,Wang Hao,Sergei Tikhodeev,Qijie Wang,Joel K. W. Yang,Cheng-Wei Qiu,Lin Wu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作通过超表面中“保持旋转对称、破缺镜面对称”的对称性设计，将高Q简并共振的线性手性映射到三次谐波辐射的偏振上，实现近完美的手性非线性响应并且对泵浦退偏具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆偏振光在 chiral 光学与自旋选择性光子技术中应用广泛，但将共振手性拓展至非线性谐波辐射仍具挑战：需在保持模式简并与偏振选择规则的同时获得强手性响应，并保证输出偏振(手性)可控、稳定。

Method: 提出并实验验证一种对称性调控机制：利用超表面设计保持旋转对称性以保护高Q简并共振的模式简并与偏振选择定则，同时破缺镜面对称性以产生强手性光学响应；该结构对泵浦偏振起滤波作用，并对非线性输出进行手性纯化，从而把线性手性的极大值映射到谐波发射的偏振态。

Result: 同时获得三次谐波圆二色性达 -0.98、圆偏振度 0.83、有效非线性Q因子 140；且在空间退偏激发下非线性辐射仍保持其手性，显示对入射偏振态变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 互补的“对称性保持+对称性破缺”可作为将共振手性从线性光学响应确定性转移至非线性响应的通用设计原则，为非线性手性光子学提供普适方案。

Abstract: Circularly polarized light underpins a broad range of chiroptical and spin-selective photonic technologies, yet extending resonant chirality to nonlinear harmonic emission remains challenging. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a symmetry-governed mechanism that maps the maximal linear chirality of high-Q degenerate resonances onto the polarization of the generated harmonic. Preserved rotational symmetry protects modal degeneracy and polarization-selection rules, while broken mirror symmetries enable a strong chiroptical response. The metasurface thereby filters the pump polarization and purifies the handedness of the nonlinear output, simultaneously achieving third-harmonic circular dichroism of -0.98, a degree of circular polarization of 0.83, and an effective nonlinear Q-factor of 140. Importantly, the nonlinear emission preserves its handedness even under spatially depolarized excitation, demonstrating robustness to variations in the incident polarization state. These results establish complementary symmetry preservation and breaking as a deterministic route for transferring resonant chirality from linear to nonlinear optical responses, providing a general design principle for nonlinear chiral photonics.

</details>


### [179] [Non-Markovian quantum-emitter dynamics and bound-state formation in an Aubry--André--Harper photonic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39707)
*Zahra Jalali-Mola,Jule Neidhart,Saeid Asgarnezhad-Zorgabad,Ortwin Hess*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究两能级量子发射体耦合到Aubry-André-Harper (AAH) 光子晶格中的单激发动力学，揭示准周期局域化转变、多重分形临界模式与非马尔可夫发射体动力学及发射体-光子束缚态形成之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫光-物质相互作用中，谱结构化的浴可通过改变真空涨落来调控量子发射体的演化。然而，由准周期势诱导的局域化转变及伴随的多重分形临界模式如何塑造发射体动力学，尚不清楚。

Method: 在单激发子空间内，将两能级量子发射体耦合到AAH光子晶格；通过外场调节准周期在位调制强度，使晶格从扩展区经多重分形临界点进入局域区。采用模式分析（modal analysis）方法，计算耦合位点的局态密度（LDOS）与有效谱耦合，并分析瞬态动力学及发射体-光子束缚态。

Result: 准周期调制重塑耦合位点的局态密度，从而控制发射体与晶格的有效谱耦合。瞬态动力学强烈依赖发射体与晶格模式在空间与谱上的重叠：临界调制以下的扩展模式通常导致快速布居衰减；临界与局域模式则引起相干反作用与持续的类Rabi振荡，且在临界点处尤为显著。此外，识别出由浴的准周期谱结构控制形成的发射体-光子束缚态，其与初始激发态的重叠导致部分长时布居囚禁。

Conclusion: 准周期局域化、非马尔可夫动力学与发射体-光子束缚态形成之间存在直接联系；AAH晶格为通过准周期调制调控量子发射体动力学提供了可控平台。

Abstract: In non-Markovian light-matter interactions, a spectrally structured bath can alter the evolution of a quantum emitter by modifying the vacuum fluctuations experienced by the emitter. This raises the question of how the localization transition and the associated multifractal critical modes induced by a quasiperiodic potential shape the emitter dynamics. We investigate this scenario in the single-excitation sector for a two-level quantum emitter coupled to an Aubry-André-Harper (AAH) photonic lattice. By externally tuning the strength of the quasiperiodic onsite modulation, the lattice is tuned from the extended to the localized regime through a critical point characterized by multifractal modes. Using modal analysis, we show that the quasiperiodic modulation reshapes the local density of states at the coupling site and thereby controls the effective spectral coupling between the emitter and the lattice. The transient dynamics depend strongly on the spatial and spectral overlap between the emitter and the lattice modes: extended modes below the critical modulation generally produce rapid depopulation, whereas critical and localized modes give rise to coherent back-action and persistent Rabi-like oscillations, with particularly pronounced behavior at the critical point. We further identify emitter-photon bound states whose formation is controlled by the quasiperiodic spectral structure of the bath and whose overlap with the initially excited state leads to partial long-time population trapping. These results establish a direct connection between quasiperiodic localization, non-Markovian dynamics, and emitter-photon bound-state formation.

</details>


### [180] [Partially temporal coherent spatiotemporal Laguerre-Gaussian and its propagation dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39832)
*Mengxiang Wu,Youngbin Park,Nianjia Zhang,Taemin Son,Qiwen Zhan,Andy Chong,Qian Cao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作从理论与实验上研究部分时间相干时空拉盖尔-高斯（STLG）场，其径向与角向指数可独立调控，并证明其在随机时间相位涨落下拓扑信息保持、可通过时空散光与群速度色散转换为STHG场、且两种结构在散射介质中均保持时空特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空涡旋（携带横向光子轨道角动量）目前主要在完全相干光脉冲框架下研究；将其拓展到部分相干光源可为应用提供另一条途径，但部分时间相干条件下复杂光场拓扑能否保持尚不明确。

Method: 以结构化的放大自发辐射（ASE）源产生部分时间相干STLG场，实现径向与角向指数独立可控；理论分析与实验验证在固有瞬时随机时间相位涨落下拓扑信息的保持；利用时空散光与群速度色散的联合作用实现STLG到STHG的模式转换；并测试两类结构经散射介质传播后的时空特性。

Result: 部分时间相干STLG场在瞬时随机时间相位涨落下仍良好保持时空拓扑信息；在时空散光与群速度色散共同作用下可转换为STHG场；STLG与STHG结构在通过散射介质后均保持其时空特征。

Conclusion: 部分时间相干结构光场为光子拓扑与复杂波动力学研究提供新视角，在高保真光通信及强散射环境中的高维信息处理方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Optical spatiotemporal vortices carrying transverse photonic orbital angular momentum have become an intense research topic in the recent study of structured light fields. Despite being mainly studied within the framework of fully coherent optical pulses, the complex topologies of the light field are also investigated with partially coherent light sources, offering an alternative pathway in applications. In this work, we theoretically and experimentally investigate partially temporally coherent spatiotemporal Laguerre-Gaussian (STLG) field with independently controllable radial and azimuthal indices. The spatiotemporal topological information, carried by the structured amplified spontaneous emission source, are well preserved in the presence of inherent instantaneous random temporal phase fluctuations. Under combined spatiotemporal astigmatism and group velocity dispersion, the partially temporally coherent STLG field can be converted into spatiotemporal Hermite-Gaussian (STHG) field. Both structures maintain their spatiotemporal characteristics after propagating through scattering media. Partially temporally coherent structured light fields may offer new insights into photonic topology and complex wave dynamics, with potential applications in high-fidelity optical communications and high-dimensional information processing in highly scattering environments.

</details>


### [181] [Integrated Circular Polarisation Beam Shaper Based on Tilted Waveguide Bragg Gratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39860)
*Joel Warner,William J. Hughes,James C. Gates,Peter Horak*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出倾斜波导布拉格光栅作为集成偏振光束整形器，并揭示单光栅双泵浦方案受波导双折射与散射效率差异限制，进而用正交叠加光栅架构实现任意偏振输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子平台需要不依赖宏观体光学和手动对准、且能同时调控空间模式与偏振的片上光学系统。

Method: 建立三维解析散射模型并结合数值仿真，用于设计与评估出射场；比较单倾斜光栅下两束共传播正交偏振泵浦的方案，并提出两光栅分别由相互垂直传播的两泵浦独立驱动的正交叠加光栅架构。

Result: 单光栅双共传播泵浦方案因波导双折射和散射效率差异，无法实现完整偏振控制；正交叠加光栅架构可产生任意目标偏振输出，仅在一狭窄空间方向区域内受限。

Conclusion: 该方案为量子技术提供可扩展的片上偏振工程光传输路径。

Abstract: Scalable quantum platforms require optical systems that deliver well-defined polarisation without relying on macroscopic bulk optics or manual alignment, motivating chip-integrated approaches capable of shaping both the spatial mode and the polarisation of the emitted field. By developing a full three-dimensional analytical model of the scattering process together with numerical simulations to design and evaluate the emitted field, we show that tilted waveguide Bragg gratings can act as integrated polarisation beam shapers. We find that scattering two co-propagating, orthogonally polarised pumps on a single grating is fundamentally limited: waveguide birefringence and the difference in scattering efficiency prevent full polarisation control. We overcome this with an orthogonal superimposed-grating architecture in which two gratings driven independently by two pumps propagating perpendicular to each other succeed in generating any desired polarisation output apart from directions in a narrow spatial region. The approach provides a scalable, chip-integrated route to polarisation-engineered light delivery for quantum technologies.

</details>


### [182] [Edge intelligence for distributed acoustic sensing with ultracompact neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39892)
*Yuan Gao,Yu Tao,Xuzhe Zhang,Junyi Duan,Da Zhou,Xingcan Liu,Qianlin Qian,Shaomin Zhu,Xuhui Yu,Xinyu Fan,Yangyang Wan,Zuyuan He*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出DAS-NanoNet框架，通过面向DAS信号的轻量时序算子与自适应神经架构搜索，为不同DAS场景-任务组合自动生成超紧凑网络，并发布六类场景的开放基准OmniDAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAS可将光纤转为密集振动传感阵列，但大规模测量数据难以用紧凑模型转化为可用信息；现有AI方法针对特定场景与任务优化，计算需求高，难以在紧凑、功耗受限的现场系统中部署。

Method: 构建OmniDAS开放基准（覆盖六种代表性DAS环境）；设计适配DAS信号的轻量时序算子；采用自适应神经架构搜索策略，针对每个场景-任务对自动推导紧凑网络。

Result: 在分类、定位、压缩、去噪任务上持续得到高度紧凑的架构；六类数据集上以仅1391个可训练参数达到99.68%准确率，相比已报道高精度网络参数量最多减少18098倍且性能相当或更优；在部署硬件上实现跨应用低于9微秒的推理延迟。

Conclusion: DAS-NanoNet为实现紧凑、低功耗、实时的DAS系统边缘智能提供了可行路径，通过针对具体传感场景与任务的超紧凑网络支撑边缘部署。

Abstract: Distributed acoustic sensing (DAS) transforms optical fibres into dense arrays of vibration sensors for continuous, long-range monitoring with high spatial resolution. Yet converting these large-scale measurements into actionable information with compact models that retain task performance remains challenging. Existing artificial-intelligence approaches are typically optimized for specific sensing environments and tasks, but their computational requirements can hinder deployment in compact, power-constrained field systems. Here, we introduce DAS-NanoNet, a framework that automatically constructs lightweight neural networks for different DAS scenarios and tasks, together with OmniDAS, an open benchmark spanning six representative environments. Using lightweight temporal operators tailored to DAS signals, an adaptive neural architecture search strategy derives a compact network for each scenario-task pairing. Across classification, localization, compression, and denoising tasks in diverse DAS applications, DAS-NanoNet consistently yields highly compact architectures. On a six-class dataset, it achieves 99.68% accuracy with only 1,391 trainable parameters, while delivering comparable or better performance with up to 18,098-fold fewer parameters than previously reported high-accuracy networks. Its compact architecture further enables edge deployment, achieving sub 9 $μ$s inference latency on the deployed hardware platform across different DAS applications. DAS-NanoNet provides a route towards edge intelligence for compact, low-power and real-time DAS systems through ultracompact networks tailored to individual sensing scenarios and tasks.

</details>


### [183] [Scattering-force-driven light-sheet fluorescence microscopy for scan-free three-dimensional imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39956)
*Sen Geng,Zhuoren Wan,Zhaoyang Wen,Dongxu Zhu,Yuan Chen,Wending Huang,Zijian Wang,Ming Yan,Heping Zeng*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出散射力驱动的光操控成像方法，用连续定向输运替代传统光镊逐级扫描，在光片中共记录粒子位置与荧光实现3D成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光操控可实现微尺度物体的非接触精确定位、定向输运与三维成像，但光束缚过程中的位置涨落引入空间采样误差，损害成像质量与采集速度。

Method: 利用低数值孔径聚焦光束产生轴向散射力，推动微粒穿越光片；同步记录粒子位置与荧光信号，建立粒子运动与断层图像之间的直接空间对应关系，取代传统光镊的步进式扫描。

Result: 降低位置涨落对3D成像的影响，轴向采样间隔达0.1 μm，成像速率250 Hz；可对具有多样结构特征的样品有效成像，光功率需求降低70%。

Conclusion: 结合稳健空间采样、高速采集与低光损伤，为微粒与生物样本的快速三维成像、动态观测及微尺度空间分析提供可行途径。

Abstract: Optical manipulation offers a powerful non-contact approach to precise positioning, directional transport and three-dimensional (3D) imaging of microscale objects. However, positional fluctuations during optical confinement introduce spatial sampling errors that compromise imaging quality and acquisition speed. Here, we demonstrate a scattering-force-driven optical manipulation imaging method that replaces the stepwise scanning of conventional optical trapping with continuous directional transport of microparticles. An axial scattering force generated by a low-numerical-aperture focused beam propels microparticles through a light sheet, while their positions and fluorescence signals are simultaneously recorded to establish direct spatial correspondence between particle motion and tomographic images. Experiments demonstrate that this approach reduces the effects of positional fluctuations on 3D imaging, achieving an axial sampling interval of 0.1 μm and an imaging rate of 250 Hz. The method also enables effective imaging of samples with diverse structural features while reducing optical power requirements by 70%. By combining robust spatial sampling, high-speed acquisition and reduced photodamage, this approach offers a promising route to rapid 3D imaging, dynamic observation and microscale spatial analysis of microparticles and biological specimens.

</details>


### [184] [Ghosts in the nanomachine: dynamical slowing-down in the phase-space of nanomechanical strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39968)
*Samer Houri,Agnes Zinth,Menno Poot*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用高灵敏度、低耗散腔光力系统，在时域与相空间中实验研究纳机械Duffing谐振子的ringdown（关断驱动后的自由衰减）动力学，揭示单音与双音驱动下迥异的弛豫行为，并将其归因于相空间结构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微纳机械器件的线性行为已被充分研究，而其非线性动力学仍不断呈现新奇现象；同时，快速瞬态过程需要极高灵敏度和低耗散的测量手段才能捕捉。作者希望借腔光力系统的高灵敏、低耗散优势，直接观测强驱动非线性谐振子弛豫至平衡态的瞬态动力学。

Method: 构建高灵敏、低耗散的腔光力测量平台，对纳机械Duffing谐振子施加强驱动后关断驱动，在时域和相空间中追踪其ringdown轨迹；对比单音驱动与双音驱动两种激励条件，并结合相空间结构分析弛豫路径。

Result: 单音驱动下ringdown表现为规则的指数衰减；双音驱动下出现libration（摆动）、相空间中散射式减速（slowing-down while scattering）、以及ghost slowing-down等奇异动力学。此外，谐振子能量随衰减过程非单调变化，且关断驱动与振幅实际衰减之间存在长延迟，形成动力学亚稳态（dynamical meta-states）。

Conclusion: 单音与双音驱动下ringdown动力学的显著差异可归因于相空间本身的结构；非线性谐振子的能量弛豫并非单调，且可因相空间中的滞留而产生长延迟亚稳态，为微纳非线性动力学与相空间工程提供了新的认识。

Abstract: The linear behavior of micro- and nanomechanical devices has been the topic of intense study over the past years. Their nonlinear behavior, on the other hand, continues to provide new and interesting dynamics. This work leverages a cavity opto-mechanical system whose very high sensitivity and low dissipation rate allows to capture the dynamics of fast transients that occur when strongly-driven nonlinear resonators relax to their equilibrium state. We experimentally study the ringdown dynamics of a nanomechanical Duffing resonator in both the time domain and in phase space. The resonator exhibits very different ringdown dynamics depending on whether it is subject to one or two tone driving. In the former case the ringdown follows a regular exponential decay, whereas the latter case shows exotic dynamics such as libration, slowing-down while scattering in phase-space, and ghost slowing-down. We demonstrate that this distinct difference can be attributed to the structure of the phase space itself. Surprisingly, we find that the energy in the resonator changes non-monotonously and that long delay between turning off the drive and the decay of the motion amplitude results in dynamical meta-states.

</details>


### [185] [Unlimited-Range Absolute Distance Interferometry Outdoors via Tunable Synthetic and Pseudo-Supersynthetic Wavelengths: The TeleYAG-II Interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40038)
*Florian Pollinger,Tobias Meyer,Frank Pilarski,Hongdan Yan,Anni Sauthoff,Günther Prellinger,Stephanie Weinrich,Frank Schmaljohann,Paul Köchert*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出利用可调谐合成波长干涉构建"伪超合成波长"链，在仅两台Nd:YAG激光器和标准外差干涉仪上实现兼具宏观无模糊度与微观干涉精度的绝对测距，并在224 m户外基线上取得0.13 mm扩展不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对距离干涉测量要真正"绝对"，必须具有无限的非模糊度范围（non-ambiguity range）；而传统合成波长方法受限于光源数量与波长间隔，难以同时兼顾大范围与小分辨率，且现场大尺度（大地测量基线检定）需要SI可溯源的便携标准。

Method: 以两台Nd:YAG激光器为光源，通过多功能光学锁相环（OPLL）精确控制二者频差，用有限数量光源生成一系列可连续衔接的合成波长，进而构造伪超合成波长；采用外差干涉结构并按现场鲁棒性要求工程化，构建TeleYAG-II干涉仪作为可溯源的测距标准，并开展户外比对测量验证。

Result: 户外测量实验中，在无需任何辅助长度测量的条件下，于224 m距离上实现0.13 mm的扩展测量不确定度，验证了连续合成波长链方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 伪超合成波长方案可在少量光源和常规干涉仪配置下获得无限非模糊度范围，兼顾宏观范围与干涉级精度，适用于大地测量基线等大长度、高精度、可溯源的现场绝对测距应用。

Abstract: Absolute distance interferometry requires an unlimited non-ambiguity range to be truly absolute. Tunable synthetic wavelength interferometry allows for the construction of pseudo-supersynthetic wavelengths using a limited number of optical sources and a standard interferometer setup. This approach facilitates the creation of a series of consecutively deployable synthetic wavelengths, ultimately combining macroscopic unambiguity with microscopic interferometric accuracy. We have implemented and demonstrated this concept in the TeleYAG-II interferometer, designed as an SI-traceable standard for the calibration of geodetic baselines. The system employs two Nd:YAG lasers as optical sources, with their frequency difference controlled by a versatile optical phase-locked loop. Specifically engineered for robustness in field conditions, the heterodyne interferometer was validated through an outdoor measurement campaign. The results demonstrate the efficacy of the method, achieving an expanded measurement uncertainty of 0.13 mm over a distance of 224 m without the need for auxiliary length measurements.

</details>


### [186] [Multistable nematic liquid crystal pores as reconfigurable optical vortex metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40123)
*Claire Doré,Brian Edwards,Nader Engheta,Kathleen J. Stebe*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 亚波长向列相液晶柱孔阵列中，不同指向矢拓扑（径向、逃逸、拱形）在孔下方近场留下可区分的相位奇异光签名，但该签名无法传播到远场；电场可切换拓扑，构成可重构、非易失的近场拓扑光读出平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索液晶在柱形孔隙中的指向矢拓扑能否在孔径缩小至光波长量级时仍产生可识别的光学印记，并厘清这类拓扑编码光学读出是否可用于远场或仅限近场，从而评估其在光学标记与防伪中的可行性。

Method: 采用Landau–de Gennes建模与Jones计算，并用微米尺度实验验证；进一步对周期性亚波长孔阵列做全波Maxwell仿真，分析近场与远场传播行为；同时用Landau–de Gennes模拟考察外加电场对拓扑的切换。

Result: 各拓扑在透射圆偏振光中携带由投影指向矢绕数决定的拓扑荷 ℓ=2m，形成不同的光学签名；孔径缩小至波长量级时机制仍成立。全波仿真显示涡旋仅为近场现象：孔阵正下方存在可区分的相位奇异签名（核多重性、核宽度、环振幅不同），但经约一个波长传播后即被衍射与孔间干涉抹除。电场可在拓扑间切换，从而切换其近场光签名，且各拓扑局部稳定。

Conclusion: 亚波长液晶孔阵列可作为电可重构、非易失的近场拓扑编码光学读出平台，适用于光学标记与防伪；但其拓扑光签名局限于近场，不具备远场可读性。

Abstract: Nematic liquid crystals confined in cylindrical pores adopt multiple stable director topologies --- radial, escaped, and arches --- each imprinting a distinct optical signature on transmitted circularly polarized light, with topological charge set by the projected director winding number, $\ell = 2m$. Using Landau--de Gennes and Jones-calculus modeling validated against micron-scale experiments, we show that this mechanism persists as the pore diameter shrinks toward the optical wavelength. However, full-wave Maxwell simulations of periodic sub-wavelength pore arrays reveal that the resulting vortex is a near-field phenomenon only: each topology imprints a distinguishable phase-singular signature --- differing in core multiplicity, core width, and ring amplitude --- directly below the pore array, but this signature does not survive propagation into the far field, where diffraction and inter-pore interference erase it within about one wavelength. Landau--de Gennes simulations further demonstrate that an applied electric field switches the pore between topologies, and hence between their near-field optical signatures. Because each topology is locally stable, sub-wavelength NLC pore arrays constitute an electrically reconfigurable, non-volatile platform for topology-encoded near-field optical readout, with potential application to optical labeling and anti-counterfeiting.

</details>


### [187] [Scale-free wave localization at topological multicriticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40125)
*Zhijie Xue,Yuan Tian,Nanyu Chen,Xiujuan Zhang,Tao Li,Shuang Zhang,Fuxin Guan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作报道了声学超材料中由拓扑多重临界性驱动的无标度边界模式，其空间包络呈严格线性衰减；作者借助突变理论建立了几何体-边对应，证明空间转移矩阵的亏损性强制边界模按(n-1)次幂衰减，并在声学平台实验验证了一维线性衰减及二维无标度角态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上边界局域模被限制为指数衰减，而既有临界拓扑框架依赖纠缠谱来诊断无能隙区模式存在性，缺乏对实空间波函数衰减速率的确定性刻画与设计能力。

Method: 将n阶拓扑多重临界性映射到Arnold A_n突变，建立基于奇点理论的普适几何体-边对应；证明空间转移矩阵的亏损性导致(n-1)次幂空间衰减；在三临界点处绕数函数的奇点表现为A2折叠突变；使用调谐至三临界点的声学超材料在一维实验验证线性包络并推广至二维角态。

Result: 观察到严格线性空间包络的边界模（非指数衰减）；一维实验证实该线性衰减；二维系统中实现沿正交方向同时线性衰减的无标度角模。

Conclusion: 把突变理论与多重临界拓扑物质相桥接，将研究从抽象的模式判定推进到确定性的实空间波函数工程，突破了以指数标度为主导的传统范式。

Abstract: Spatial wave localization at boundaries is conventionally restricted to exponential decay. Here, we observe a scale-free boundary mode exhibiting anomalous polynomial decay, highlighted by strictly linear spatial envelopes, driven by topological multicriticality in acoustic metamaterials. We establish a universal geometric bulk-boundary correspondence rooted in singularity theory by mapping n-th order topological multicriticality onto Arnold An catastrophe. We prove that defectiveness in the spatial transfer matrix enforces a (n-1)-th power spatial decay of boundary modes. At a topological tri-critical point, the singularity of winding function manifests as an A2 fold catastrophe. This dictates a strictly linear spatial decay, marking a sharp departure from the conventional exponential scaling paradigm. Using acoustic metamaterial platforms tuned to a tricritical point, we experimentally demonstrate this linear decay envelope in 1D, and further generalize it to two-dimensional systems to realize scale-free corner modes with simultaneous linear decay along orthogonal directions. While existing critical topology frameworks rely on entanglement spectra to diagnose mode existence in gapless regimes, our work bridges catastrophe theory with topological matter at multicriticalities, advancing the field from abstract mode identification to deterministic real-space wavefunction engineering.

</details>


### [188] [Contact-free charge carrier density determination of doped sub-μm thin cubic GaN layers with terahertz time-domain spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40142)
*Joshua Hennig,Mario F. Zscherp,Silas A. Jentsch,Daniel Anders,Jörg Schörmann,Till Fuchs,Anja Henss,Sangam Chatterjee,Christian Röder,Franziska C. Beyer,Christina Jörg,Joachim Jonuscheit,Georg von Freymann,Daniel Molter*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 采用太赫兹时域光谱（THz-TDS）对0.6–2 μm厚、掺杂浓度10^18–10^20 cm^-3的立方GaN层进行非接触式载流子密度测量，并用TOF-SIMS验证结果，与灵敏度模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方GaN是光电子应用的潜力材料，需精确掺杂；现有接触式载流子密度测量方法不及非接触光学方法便捷，故探索无接触的表征手段。

Method: 制备厚度0.6–2 μm、掺杂10^18–10^20 cm^-3的立方GaN样品；先用傅里叶变换红外光谱（FTIR）标定层厚，再进行太赫兹时域光谱（THz-TDS）测量以提取载流子密度，并与飞行时间二次离子质谱（TOF-SIMS）结果及技术灵敏度范围的模拟进行比对。

Result: THz-TDS提取的载流子密度与TOF-SIMS测量结果相符，且落在模拟预测的该技术灵敏掺杂区间内；FTIR验证了层厚标定的可靠性。

Conclusion: 光学方法可覆盖较宽的掺杂立方GaN样品范围，有望实现生长后乃至生长过程中的快速、无损表征。

Abstract: As cubic GaN is a promising material for optoelectronic applications, its layers need to be precisely doped to realize the desired properties. While contact-based methods are established to measure the realized charge carrier density, contact-free techniques are more advantageous for this purpose. We show terahertz time-domain spectroscopy measurements of cubic GaN layers with thicknesses between 0.6 $μ$m and 2 $μ$m with doping densities in the range from $10^{18}$ cm$^{-3}$ to $10^{20}$ cm$^{-3}$. From these measurements, we determine the charge carrier densities and verify the results with time-of-flight secondary-ion mass spectrometry measurements. The results are in accordance with simulations of the doping ranges which the technique is expected to be sensitive in. In preparation for the terahertz time-domain spectroscopy measurements, we validated the layer thicknesses by Fourier-transform infrared spectroscopy measurements. With this, a wide range of doped cubic GaN samples can be addressed by optical methods. In the future, these techniques could allow for a fast and non-destructive characterization of GaN layers, right after or even during the growth process.

</details>


### [189] [Temporal equivalent of the Sommerfeld half-plane problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40171)
*Asma Fallah,Shixiong Yin,Andrea Alù,Nader Engheta*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究Sommerfeld半平面问题的时间类比：半无限Drude色散介质与空气相邻，等离子体频率突降为零发生时间界面，分析低于等离子体频率的单色波正入射时场的传播行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Sommerfeld问题描述PEC半无限薄板对单色波的衍射，是电磁散射理论的基石。作者希望建立其时间域类比，考察时间界面（而非空间边界）在色散介质体系中引发的场演化，以理解时间与时空超材料中的电磁波行为。

Method: 构造半无限Drude介质-空气体系，使等离子体频率在某一时刻快速降为零（介质瞬时变为空气），同时以低于原等离子体频率的单色波正入射。利用时间界面前Drude介质中已有的倏逝穿透场和空气中的驻波作为初值，解析推导时间演化后的电场与磁场结构。

Result: 时间跃迁迫使空气侧驻波与Drude介质内倏逝场同时向两个方向传播，但保持各自的空间场结构不变；此外，与时间界面相关的"DC"电流密度激发产生额外的电场波和磁场波。

Conclusion: 时间界面可作为Sommerfeld问题中尖锐空间边界的类比，但分隔的是两个时间区间而非两个空间半平面。该结果揭示了色散介质中时间跃迁处的电磁波行为，可应用于时间超材料与时空超材料设计。

Abstract: Sommerfeld's half-plane problem, describing diffraction of a monochromatic wave by a semi-infinite perfectly electric conducting (PEC) infinitesimally thin plate, is one of the cornerstones of classical electromagnetic scattering theory. Here we study its temporal analogue: a semi-infinite medium with the Drude dispersion, adjacent to air, whose plasma frequency is rapidly reduced to zero, thus turning it into air, while a monochromatic wave with frequency below the plasma frequency illuminates it under normal incidence. Unlike the PEC plate, the Drude medium supports evanescent penetration of the field prior to its temporal transition; the temporal interface here acts as the analogue of the sharp spatial boundary in the Sommerfeld problem, but now separates two time intervals rather than two spatial half-planes. We show that this transition forces the standing wave in air in front of the Drude medium and the evanescent fields inside it to propagate in both directions, while preserving their spatial field structure. In addition, we observe the excitation of additional electric and magnetic field waves generated by a "DC" current density associated with the time interface. Our findings offer insight into the electromagnetic wave behavior at temporal transitions in dispersive media and may find applications in time- and space-time metamaterials

</details>


### [190] [Nonlinear Jackiw-Rebbi exciton-polaritons at van der Waals topological interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40479)
*Oscar J. Palma Chaundler,Paul Bouteyre,Xuerong Hu,Sam A. Randerson,Daniel J. Gillard,Owen R. Wolley,Yue Wang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Helgi Sigurðsson,Alexander I. Tartakovskii*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作将图形化的低损耗hBN介质光栅与封装WSe2单层结合，构成一维双层光栅异质结构，实现了局域于两个拓扑不同光栅界面的Jackiw-Rebbi激子极化激元；通过4.7–300 K变温调谐激子穿越光子带隙，观测到上下两支Jackiw-Rebbi极化激元分支的反交叉演化，并揭示其显著的非线性响应（最大5 meV蓝移及强度标度与线宽的相关变化），确立vdW光子异质结构为可调、非线性拓扑界面极化激元的通用平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑光子结构通过不同拓扑相界面处的界面态实现光场局域与导引；范德华材料则可从丰富层状晶体中选取光学性质，并因原子级平整表面与无晶格匹配限制而支持逐层堆叠复杂异质结构。二者结合可使拓扑光子局域与强激子耦合在同一结构中分别独立设计，从而构建可调、可非线性的拓扑界面极化激元。

Method: 以图形化低损耗介质六方氮化硼（hBN）定义光子模式，以封装WSe2单层提供与受限光场直接耦合的激子共振；构建一维hBN双光栅异质结构，在两个拓扑不同光栅界面处形成光子Jackiw-Rebbi态。通过4.7–300 K变温使激子扫过光子带隙，利用角分辨反射与光致发光光谱追踪上下Jackiw-Rebbi极化激元分支的演化与反交叉；并进行功率依赖光致发光测量。

Result: WSe2激子与局域Jackiw-Rebbi光子态强耦合，形成上、下两支Jackiw-Rebbi极化激元分支；变温调谐下清晰观测到两支的反交叉行为与色散演化。功率依赖光致发光显示局域Jackiw-Rebbi极化激元具有显著非线性响应，包括最高约5 meV的蓝移，以及强度标度指数与线宽的关联变化。

Conclusion: 范德华光子异质结构可作为工程化可调、非线性且局域于拓扑界面的极化激元的通用平台，为拓扑光局域与激子耦合的协同设计提供新途径。

Abstract: Topological photonic structures provide a powerful means of confining and directing light through interface states formed between photonic phases with distinct topological properties. Van der Waals (vdW) materials offer complementary opportunities for nanophotonics: their optical properties can be selected from a wide family of layered crystals, while their atomically smooth surfaces and lack of lattice-matching constraints enable complex photonic heterostructures to be assembled layer by layer. This flexibility allows topological photonic confinement and strong excitonic coupling to be engineered separately within the same structure. Here, patterned low-loss dielectric hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) defines the photonic modes, while an encapsulated WSe2 monolayer provides the excitonic resonance that couples directly to the confined optical field. We use this approach to realise Jackiw-Rebbi exciton-polaritons in a one-dimensional hBN double-grating heterostructure. Strong coupling between the WSe2 exciton and a photonic Jackiw-Rebbi state localised at the interface between two topologically distinct gratings produces upper and lower Jackiw-Rebbi polariton branches. By varying the temperature from 4.7 to 300 K, we tune the exciton across the photonic bandgap and trace the evolution and anticrossing of these branches using angle-resolved reflectance and photoluminescence spectroscopy. Power-dependent photoluminescence further reveals a pronounced nonlinear response of the confined Jackiw-Rebbi polariton, including a blueshift of up to 5 meV and correlated changes in its intensity scaling and linewidth. These results establish vdW photonic heterostructures as a versatile platform for engineering tunable and nonlinear polaritons localised at topological interfaces.

</details>


### [191] [On the gauge-invariant orbital angular momentum of vector optical vortices in the paraxial approximation and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40523)
*E. V. Maliutina,E. A. Vashukevich,V. A. Leonov,D. D. Reshetnikov,T. Yu. Golubeva*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出标量与矢量光学涡旋的自旋和轨道角动量的规范不变形式化描述，引入由横向矢势非局域性导致的修正项以恢复规范不变性并实现角动量分解，并据此分析多模矢量涡旋场的角动量结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋—轨道角动量分解在横向场的非局域处理下会破坏规范不变性，难以严格区分总角动量的自旋与轨道成分，因此需要建立规范不变的描述框架，以正确刻画矢量光学涡旋的角动量结构。

Method: 构建规范不变的形式化体系：在自旋与轨道角动量定义中引入来自横向矢势非局域性一致处理的修正项；提出计算多模矢量涡旋场轨道与自旋角动量的方法；并以螺旋拓扑荷为基模讨论空间非均匀偏振场的量子化程序，同时考察该形式化的适用范围与限制。

Result: 修正项恢复了规范不变性，使总角动量可分离为自旋与轨道分量；多模矢量涡旋光束的轨道角动量包含两个性质不同的成分：与偏振无关、由决定相位奇点阶数的拓扑荷决定的部分，以及与偏振有关、正比于决定偏振空间非均匀性的螺旋拓扑荷的部分。

Conclusion: 规范不变的自旋—轨道分解需纳入横向矢势非局域性修正项；多模矢量涡旋场的轨道角动量具有双重成分结构（拓扑荷贡献与螺旋拓扑荷贡献），基于螺旋拓扑荷模的量子化描述适用于具有空间非均匀偏振的场，但其适用性存在明确边界。

Abstract: In this paper we present a gauge-invariant formalism for describing the spin and orbital angular momenta of scalar and vector optical vortices. The correction term, arising from consistent incorporation of the transverse vector potential's nonlocality, restores gauge invariance and makes it possible to separate the total angular momentum into spin and orbital components. We propose a method for calculating the orbital and spin angular momenta of multimode vector vortex fields and show that the orbital angular momentum of such beams contains two qualitatively different components. The polarization-independent contribution to the orbital angular momentum is related to the topological charge that determines the order of the phase singularity, while the polarization-dependent one is proportional to the helical topological charge that determines the spatial inhomogeneity of the polarization. We also discuss the quantization procedure for fields with spatially inhomogeneous polarization in terms of modes with a helical topological charge, and examine the limits of applicability of the proposed formalism.

</details>
